Generation often fails to express this information, and on the two intervention-tested backbones, activation-restoration effects at the patched entity position vanish while decodability persists across depth. Mean-difference steering also does not reliably control outputs. These results show that decodability, generation, activation-level influence, and steerability can diverge in the tested setting. The audit provides a controlled way to distinguish failures to encode geometric structure from failures to express or control encoded information.

</details>


### [126] [BayesPrompt: human readable prompts that make sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17866)
*Franky Kevin Nando Tezoh,Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio,Guido Sanguinetti,Riccardo Rende*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将提示词优化重新框架为贝叶斯后验推断问题，通过高效采样算法生成既高效（低困惑度）又具有人类可读性的提示词，在真实数据集上相较现有方法取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中能够引发期望答案或行为的提示词重构是一个重要且开放的研究课题。现有的优化方法（如最小化困惑度）虽然能产生有效提示词，但往往生成不可理解的"伪提示词"（pseudoprompts），缺乏人类可解释性。作者认为这是提示词优化任务本身不适定（ill-posed）的结果。

Method: 将提示词优化任务重新表述为对提示词的后验推断（Bayesian posterior inference）问题，在此基础上提出一种高效算法，用于采样既高效（困惑度低）又具有人类可读性的提示词。

Result: 与最先进的替代方法进行比较，在真实数据集上多个评估指标下取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯视角重新框架提示词优化问题，能够在保持提示词有效性的同时获得人类可读的提示词，解决了传统优化方法产生的伪提示词问题。

Abstract: Reconstructing prompts that can elicit a desired answer or behaviour in an LLM is an open and important research topic. Optimisation methods which aim at minimising the perplexity of a given answer, however, consistently yield so-called pseudoprompts, unintelligible strings of tokens which can lack human interpretability. We argue that this is a consequence of the ill-posedness of the prompt optimisation task. By reframing the task as a Bayesian posterior inference over prompts, we propose an efficient algorithm to sample prompts which are both efficient (in terms of perplexity) and human readable. We compare our approach with state of the art alternatives showing on a real data set a marked improvement over a range of metrics.

</details>


### [127] [BEAR-Bench: A Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17895)
*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEAR-Bench（双语企业学术推理基准），一个包含1000个人工标注问题的自包含英俄双语基准，专门评估MLLMs在文本密集的专业文档（商业和科学）上的复杂推理能力。作者评估了16个专有和开源MLLMs，并利用模型输出比较了现有的幻觉检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM基准在评估文本密集专业文档推理能力方面存在不足：要么侧重信息提取，要么需要外部领域知识，要么将专业文档仅作为多场景之一。此外，大多数基准以英语或中文为中心，俄语等其他语言被严重忽视。

Method: 构建了BEAR-Bench基准：包含1000个基于文本丰富的商业和科学文档的人工标注问题，覆盖英语和俄语双语。评估了16个专有和开源MLLMs（包括Gemini 3.1 Pro和Qwen3.5-397B），并使用模型输出对现有幻觉检测方法进行了对比研究。

Result: 最强系统的表现仍存在明显的改进空间（clear headroom），表明BEAR-Bench具有足够的挑战性。此外，通过比较不同幻觉检测方法，不仅评估了模型在BEAR-Bench上的失败频率，还评估了这些失败被可靠识别的程度。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench填补了多语言（特别是俄语）专业文档复杂推理评估的空白，为MLLM在这一方向的进步提供了新的评测标准，同时对幻觉检测方法的系统性比较为该领域提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [128] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CABLE（Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion），一种即插即用的记忆增强方法，通过构建与宿主检索器互补的稀疏有向图链接，解决长期对话记忆中语义相似检索无法恢复早期经验、计划或动机等证据的可达性问题。在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval等多个基准和主流记忆系统上，CABLE在所有系统级设置中均取得更高的LLM评判分数，尤其在跨会话、开放域和偏好类问题中提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在结构化工流程和多会话中运行时，仅保留长期历史并不能保证后续上下文通过有界记忆接口恢复相关证据。现有检索主要依赖语义相似性，对主题性回忆有效，但往往遗漏与后续事件语义距离较远的早期经验、计划或动机。已有记忆图谱虽提供跨记忆结构，但其链接主要由语义重叠驱动，会与宿主检索器产生重复而非互补。

Method: CABLE作为插件式增强模块，针对每条新记忆生成前因导向的查询，检索先前的记忆，从候选中减去直接语义邻域内的条目，验证剩余候选后再将可接受的互补关联加入稀疏有向图。在检索阶段，CABLE沿这些链接扩展宿主系统检索到的种子记忆，以揭示隐含的支持证据。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval上以A-MEM为宿主系统评估，并在LoCoMo上集成到SimpleMem和Mem0g，使用Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat和GPT-4o-mini。CABLE在所有评估的系统级设置中均获得更高的平均LLM评判分数，在证据分散于多个记忆或会话的类别（开放域、多会话和偏好导向问题）中提升最大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，优先构建稀疏的、与推理相关的、且与宿主检索器互补而非重复的关联，比依赖语义重叠的密集链接更有效，为长时记忆接口中的证据可达性问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [129] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechSense，一个用于细粒度语音情感分析的数据集，定义了一个8类人际立场分类体系，通过高质量语音合成和人工验证构建。实验表明，具备声学信息访问的模型持续优于仅文本的基线，验证了声学线索在检测细微说话者态度中的首要地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析存在两大局限：(1)主流方法依赖文本中心流水线（ASR+文本分析），不可避免丢失韵律、语调等关键声学特征，无法捕捉声学模糊话语中的态度含义；(2)现有基准存在标签粒度不匹配问题，优先关注基本情绪（如开心、悲伤）而非细粒度的人际立场（如自信、不耐烦），缺乏社交敏感性所需的能力。

Method: 提出SpeechSense数据集：首先定义了一个基于韵律线索（而非词汇内容）可检测的8类人际立场专门分类体系；然后基于该分类体系，通过高保真语音合成和严格人工验证构建了精选数据集；最后在多种模型（多模态大语言模型、仅文本大语言模型、语音编码器）上进行综合实验。

Result: 综合实验表明，具有声学访问能力的模型始终优于仅文本的基线模型，实证验证了声学线索在检测细微说话者态度方面的首要地位，证明了SpeechSense的必要性。

Conclusion: 声学线索对于检测细微说话者态度至关重要，SpeechSense弥补了现有语音情感分析在细粒度人际立场标签方面的空白，为语音情感分析提供了更好的基准支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [130] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"any-to-bench"框架：由前沿模型在文档摄入时一次性提取题目与评分标准（rubric），再由低成本模型完成所有重复评分工作。实验表明，低成本模型在显式rubric指导下，其评分可靠性与昂贵模型相当。答案本身解释了95.6%的分数方差，而评分者身份仅占0.2%；rubric（尤其是其中的官方参考答案）是使评分与评判者智能解耦的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放型考题的自动评分通常依赖昂贵的前沿模型，成本高昂。作者希望验证"小型语言模型在显式rubric指导下可以达到与昂贵模型相当的评分可靠性"，从而降低大规模考试评分的成本门槛。

Method: 设计"any-to-bench"流程：(1) 前沿模型在文档摄入阶段一次性读取源文档，提取每道题及其rubric；(2) 所有重复评分工作交由低成本模型完成。实验使用两个模型家族的六种成本高效配置（三档推理努力水平），每种配置回答24道开放型考题，并对每份答卷评分三次，共产生3,456个逐题评分。补充六个前沿级评分者作为对照。另设两个消融实验：(a) 移除rubric的标准和等级但保留官方答案；(b) 同时移除官方答案。

Result: 答案身份解释95.6%的分数方差，评分者身份仅解释0.2%。提高答题者的推理努力可使得分最多变化0.143（满分比例），而提高评分者的推理努力最多仅影响0.006。六个前沿级评分者的评分与低成本模型一致，且面板并不更可靠。消融实验：仅移除rubric标准和等级而不动官方答案时无显著变化；同时移除官方答案后，可靠性从ICC 0.888降至0.628，分数虚高，且评分者推理努力重新变得重要。未发现长度偏好或同族偏好。

Conclusion: rubric是实现低成本模型可靠评分的关键，而rubric中的官方答案承担了绝大部分作用。在rubric锚定下，评分可靠性几乎完全取决于被评分的答案内容，而非评分者的智能水平或模型家族。这为大规模、低成本的开放题自动评分提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%. Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕过注意力权重，直接从隐藏状态流形的动态几何出发，证明深层LLM潜在空间天然组织为小世界网络，并在深层推理层出现尖銳的拓扑相变——巨大的概念距离被压缩为严格受"六度分离"限制的可导航路径。该框架还被应用于RAG场景下的零样本幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于注意力的可解释性方法因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影，无法真正反映语义邻近性；而LLM在多跳长上下文推理中的内部机制仍然难以理解。作者希望绕过注意力权重，从几何视角直接揭示深层模型如何进行抽象的远距离推理。

Method: 将长上下文表示的连续相似度矩阵稀疏化为无权重图，在两种不同架构中追踪高度不连通的语义锚点之间的连通性；通过分析隐藏状态流形的动态几何，证明深层LLM潜在空间天然形成小世界网络结构。

Result: 发现早期句法层保持完全断裂，而深层推理层发生锐利的拓扑相变，将庞大概念距离压缩为严格≤6语义跳的可导航路径（六度分离）。在RAGognize数据集上的幻觉检测中，事实性生成与源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉内容则引发严重的拓扑坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作数学形式化了Transformer执行抽象推理的机制，提供了一种纯粹的几何签名来评估事实可靠性，为LLM可解释性和幻觉检测提供了全新的非注意力视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AVShift，这是首个用于系统评估多分布漂移下作者归属验证（AV）任务的德语基准，包含超过15万文本对，横跨三种体裁和21年。实验表明，微调LLM在跨体裁泛化上表现最佳，时间漂移是影响AV的最强因素之一，而未发现AI时代分布漂移的可测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV基准通常孤立地研究体裁、时间、AI辅助写作等分布漂移因素，且主要集中于英语，缺乏在现实条件下对模型鲁棒性的系统性理解。作者认为这种孤立研究方式无法真实反映实际应用中多种漂移同时存在的情况，因此需要构建一个统一框架下的多分布漂移德语基准。

Method: 构建AVShift基准，包含超过15万文本对，覆盖三种体裁和21年时间跨度，在统一框架内支持跨体裁、时间及AI时代漂移的受控评估。对基于特征、基于嵌入和基于LLM的三类代表性AV方法进行系统性评测。

Result: 微调LLM在跨体裁场景下泛化能力最佳，且能从风格多样的训练数据中显著获益；时间漂移是影响AV最强的因素之一，文档间时间间隔增大导致性能显著下降；在AVShift中未发现可测量的AI时代分布漂移；特征分析显示部分风格特征在跨体裁中保持稳定，但其相对重要性随具体体裁转换而变化。

Conclusion: AVShift为德语AV任务提供了首个多分布漂移的系统性评估基准。研究揭示了时间漂移对AV性能的严重影响以及微调LLM的跨体裁优势，同时发布AVShift及代码以供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个风险受控的LLM裁判框架，通过校准不确定性阈值控制接受判决的假发现率（FDR），并在参数模式置信不足时路由到检索增强模式，实现用户指定风险水平下的可靠评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为裁判已成为大规模评估的标准做法，但客观任务中无参考答案的裁判面临可靠性挑战：参数化知识模式可能产生幻觉或证据不足，工具增强模式虽能提供额外证据但增加计算成本，且两种模式单独都无法对接受判决的风险提供形式化控制。

Method: 提出了一个风险受控框架：在留出集上校准不确定性阈值，利用有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间确保接受判决中的假发现率（FDR）以高概率低于用户指定水平α。当参数模式置信不足时，将实例路由到检索增强模式，由裁判收集网络证据并在第二个校准阈值下重新评估，双阈值路由的有限样本保证无需额外假设即可保持。

Result: 在开放域问答基准和不同规模的裁判上，该框架能维持目标错误率，同时相较于单模式基线实现了显著更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该风险受控框架为参考自由的LLM裁判在客观任务中提供了可量化的可靠性保证，通过参数模式与检索增强模式的灵活路由，在计算效率与评估可靠性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IOL-AI挑战赛，一个基于国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛（IOL）2026个人赛未公开题目的开源竞赛，首次由官方IOL评委会使用与人类选手相同的评分标准进行评估。比赛吸引了46支队伍的731份提交，使用严格计算预算（单张T4 GPU，30分钟）。结果显示Claude Opus 4.8获得金牌级评分，而资源受限系统仅处于人类选手垫底5%水平。关键发现包括：能力不取决于模型规模（14B模型表现优于两倍规模模型）、收益来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量、自动评估与人工评审排名一致但存在系统性偏差（高估弱系统约13分、低估强系统）。此外，前沿模型对问题语言的先验知识并未显著帮助其解决语言推理任务，使语言推理成为通用推理能力的强基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理研究几乎都集中在提供明确规则的领域（数学和代码），而语言学谜题要求求解者先发现系统规则再在其中推理，这一认知过程是传统基准无法覆盖的。作者希望通过IOL-AI挑战赛挖掘LLM在规则发现与归纳推理方面的能力，并首次引入官方IOL评委会的人类评分机制，以最严格的标准评估AI的语言推理能力。

Method: 作者举办了IOL-AI挑战赛，使用IOL 2026个人赛的未公开题目，采用双重评估：自动指标和官方IOL评委会按人类参赛者相同评分标准进行人工评分。比赛设有严格计算预算（单张T4 GPU、30分钟），共收到46支队伍的731份提交。作者同时测试了15个不受约束的前沿及开源模型作为基准，并分析了模型规模、解码策略、输出处理方法与性能的关系，以及自动评分与人工评分的对齐程度和先验知识的影响。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8在人工评审中获得金牌级分数，而资源受限的提交系统仅处于人类参赛者分数垫底5%的范围。14B参数的模型性能优于两倍规模的模型，表明能力并非由规模决定，增益主要来自解码和输出处理策略。自动指标的系统排名与人工评审完全一致，但自动评分存在系统性偏差：将弱系统高估约13分，同时低估强系统。前沿模型对问题语言的先验知识并未显著提升其解决语言推理任务的能力。

Conclusion: 语言学推理是一个与数学和代码推理互补的强基准，能够有效评估LLM在规则发现和归纳推理方面的泛化能力。当前前沿模型在资源充足时已能达到人类金奖水平，但在严格计算预算下表现不佳，且能力提升更依赖工程策略（解码和输出处理）而非模型容量。自动评估可用于排名但需谨慎解读绝对分数。语言推理领域仍有巨大的进步空间，是衡量LLM通用推理能力的重要代理指标。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)框架，研究LLM在测试阶段通过迭代交互持续学习的能力。实验表明，利用迭代经验（尤其结合模型自反馈和环境信号）相较于无反馈基线能持续取得更优性能，总体提升5.6%并降低19%的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评测仅评估单次零样本推理能力，忽略了模型通过测试时交互迭代积累经验、持续改进的潜力。现有评测范式无法反映模型在真实使用场景中的"持续学习"能力，需要新的评测范式来刻画LLM在推理过程中利用经验进行自我提升的能力。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)评测框架：模型在测试时间通过迭代交互累积经验轨迹，形成超越零样本推理的持续改进循环。具体实现包含多样化反馈机制，包括模型自反馈（self-feedback）和环境信号（如正确性标注、公开编码测试通过率）。在数学、编码和知识三个领域，使用8个LLM（含GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet）进行系统评估。

Result: ①利用迭代经验一致优于无反馈基线，仅自反馈就能带来显著增益；②总体平均提升5.6%，API成本降低19%；③结合互补反馈通道（模型信号+正确性信号）可带来额外增益；④CoE在每token准确率上优于现有测试时策略；⑤LLM基础能力与改进能力呈正相关；⑥模型在弱/虚假反馈下仍保持鲁棒性；⑦不同反馈类型贡献于不同改进维度，且大部分增益在迭代早期出现。

Conclusion: CoE展示了LLM在测试时间通过迭代经验持续学习的重要能力，为LLM评测提供了超越静态单次推理的新范式。该工作揭示了反馈机制设计、模型能力与改进潜力之间的关系，并验证了CoE在成本效益和性能上的综合优势，为未来LLM评测与推理策略研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言的第二参数"相位"（phase），与传统的词频/嵌入所代表的"幅度"（amplitude）相对。相位存在于意义之间，决定共同激活的意义如何组合，甚至能在意义保持在场的情况下逆转其贡献。该参数是带符号、持久的，且索引到个体和二元关系（dyads），而非群体平均状态。标准transformer在冻结推理中没有显式表达相位，可解释性追求的单义性（monosemanticity）实际上是在对抗相位机制——因为它所视为缺陷的意义共存正是反讽、引用和言外之意的条件。文章提出六项预测及弱/强两种版本的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型和语言统计方法只捕捉了词与词共现的"幅度"（关联强度），包括嵌入和注意力权重本质上都是对语料库中所有作者求和的结果，丢失了历史索引的关系信息。作者试图论证解释意义需要第二个关系参数——相位，它决定共同激活的多个意义如何结合，且能逆转意义的贡献。

Method: 理论论证与预测性实验设计：首先从语言学的角度论证相位与幅度的区别（相位存在于意义之间而非词之间）；指出群体平均（population averaging）删除了历史索引的相位信息；分析标准transformer缺乏显式的相位表示；最后提出六项可检验的预测，涵盖：压制意义是否保持活跃、遭遇顺序是否改变短语功能、信号标记是否改变共享短语的理解方式、模型有历史时是被改变还是仅被告知。区分弱主张（解释需要第二个关系参数）与强主张（量子演算约束这些现象）。

Result: 论文本身是理论主张框架而非实证结果。其主要"结果"是：1）论证了群体平均语料库无法确定个体或二元状态；2）揭示了单义性（monosemanticity）可解释性目标与相位机制的根本冲突；3）提出六项可检验预测；4）主张弱版本成立（第二个关系参数存在且必要），强版本（量子约束）尚未推得遭遇顺序约束因而不成立。

Conclusion: 语言解释需要第二个关系参数——相位，它是带符号、持久、索引到个体和二元关系的，且与幅度参数正交。试图消除意义共存的单义性解释学方向是错误的，因为意义共存正是反讽、引用和言外之意的必要条件。理论预测的架构是"具有Agent索引、带相位语义状态的语言模型"。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokEval框架，一套超越传统度量（如生育率和压缩率）的tokenizer评估指标体系，捕捉语言学与结构上有意义的属性（如UTF-8字符边界完整性和数字位值边界对齐）。通过控制变量的语言模型预训练实验验证，信息论指标预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho达0.80），而结构敏感指标与任务准确率相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型tokenizer的选择通常缺乏系统评估，因为对其设计选择如何影响下游性能的理解有限。现有标准度量（如fertility和compression rate）不足以反映tokenizer在语言和结构层面的重要属性。

Method: 提出TokEval指标框架，涵盖UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐等结构敏感指标及信息论指标。通过仅变更tokenizer的预训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法的控制性预训练实验，在bits-per-byte和多项基准（语言理解、数学推理、代码生成）上验证指标预测力。

Result: 不同内在属性对不同模型能力的影响不同：信息论度量预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho最高0.80），而结构敏感度量（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval有望实现更原则化的tokenizer评估，在二者一致的场景下以内在度量替代预训练sweep，减少计算成本并提升评估效率。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告的标准化结构和质量保证。该系统在638份CT报告中成功将22270个句子按解剖学节段进行结构化，并识别出14.1%的报告存在质量问题（最常见为发现与印象段落不一致）。放射科医生评估显示69%的报告重构正确，无临床重要信息遗漏或虚构内容，84%报告的质量保证性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告内容结构不规范、质量参差不齐，对于临床沟通和后续数据利用造成困难。传统方法难以同时实现报告的标准化结构化和质量保证，需要一种自动化解决方案来提升报告的规范性和准确性。

Method: 开发了一个多智能体AI流水线，使用正则表达式规则和本地大语言模型，在句子层面将报告结构化为标准解剖学节段。系统检测发现与印象部分之间的不匹配、章节内部矛盾、性别-解剖学冲突以及关键发现未记录沟通等问题。由两名委员会认证的放射科医生独立评估45份报告子集。

Result: 系统成功将所有22270个句子按预定解剖学格式结构化，保留了原始报告内容。标记了90份（14.1%）报告存在问题，最常见为节段不匹配（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估中，两位审阅者一致认为69%报告重构正确、4%错误、27%存在分歧；一致认为无临床重要信息遗漏或虚构内容。84%报告的QA表现被评为"优秀"或"良好"。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统能够在一个工作流程中同时实现放射学报告的结构化和质量保证，在放射科医生评估中表现出良好性能，有望支持放射学实践中的报告标准化和质量保证工作。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [VLCP: Vision Language Control Policy Closed-Loop Code Replanning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16978)
*Dhia Naouali,Minghan Wu,Claudia Wong,Abhinav Puthran,Omar G. Younis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLCP提出一种无需微调的机器人策略方法：保持视觉语言模型（VLM）冻结，让其以短Python控制函数形式编写策略，并在单次episode内每K步重新观察场景、重写控制代码形成闭环，在57个任务上达到35.1%的池化成功率，比单次查询的open-loop系统（3.5%）提升近十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将视觉语言模型微化为机器人策略时，需要让其输出预训练中从未见过的动作表示，这会丢弃模型原有的推理能力。而现有的闭环方法反应层面错误——它们只会重试固定策略或选择不同子任务，但从不重写失败的代码本身。

Method: VLCP保持VLM完全冻结，让其直接编写短Python控制函数作为策略（无需演示和微调）。闭环机制在控制代码层面工作：每个episode内每K步，VLM从多视角RGB、本体感受状态和状态增量重新观察场景，然后重写控制函数，在失败累积之前捕捉并修正错误。重规划期间编写的控制代码可持久化为跨episode的技能库，供后续提示复用。

Result: 在57个MuJoCo/RoboVerse任务的评估中，无训练策略达到35.1%的池化成功率，而相同系统单episode只查询一次时仅3.5%。每个场景家族中十倍差距均具有非重叠置信区间。收益源于27.3%的episode内失败抓取恢复率。效率方面：中位数84%的输入token命中缓存，每个episode仅需约10次紧凑查询。

Conclusion: 在控制代码层面进行闭环重写是可行且高效的方式——通过在失败真正发生的地方闭环，可以大幅提升无训练VLM机器人策略的成功率，同时保持较低的计算开销，并可通过技能库实现跨episode的知识复用。

Abstract: Turning a frontier vision-language model into a robot policy usually means fine-tuning it to emit an action representation it never saw in pretraining, which throws away much of the reasoning that made the model worth reaching for. We go the other way and keep the VLM frozen. It writes the policy as a short Python control function, with no demonstrations and no fine-tuning. Writing that code once is open-loop, though. Existing closed-loop methods react at the wrong level: they retry a fixed policy or pick a different subtask, but never rewrite the code that failed. VLCP closes the loop where the failure actually lives, on the control code, within a single episode. Every $K$ steps the VLM re-observes the scene from multi-view RGB, proprioceptive state, and a state delta, then rewrites the control function from what it just saw, so a failure is caught before it compounds.
  We evaluate on a 57-task MuJoCo/RoboVerse sweep. This training-free policy reaches $35.1\%$ pooled success, against $3.5\%$ for the identical system queried once per episode. That tenfold gap holds with non-overlapping confidence intervals in every scene family. The gain traces to a $27.3\%$ within-episode recovery rate on failed grasps: a miss an open-loop controller would carry to the end of the episode gets re-observed and fixed at the next replan. And the loop stays cheap. A median $84\%$ of input tokens hit cache, an episode needs only about $10$ compact queries, and control blocks written during any replan persist to a cross-episode skill library reused in later prompts.

</details>


### [140] [FetchMan: Learning Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Policies from Simulated Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17027)
*Omar Rayyan,Zhi Li,Max Argus,Yuxin Jiang,Chang Yu,Chenfanfu Jiang,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FetchMan，一种端到端仿真到现实的全身移动操控（loco-manipulation）策略训练管线。该管线通过三个关键发现构建：逼真模拟生成的合成演示克隆受限于性能天花板，强化学习突破该瓶颈，Flow-GRPO在单一稀疏奖励下精修克隆策略可获得超越行为克隆的性能。整个管线覆盖超15万场景，在FetchMan-Bench仿真基准上评估，并在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上零样本部署，实现的单目标到达-抓取策略在未见场景下成功率达73.3%。此外还将该方法扩展到多目标训练，为大规模移动操控通才策略迈出第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉移动操控策略要泛化到新场景和新物体是机器人学的长期目标，但当前数据驱动算法在桌面操控上收集演示已很困难，对于还需行走和保持平衡的人形机器人更是难上加难。借鉴运动控制（locomotion）中从模拟学并在现实迁移的成功经验，作者希望将其复刻到移动操控领域，以绕过真实世界演示数据收集的瓶颈。

Method: 该方法是一个三阶段端到端仿真到现实管线：(1) 在超15万个随机合成的仿真场景中收集演示数据，进行行为克隆训练合成策略；(2) 发现行为克隆存在性能天花板，进而使用强化学习突破该瓶颈；(3) 使用Flow-GRPO算法在单一稀疏奖励下精修克隆得到的策略。最终策略在FetchMan-Bench仿真基准上评估，并在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上零样本部署。

Result: FetchMan在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上零样本部署，单目标到达-抓取策略在未见场景下实现了73.3%的成功率。仿真基准FetchMan-Bench展示了行为克隆的性能天花板效应以及强化学习精修带来的显著提升。该方法还成功扩展到多目标训练场景。

Conclusion: 仿真到现实的训练策略对于人形移动操控是可行的，合成演示克隆虽简单但存在性能瓶颈，需通过强化学习精修才能突破。该工作不仅验证了规模化仿真数据管线训练移动操控策略的有效性，也为未来训练大规模移动操控通才策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Visual loco-manipulation policies that can generalize to novel scenes and objects have long been a goal of robotics research. However, today's data-hungry algorithms make collecting sufficient demonstrations a struggle for tabletop manipulation, and even more so for humanoids that must also walk and balance. Learning from simulated data and transferring that behavior to the real world, as is commonly done in locomotion, sidesteps this struggle, so we replicate that recipe for loco-manipulation. In doing so, we find that cloning synthetic demonstrations results in a low performance ceiling no matter the amount of training data. Reinforcement learning breaks through it, and refining the cloned policy with Flow-GRPO on a single sparse reward yields performance that synthetic behavior cloning cannot match. Together, these stages form our end-to-end sim-to-real pipeline spanning more than 150,000 scenes, which we use to train FetchMan. We evaluate it on FetchMan-Bench, a simulation benchmark we release, and deploy it zero-shot on a real Unitree G1, where our single-object reach-and-pick policy walks to and grasps a target across unseen scenes at 73.3% success. Finally, we extend this recipe to multi-object training, a first step toward loco-manipulation generalist policies at this data scale.

</details>


### [141] [Lambda-Hold Control: Human-Like Movement Emerges from a Minimal Task Reward in Predictive Musculoskeletal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17030)
*Jun Hyuk Lee,Chihyeong Lee,Jooeun Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出λ-hold控制器，基于平衡点（EP）假说，通过将每个肌肉的EP阈值长度λ作为策略控制变量，并结合牵张反射招募法则自动计算肌肉兴奋，从而显著降低强化学习探索空间维度和策略查询频率，首次实现肌肉驱动骨骼模型在仅用最小奖励和一小时内学习类人冲刺动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体肌肉骨骼系统的过度驱动（massive overactuation）导致强化学习在高维、冗余动作空间中的探索效率极低，训练肌肉骨骼模型生成类人运动面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出λ-hold控制器：将策略的控制变量设为每块肌肉的平衡点阈值长度λ，通过牵张反射招募法则自动计算肌肉兴奋度；在每个λ值在步态相位区间内保持（hold），大幅降低策略查询频率，从而压缩探索空间、提升学习效率。

Result: 首次实现肌肉驱动的骨骼模型仅用最小奖励（minimal reward）在一小时内学会类人冲刺动作，证明了λ-hold控制器的探索高效性。

Conclusion: 该控制器并非单纯的工程技巧，而是扎根于生理学的理论方法，结合了平衡点假说、间歇控制和最优反馈控制，为开发可学习的人体运动控制器模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: The massive overactuation in the human musculoskeletal system makes it challenging to train musculoskeletal models to generate human-like motion via reinforcement learning, primarily because exploration in the resulting high-dimensional and redundant action space is extremely inefficient. To address this problem, we propose the $λ$-hold controller, inspired by the equilibrium-point (EP) hypothesis, which has been widely supported by extensive evidence from human motor control studies. The policy's control variable is the per-muscle EP threshold length $λ$, from which a stretch-reflex recruitment law computes the muscle excitations automatically. Holding each $λ$ over an interval of the gait phase also sharply reduces the frequency at which the policy must be queried. Consequently, the controller, to our knowledge for the first time, enables a muscle-actuated skeletal model to learn human-like sprinting using only a minimal reward within an hour of training. The efficient exploration through the proposed $λ$-hold controller is not merely an engineering trick but an approach grounded in physiology, bringing together the EP hypothesis, intermittent control, and optimal feedback control. Beyond encapsulating human-like behavior in predictive simulation, this achievement contributes to developing a learnable model of the human motor controller.

</details>


### [142] [Terrain-Aware Local Path Planning with Global DEM Data Integration for Autonomous UGV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17038)
*Devender Singh,Issah Nazif Suleiman,Paul Mitten,Glenn Cutler,Vinicius Prado da Fonseca,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合框架，将低分辨率DEM全局路径规划与实时LiDAR局部感知结合，实现户外复杂地形下无人地面车辆的高效自主导航，在Gazebo仿真中达到95%避障率和更低坡度通行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外复杂地形下的自主导航存在全局地图与实时传感器反馈之间的固有脱节问题，单一的全局规划或局部感知方法难以兼顾长期路径效率与实时安全避障，因此需要一种能融合两者的混合框架。

Method: 首先利用预处理后的DEM格网开展基于A*算法的全局路径规划；随后以实时LiDAR数据驱动局部路径自适应修正，实现障碍物检测与地形分析，使UGV能够应对突发环境变化；最后在Gazebo自定义地形中搭建仿真环境验证整体性能。

Result: 在Gazebo自定义地形仿真中，相较于基线方法，该框架实现了95%的障碍物规避成功率，并将平均遭遇坡度从8°降低至2.7°，在路径效率、地形可通行性及实时适应性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该混合框架有效弥合了全局规划与局部感知之间的鸿沟，兼具路径效率与实时鲁棒性，为动态户外环境中更可靠的自主导航提供可行方向。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex outdoor terrains presents critical challenges for unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) due to the inherent disconnect between global mapping and real-time sensor feedback. This work proposes a hybrid framework that integrates low-resolution Digital Elevation Model (DEM) data with real-time LiDAR-based obstacle detection and terrain analysis for efficient path planning. A global path is initially computed using a preprocessed DEM-based A* algorithm. Subsequently, local sensor data drives adaptive path correction, enabling the UGV to negotiate sudden environmental changes while maintaining safety and efficiency. Simulation results in Gazebo demonstrate significant improvements over a baseline approach, achieving a 95\% obstacle avoidance rate and reducing the average encountered slope from $8^\circ$ to $2.7^\circ$ in custom terrain. This integration enhances path efficiency and terrain traversability and supports robust real-time adaptation, paving the way for more reliable autonomous navigation in dynamic outdoor environments.

</details>


### [143] [PDDL-ART: Autonomous Symbolic Abstraction From Demonstration For Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17146)
*Disha Kamale,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PDDL-ART是一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的框架，能从单个专家演示、自然语言任务描述和动作名称库中自主生成任务特定的PDDL领域和问题描述，无需领域模板、动作签名或微调，通过多阶段校正管道（语法、语义、执行层面）确保生成描述的正确性，在引擎维护和家务操作任务中达到93.3%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号规划（PDDL）为长时程机器人操作提供了有原则的框架，但构建精确的PDDL领域和问题描述需要大量领域专业知识，是显著瓶颈。现有方法依赖人工构建或需要领域模板、动作签名和微调，缺乏能自动生成准确PDDL描述的方案。

Method: PDDL-ART采用VLM从单个专家演示、自然语言任务描述和高层动作名称库中自主生成PDDL领域与问题描述。框架包含多阶段校正管道：语法级校正确保有效性，语义级校正确保与演示任务对齐，执行级校正通过符号谓词接地（symbolic predicate grounding）验证执行正确性。关键创新是利用现代VLM的工具调用能力，融合几何和时序推理来评估无法仅从图像判别的关系谓词，模型自主决定何时调用工具及如何解释工具输出。

Result: 在引擎维护和家务领域中的挑战性操作任务上评估，包括需要记忆、抽象谓词推理以及目标状态与初始状态视觉上难以区分的任务。PDDL-ART平均成功率达93.3%，显著优于基线VLM规划器的78.3%。

Conclusion: PDDL-ART证明了无需领域专业知识或模板，VLM即可自主生成准确且语义对齐的PDDL描述，通过多阶段校正和执行引导的谓词接地机制有效应对长时程机器人操作规划中的复杂挑战。

Abstract: Symbolic planning with PDDL offers a principled framework for long-horizon robot manipulation, but constructing accurate PDDL domain and problem descriptions remains a significant bottleneck, typically requiring substantial domain expertise. We present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based approach called PDDL-ART, a framework that autonomously generates task-specific PDDL domain and problem descriptions from a single expert demonstration, a natural language task description, and a library of available high-level action names. PDDL-ART does not require any domain templates, action signatures, or fine-tuning. To ensure the generated descriptions are not only syntactically valid but semantically aligned with the demonstrated task, PDDL-ART introduces a multi-stage correction pipeline operating at syntactic, semantic, and execution levels. A key component of execution-guided correction is symbolic predicate grounding. Instead of relying solely on visual observations, PDDL-ART leverages the tool-use capabilities of modern VLMs to incorporate geometric and temporal reasoning for evaluating relational predicates that are not directly discernible from images alone. Critically, the model autonomously determines when to invoke these tools and how to interpret their outputs. We evaluate PDDL-ART on challenging manipulation tasks in engine maintenance and household domains, including tasks that require memory, abstract predicate inference, and goal states that are visually indistinguishable from the initial state. PDDL-ART achieves an average success rate of 93.3%, compared to 78.3% for a baseline VLM-based planner.

</details>


### [144] [Teach and Grow: An Agent-Centered Architecture for General Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17209)
*Chang Nie,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Teach-and-Grow Learning (TGL)，一种以智能体为中心的学习架构，通过将少量成功示范转化为可复用的Skill Blocks（技能块），在新场景中组合、执行并基于物理反馈修正路线，从而避免每次遇到新任务都需要重新收集数据、更新策略和回归测试的"重训练税"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型和世界-行动模型虽然为通用机器人提供优雅路径，但其可靠性受限于已验证的物理覆盖范围。当遇到陌生物体、传感器、形态或接触超出覆盖且无验证回退方案时，修复失败需要新的机器人数据、策略更新和回归测试，这种反复的负担被称为"重训练税"（retraining tax）。与文本数据不同，具身数据往往需要通过操作机器来产生，成本高昂，因此迫切需要一种能持续学习、避免重复训练的学习架构。

Method: TGL的通用形式为：多模态智能体将少量成功示范转化为可复用的Skill Blocks（针对有意义子目标的闭环行为）；在新场景中，智能体对这些模块进行接地（grounding）与组合，选择学习或几何工具，观察物理结果，并在执行偏离意图时修改路径。Skill Library存储可执行行为，结构化的Experience Memory记录成功、失败与修复经验。新任务获取不需要针对特定任务的策略重训练。此外，作者提出Teach-and-Grow规模定律假说：若X表示有效可复用经验，则未来任务误差和教学需求应以X的幂律逼近不可降低的下限。

Result: LIBERO评测达到最先进水平；控制实验证明了技能归纳（skill induction）、持续复用（persistent reuse）和智能体导向的自适应（agent-directed adaptation）能力。

Conclusion: TGL将部署视为持续学习的过程，在此过程中一个任务可以使下一个任务更容易，从而有效规避"重训练税"。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models offer an elegant route to general-purpose robotics, but their reliability is bounded by validated physical coverage. When an unfamiliar object, sensor, embodiment, or contact falls outside that coverage and no validated fallback exists, correcting the failure requires new robot data, a policy update, and regression testing. This recurring burden is the retraining tax. Unlike text, embodied data must often be created by operating machines. We present Teach-and-Grow Learning (TGL), an agent-centered architecture for general robot learning. In its general form, a multimodal agent turns a few successful demonstrations into reusable Skill Blocks: closed-loop behaviors for meaningful subgoals. In a new scene, the agent grounds and composes these blocks, selects learned or geometric tools, observes the physical outcome, and revises the route when execution departs from intent. A Skill Library stores executable behavior, while structured Experience Memory carries forward success, failure, and repair. New tasks are acquired without task-specific policy retraining. Our LIBERO evaluation attains state-of-the-art performance; controlled studies expose skill induction, persistent reuse, and agent-directed adaptation. Finally, we propose the Teach-and-Grow scaling-law hypothesis: if X denotes effective reusable experience, future-task error and teaching demand should approach irreducible floors as power laws in X. The architecture therefore treats deployment as a period of continued learning, in which one task can make the next easier.

</details>


### [145] [Robust Brachiation on a Life-Sized Dual-Arm Robot Using Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17320)
*Ayumu Iwata,Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Takahiro Hattori,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的路点引导强化学习（WGRL）框架，用于在真人大小的双机械臂机器人上实现猿猴式摆荡运动（brachiation）。通过稀疏指定末端执行器路点来引导运动习得，结合任务成功与机械能奖励，并在Sim-to-Real友好环境中训练，最终在几何变化的猴子杠环境及硬件实验中验证了鲁棒的摆荡行为，包括失败恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摆荡运动（brachiation）是灵长类仅用上肢进行移动的 locomotion 方式，可在无立足点的环境中通行，但该运动需要高度协调的全身运动以及对抓杠/放杠的精确时序控制，在真人大小的机器人平台上实现鲁棒行为仍具有挑战性。

Method: 提出WGRL（Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning）框架：在缺乏模仿学习数据的高难度任务中，通过稀疏指定末端执行器轨迹路点来引导行为习得，全身运动由强化学习自主生成；将路点跟随引导与基于任务成功和机械能的奖励相结合，并在专门为Sim-to-Real迁移设计的环境中训练。

Result: 通过几何变化的猴子杠环境下的Sim-to-Sim实验以及硬件实验，验证了所习得行为的鲁棒性，包括失败的恢复行为；确认了所提方法可实现向前推进与运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究为在真人大小的机器人硬件上实现基于臂部的运动提供了有效的学习设计准则，并拓展了机器人的可通行工作空间。

Abstract: Brachiation is a form of locomotion in which primates move primarily using their arms, enabling traversal in environments without footholds. However, this motion requires highly coordinated whole-body movement and precise timing control for bar grasping and release. As a result, achieving robust behavior on life-sized robotic platforms remains challenging. In this study, we present a reinforcement learning-based method to realize brachiation on a life-sized dual-arm robot. The core of the proposed approach is Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning (WGRL), a learning framework for inducing non-linear and complex motions. For high-difficulty tasks where imitation learning data are unavailable, WGRL guides behavior acquisition by sparsely specifying waypoints for the end-effector trajectory, while whole-body motion is generated through reinforcement learning. In addition, by integrating the waypoint-following guidance with rewards based on task success and mechanical energy, and training in an environment designed for Sim-to-Real transfer, the proposed method achieves both forward progression and motion stability. The acquired behavior is evaluated through Sim-to-Sim experiments under monkey-bar environments with geometric variations and hardware experiments, confirming robust brachiation including failure recovery behavior. This study provides effective learning design guidelines for realizing arm-based locomotion on life-sized robotic hardware and expanding the traversable workspace of robots.

</details>


### [146] [ORPA: Online Residual Policy Adaptation for Robot Manipulation Control with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17323)
*Muhammad A. Muttaqien,Tomohiro Motoda,Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线残差策略适应（ORPA）框架，通过轻量级反馈条件模块预测关节空间的残差调整，在不修改基础策略参数的情况下实现机器人的实时动作修正，在ALOHA平台上的精细操作任务中提升了成功率并增强了抗扰动恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模仿学习的机器人操作策略（如ACT）在理想条件下表现良好，但对小执行误差和分布漂移敏感；传统修正方法需要数据集聚合和全策略重训练，计算成本高且不适合实时部署。

Method: ORPA框架：在预训练控制策略上附加一个轻量级、反馈条件化的模块，该模块直接在关节空间中预测残差调整量，从而在运行时即时修正机器人动作，无需改变底层策略参数。

Result: 在ALOHA平台上的一组精度敏感操作任务中，相比基线控制策略和基于规则的运动学逆解修正，ORPA在成功率和从微小扰动中恢复的能力上均有提升。

Conclusion: ORPA提供了一种轻量、即时的在线策略修正方案，能够在运行时实现反馈驱动的动作自适应，兼顾了修正效果与部署效率。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies trained via imitation learning, such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), can achieve strong performance under ideal conditions but often remain sensitive to small execution errors and distribution shifts. Correcting these failures typically requires dataset aggregation and full-policy retraining, which is computationally expensive and unsuitable for real-time deployment. In this work, we propose Online Residual Policy Adaptation (ORPA), a framework that enables immediate, feedback-driven correction of robot actions without modifying the underlying policy parameters. ORPA augments a pretrained control policy with a lightweight, feedback-conditioned module that predicts residual adjustments directly in joint space, allowing the system to adapt its behavior at runtime. We evaluate ORPA on a set of precision-sensitive manipulation tasks using the ALOHA platform, demonstrating improvements in success rate and recovery from small perturbations compared to baseline control policies and rule-based inverse kinematics corrections.

</details>


### [147] [Reconfiguration-Complete Motion Primitives with Constructive Planning for Deformable Planar Modular Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17324)
*Jie Gu,Tingting Wang,Hongrun Gao,Yirun Sun,Zhihao Xia,Chunxu Tian,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对可变形模块化机器人的重配置规划问题，提出了一种方形单元抽象方法，将可变形菱形模块映射为固定大小的网格单元，并通过枢转（pivoting）和剪切（shearing）两种基元保留物理可解释的局部运动。作者证明了任意N≥7的非直线边连接配置都能通过合法基元运动变换为固定的规范阶梯构型，由于运动可逆，任意两个同类配置可互相重配置。该证明是构造性的，直接产出一个阶梯规范化规划器，并附带一个边界到投递的前瞻选择器来加速规划。实验验证了构造过程并表明该选择器显著缩短了规划时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形模块化机器人的几何形状是连续可变的，这使得为重配置规划和分析定义固定表示变得困难。现有的表示方法无法同时兼顾物理可解释性和规划可计算性，因此需要一个既能抽象表示又能保留物理运动语义的形式化框架。

Method: 提出方形单元抽象，将可变形菱形模块映射到固定大小的网格单元，定义枢转和剪切两种物理可解释的基元运动；在此抽象下，通过构造性证明表明任意N≥7的非直线边连接配置可变换为规范阶梯构型，并据此实现一个阶梯规范化规划器，通过可逆性推导任意两配置间的可重配置性；还引入边界到投递的前瞻选择器对高层选择进行排序。

Result: 实验展示了几何上可解释的构造性重配置过程；前瞻选择器显著降低了规划时间；与先前框架在共享模块数量上的参考比较显示本文方法具有更低的规划时间。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方形单元抽象为可变形模块化机器人的重配置规划提供了一个既有完备性保证又具备物理可解释性的新框架，且构造性证明直接转化为高效规划器，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The continuously deformable geometry of modular robots makes it difficult to define a fixed representation for reconfiguration planning and analysis. This letter introduces a square-cell abstraction that maps deformable rhombus modules to fixed-size grid cells while retaining physically interpretable local motions through two primitives, pivoting and shearing. Under this abstraction, we prove that every non-straight edge-connected configuration with $N \geq 7$ can be transformed to a fixed canonical staircase using only admissible primitive motions. Since these motions are reversible, any two configurations in this class are mutually reconfigurable. The proof is constructive and directly yields a staircase-canonicalization planner that transports removable boundary modules while preserving connectivity. As a practical enhancement, we further introduce a boundary-to-delivery lookahead selector that ranks admissible high level choices without affecting the completeness guarantee. Experiments demonstrate the constructive reconfiguration process and show that the selector substantially reduces planning time, while reference comparisons indicate lower planning times than the prior framework over the shared module counts.

</details>


### [148] [MANIGUARD: A Benchmark and Data Suite for Specification-Grounded Safety Evaluation and Improvement of Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17386)
*Yiyan Peng,Philip Wang,Simon Sinong Zhan,Yiqi Lyu,Zhenyang Ni,Jixin Yan,Fiorelli Wong,Ruochen Jiao,Hang Yin,Xinyu Cao,Huajie Shao,Manling Li,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ManiGuard，一个基于规范（specification-grounded）的框架，用于评估和提升基础模型操控策略的安全性。它包含200个锁定基础任务（6个接触密集型家庭任务族）和8000个安全标注演示。通过超过23,000次rollout评估发现：成功rollout中有6-21%违反安全规范；微调可将安全任务完成率从近零提升至7.5-29.8%；但存在仅靠扩充演示无法弥合的安全差距，21-42%的engaged rollout仍违反规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型操控策略在任务成功率上快速进步，但缺乏对其安全性的严格评估框架。当前安全评估依赖学习型分类器或LLM裁判，缺乏规范约束和物理可验证性，因此需要一个将安全与任务成功解耦、可验证的评测体系。

Method: 构建ManiGuard框架，包括：(1) ManiGuard-Bench任务套件，按技能×约束分类学组织6个任务族200个基础任务，每个任务在1个分布内和4个单轴分布外扰动下评估，共1000个锁定场景；(2) 基于LTL_f的自动机监控器对物理基础谓词进行运行时检查（非学习分类器）；(3) 配对安全标注轨迹生成流水线，结合自动运动规划生成器与人工遥操作，支持安全感知微调，发布8000个演示。

Result: 在超过23,000个rollout上测试零样本和微调VLA：(i) 6-21%的成功rollout违反安全规范，证明安全与成功必须独立评估；(ii) 微调后安全任务完成率从近零提升至7.5-29.8%，engaged且安全行为从16-40%提升至51-72%；(iii) 仅扩展演示无法弥合差距，21-42%的engaged rollout仍违规，6个任务族中有2个在所有策略下安全成功率低于2%，且失败在分布偏移和硬件上持续存在。

Conclusion: 安全必须作为与任务成功独立的维度进行评估和优化。虽然安全感知微调有效，但当前基础模型策略仍存在系统性安全缺陷，尤其在某些任务族上，单纯扩大训练数据规模无法解决，需要新的方法突破。

Abstract: Foundation-model policies for robotic manipulation are advancing rapidly on task success, but rigorous evaluation of whether they succeed safely is still lacking. We introduce ManiGuard, a specification-grounded framework for evaluating and improving the safety of foundation-model manipulation, comprising the ManiGuard-Bench task suite and a paired safety-annotated trajectory-generation pipeline. ManiGuard-Bench organizes six contact-rich household task families into 200 locked base tasks along a skill $\times$ constraint taxonomy, with safety specified independently of task success. Each task is evaluated under one in-distribution and four single-axis out-of-distribution perturbations that hold the safety specification fixed, giving 1,000 locked scenarios. Every rollout is runtime-checked by LTL$_f$-grounded automaton monitors over physics-grounded predicates rather than learned classifiers or LLM judges, in simulation and on a physical Franka platform. The pipeline pairs an automated motion-planning generator with human teleoperation, annotated by the same per-step monitor, and directly supports safety-aware fine-tuning; we release 8,000 safety-annotated demonstrations, 40 per base task. Benchmarking zero-shot and fine-tuned VLAs across more than 23,000 rollouts, we find: (i) safety must be evaluated independently of task success, as 6-21% of successful rollouts violate the specification; (ii) fine-tuning on our suite raises safe task completion from near zero to 7.5-29.8% and engaged-and-safe behavior from 16-40% to 51-72%; but (iii) a gap remains that scaling demonstrations does not close, with 21-42% of engaged rollouts still violating, two of six families below 2% safe success for every policy, and these failures persisting under distribution shift and on hardware.

</details>


### [149] [Bi-Layer Ant Colony Optimization for Multi-Robot Task Allocation and Routing in Delivery Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17416)
*Le Na Nguyen,Thanh Long Nguyen,Thanh Thao Ton Nu,Quan Le,Manh Duong Phung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层蚁群优化（ACO）算法，通过新的代价函数将多机器人任务分配（MRTA）问题统一建模为兼具任务分配与路径规划的优化问题，在单一蚁群过程中集成两个相互依赖的决策层，实现多机器人任务分配与路径规划的联合优化，相比MILP和PSO基线方法，总行驶距离减少多达17.7%，完成时间缩短近20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人任务分配（MRTA）对物流和配送应用至关重要，现有方法往往将任务分配与路径规划分离处理，难以获得全局最优解。

Method: 首先定义新的代价函数将MRTA转换为统一优化问题，同时涵盖任务分配和路径规划两方面；然后提出双层蚁群优化（bi-layer ACO）算法，在单一蚁群过程中集成两个相互依赖的决策层，通过分层框架实现跨多机器人的任务分配与路线规划同步优化。

Result: 与MILP和PSO基线方法对比，所提双层ACO在所有任务规模下均取得最短总行驶距离和最快完成时间；总行驶距离减少达17.7%，完成时间缩短近20%。

Conclusion: 双层ACO算法在效率、可扩展性和可靠性方面均表现出色，适用于多机器人配送任务，验证了其作为MRTA求解方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) problem, which is essential for delivery and logistics applications. Our approach first defines a new cost function that transforms the MRTA into a unified optimization problem capturing both task assignment and routing. A bi-layer ant colony optimization (ACO) algorithm is then introduced, integrating two interdependent decision layers within a single colony process to solve the problem. This hierarchical framework enables simultaneous optimization of task allocation and route planning across multiple robots. Comparative experiments with mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) demonstrate that the proposed bi-layer ACO achieves the shortest total travel distance and fastest completion time across all task sizes. Specifically, it reduces total travel distance by up to 17.7% and completion time by nearly 20% compared with baseline methods. These results confirm the efficiency, scalability, and reliability of the proposed bi-layer ACO for multi-robot delivery tasks.

</details>


### [150] [Prism-GRPO: Faster VLA Policy Optimization via Splitting Same-outcome Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17423)
*Zeyun Deng,Yuzhe Lu,Yawei Wang,Linbo Liu,Qing Ping,Han Ding,Guande Wu,Panpan Xu,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prism-GRPO通过将二值成功奖励与轨迹级执行质量分数结合，解决了GRPO在VLA策略强化学习中因同结果组零优势而浪费机器人rollout预算的问题，在四个RoboTwin任务上以更少的rollout（最多节省56%）达到目标成功率，且抑制奖励黑客行为并成功迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO无需训练critic而被广泛用于VLA策略强化学习，但代价是需要多次rollout计算组相对优势。在二值成功奖励下，全成功或全失败的组优势为零且被动态采样丢弃，这在训练早期（大多数rollouts失败）尤为严重，极大浪费昂贵的机器人rollout预算。

Method: Prism-GRPO在二值结果奖励上叠加一个加权轨迹级执行质量分数，将同结果组拆分为质量谱系以恢复训练信号，同时保证所有成功仍排在所有失败之上。质量分数可从仿真接触、执行动作或视觉观测中获取，无需任务特定的进度奖励。论文证明Prism-GRPO不会增加采样组因零优势被丢弃的概率，并推导了梯度对齐条件确保组合更新是任务成功的局部上升方向。

Result: 在四个覆盖不同时间跨度和协调模式的RoboTwin任务上，Prism-GRPO在匹配rollout预算下提升成功率和质量，达到目标成功率所需rollout最多减少56%；它抑制了奖励黑客捷径，更清洁的行为在真实机器人直接部署中得以迁移；消融实验显示contact、smoothness和VLM-derived质量信号均带来一致收益。

Conclusion: Prism-GRPO通过引入轨迹级质量分数有效缓解了GRPO在二值奖励下的rollout浪费问题，在效率、成功率和行为质量上均有显著改善，且具有理论保证，是一个实用且可泛化的VLA强化学习改进方案。

Abstract: GRPO is increasingly used for reinforcement learning of vision-language-action (VLA) policies because, unlike PPO, it does not require training a critic. This simplification comes with a sampling cost: group-relative advantages require multiple rollouts from each scene. Under binary success rewards, groups whose rollouts all succeed or all fail have zero advantage and are discarded by dynamic sampling. These groups are especially common early in training, when most rollouts fail, wasting much of the expensive robotic rollout budget. We introduce Prism-GRPO, which augments binary outcome reward with a weighted trajectory-level execution-quality score. By splitting same-outcome groups into a quality spectrum, Prism-GRPO recovers training signal while ensuring that every success still outranks every failure. Quality scores can be derived from simulator contacts, executed actions, or visual observations, avoiding task-specific progress rewards. We prove that Prism-GRPO never increases the probability that a sampled group is discarded for having zero advantages, and derive a gradient-alignment condition under which its combined update remains a local ascent direction for task success. Across four RoboTwin tasks spanning different horizons and coordination patterns, Prism-GRPO improves success and quality at matched rollout budgets and reaches target success rates with up to 56% fewer rollouts. It also suppresses a reward-hacking shortcut, with the cleaner behavior transferring under direct deployment to a real robot. Through ablations, we show consistent gains across contact-, smoothness-, and VLM-derived quality signals.

</details>


### [151] [UniReflex: Plug-and-Play Force Control for Pretrained Generative Policies via Fast-Slow Reflex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17432)
*Yan Huang,Shoujie Li,Ziwu Song,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniReflex是一个即插即用的通用框架，通过在冻结的生成式模仿学习策略上叠加可变阻抗控制（VIC）和快速反射网络，实现接触力调节，无需重新训练主干网络，显著降低推理延迟并提升接触稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模仿学习策略在轨迹规划方面表现出色，但缺乏闭环力调节能力；而直接引入力模态通常需要重新设计或重新训练网络，成本高昂。作者希望在不修改冻结骨干网络的前提下，为现有生成式策略赋予接触力调节能力。

Method: UniReflex从动作头的深层潜在表示中非侵入式提取特征，驱动一个快速反射网络，该网络将主动力的施加与外部交互响应解耦。通过预测归一化的各向异性刚度方向实现方向性柔顺分配，并集成自适应门控机制，实现位置主导规划与力主导执行之间的无缝切换。

Result: 真实双臂实验表明，UniReflex显著提升了接触稳定性和成功率，同时保持了原有的位置精度。在所评估的骨干网络上，相比联合训练策略，每步反向延迟降低了25-66倍。

Conclusion: UniReflex证明了一种轻量级、模块化的力调节框架可以有效扩展冻结生成式策略的接触能力，为通用机器人操作中的力控提供了一种低延迟、高兼容性的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative imitation learning policies excel at trajectory planning but lack closed-loop force regulation, while directly incorporating force modalities often requires redesigning or retraining the network. We present UniReflex, a universal plug-and-play framework that equips frozen generative policies with variable impedance control (VIC) for contact regulation, guided by force-direction intent collected during demonstration, without further slow-backbone fine-tuning. By non-invasively intercepting deep latent representations from the action head, UniReflex drives a fast reflex network that decouples active force exertion from external interaction response. This scheme predicts normalized anisotropic stiffness directions for directional compliance allocation. Furthermore, UniReflex integrates an adaptive gating mechanism that enables seamless transitions between position-dominant planning and force-dominant execution. Real-world bimanual experiments demonstrate that UniReflex significantly improves contact stability and success rates while preserving original position accuracy. Our approach achieves 25-66x lower per-step backward latency relative to joint training strategies on the evaluated backbones.

</details>


### [152] [EATR-Stereo: Embodiment-Aware Routing of Paired Stereo Evidence for Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17453)
*Songwei Wu,Rui Zhao,Fan Yang,Zhongqiang Nie,Zhiduo Jiang,Wandong Sun,Yuwei Li,Yang Liu,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EATR-Stereo是一个面向长时程人形机器人VLA控制的具身感知token路由框架，通过保留主视图token并构建主视图对齐的跨视角辅助token（CVAT），结合身体分段的本体感知编码器实现选择性立体视觉证据融合，在33自由度物理人形机器人上实现了60%的全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人VLA视觉接口要么丢弃互补的立体视觉证据，要么在融合额外观测时未能保留原生主视图通路，也未将辅助信息适配到机器人具身结构上。这导致长时程任务中空间接地感不足，限制了控制可靠性。

Method: 1) 保留主视图token并构建primary-aligned的Cross-View Auxiliary Tokens (CVATs)，通过查询同步的辅助视角token序列实现跨视角对齐；2) 引入body-segmented本体感知编码器，基于机器人配置历史对token级辅助信息使用进行条件化，实现选择性立体证据融合；3) 在执行动作生成时，路由的辅助流增强预训练VLA的语言和主视觉上下文，同时保持其视觉-语言模型冻结。

Result: 在33-DoF物理人形机器人（37-D本体感知状态）上，评估9种配置、100+次搜索-接近-抓取-放置-返回任务。EATR-Stereo实现60.0%全任务成功率、100.0%抓取成功率和80.0%阶段成功率。在严重不对称遮挡下，恢复能力从CVAT单独的30%提升至80%。消融实验验证了保留主token和结合跨视角辅助特征与结构化本体路由的重要性。

Conclusion: 选择性路由的成对立体证据能够改善空间接地感，为可靠的长时程人形机器人VLA控制提供了有效方案，证明了具身感知的辅助信息适配对提升VLA性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid vision--language--action (VLA) control with head-mounted stereo cameras requires visual interfaces that can exploit complementary views while maintaining compatibility with pretrained representations. Existing interfaces often discard complementary stereo evidence or fuse additional observations without preserving the native primary-view pathway and adapting auxiliary information to robot embodiment. We present EATR-Stereo, an embodiment-aware token-routing framework that retains primary-view tokens and constructs primary-aligned Cross-View Auxiliary Tokens (CVATs) by querying the synchronized auxiliary-view token sequence. A body-segmented proprioceptive encoder further conditions token-wise auxiliary usage on robot configuration history, enabling selective incorporation of stereo evidence during action generation. The routed auxiliary stream augments the language and primary-visual context of a pretrained VLA while keeping its vision--language model frozen. On a 33-DoF physical humanoid with a 37-D proprioceptive state, we evaluate nine configurations in over-100-s search--approach--grasp--place--return tasks. EATR-Stereo achieves 60.0% full-task success, 100.0% grasp success, and 80.0% stage success. Under severe asymmetric occlusion, it improves recovery to 80% compared with 30% for CVAT alone. Ablation studies further show the importance of preserving primary tokens and combining cross-view auxiliary features with structured proprioceptive routing. These results demonstrate that selectively routed paired stereo evidence improves spatial grounding for reliable long-horizon humanoid VLA control.

</details>


### [153] [Reuse Before You Retrieve: Diagnosing Headroom and Complementarity for Test-Time Augmentation of Embodied Multimodal Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17484)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在测试时增强（采样与外部检索）的选择决策问题，提出可恢复余量（recoverable headroom）和检索互补性（retrieval complementarity）两个可测指标来指导干预方式的选择，并通过实验验证该决策框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结VLA策略在测试时可以通过额外采样或引入外部演示来提升性能，但缺乏明确的指导来决定部署的策略到底需要哪种干预。额外采样仅在策略随机rollout中已存在更好行为时有用，而检索仅在策略未能可靠表示相关动作先验时最有效。

Method: 提出两个可测量的因素——可恢复余量（表征策略已有但待恢复的有用行为量）和检索互补性（表征外部动作先验是否填补了可测量的缺口）。设计了一个episode级别的重试选择器（retry selector），支持可重试或并行执行，并配合外部检索，在多个冻结VLA策略和环境中进行评估。

Result: 选择器在LIBERO上所有测试的VLA骨干网络上均能持续恢复显著的潜在能力，提升最高达21.0个成功率百分点，且与可恢复余量紧密相关。该方法可迁移到不同的机器人和模拟器，在观测退化情况下仍保持有效。检索行为不同：仅改进动作先验缺口最大的策略，且与选择结合时可进一步增益。自回归OpenVLA实验表明可恢复余量与候选rollout排序能力之间存在区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果为测试时增强机会的表征提供了实证基础，通过区分可从冻结策略中恢复的能力与可能需要外部引入的行为先验，指导何时采用采样选择、何时采用检索这两种干预手段。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies are increasingly improved at test time by sampling additional policy behaviors or introducing external demonstrations. Yet there is little guidance for deciding which intervention a deployed policy actually needs. Additional sampling is useful only when better behavior already exists within the policy's stochastic rollouts and can be identified, whereas retrieval is most useful when the relevant action prior is not reliably represented by the policy. We study this decision through two measurable factors, recoverable headroom and retrieval complementarity, which characterize how much useful behavior is already available to recover and whether an external action prior fills a measurable gap. We evaluate an episode-level retry selector under retryable or parallel execution, together with retrieval across multiple frozen VLA policies and environments. The selector consistently recovers substantial latent capability across all tested VLA backbones on LIBERO, with gains of up to 21.0 success-rate points that closely track recoverable headroom. It also transfers to a different robot and simulator and remains effective under degraded observations, while experiments with autoregressive OpenVLA illustrate the distinction between available headroom and the ability to rank candidate rollouts. Retrieval behaves differently, improving the policy with the largest measured action-prior gap and providing further gains when combined with selection. Together, these results provide an empirical basis for characterizing test-time augmentation opportunities by separating capability that can be recovered from the frozen policy from behavioral priors that may need to be introduced externally.

</details>


### [154] [Calibrated Predictive Safety for Heterogeneous Robots: An Action-Conditioned JEPA Framework with Model-Based Safety Shields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17496)
*Kaiming Zhong,Tianhua Liu,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将动作条件JEPA世界模型与确定性安全盾牌相结合的递推时域决策流水线，用于在异构机器人上执行前预测任务进度和物理风险，并进行候选动作块的筛选与重排序。通过确定性盾牌提供执行期保证，JEPA仅负责重排合法候选，在LIBERO-Long模拟中相比仅盾牌基线提升了成功率并降低了碰撞漏报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略泛化能力强但缺乏执行时保证；经典基于模型的规划器能尊重运动学与几何约束但泛化能力差。作者希望在不牺牲泛化能力的前提下，为异构机器人提供可部署的执行期安全性保障，同时预测任务进度与物理风险。

Method: 提出递推时域决策流水线：（1）提议器生成K个候选动作块；（2）动作条件JEPA在冻结编码器的隐空间中，基于具体化嵌入（embodiment embedding）前向滚动每个候选；（3）校准过的风险头与进度头为每个滚动打分并输出不确定性；（4）确定性的按具体化（per-embodiment）安全盾牌过滤不可行候选；（5）回退阶梯处理无可采纳候选的情况。学习得到的排序仅重排合法候选，执行保证完全来自确定性盾牌与回退阶梯。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long模拟中采用预注册协议评估：在600回合配置下，完整框架相比仅盾牌基线提升了成功率，并在匹配召回率下减少了碰撞假阴性。此外提供了目标机器人与边缘加速器上的部署效率测量。真实机器人实验和离线重排显著性检验留作未来工作。

Conclusion: 将学习的JEPA世界模型预测与确定性安全盾牌解耦是可行的：学习组件负责提升决策质量（重排候选），确定性组件负责执行安全保证，从而兼顾VLA的泛化能力与经典规划器的安全性，为异构机器人提供可部署执行前风险评估流水线。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies generalize broadly but provide no execution-time guarantees; classical model-based planners respect kinematic and geometric constraints but generalize poorly. We study whether an action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world model can predict, before execution, both task progress and physical risk for candidate action chunks, and whether coupling these predictions to an embodiment-specific model-based safety shield yields a deployable pipeline for heterogeneous robots.
  We propose a receding-horizon decision pipeline: (1) a proposer produces K candidate action chunks; (2) an action-conditioned JEPA rolls each candidate forward in a frozen-encoder latent space conditioned on an embodiment embedding; (3) calibrated risk and progress heads score each rollout and report uncertainty; (4) a deterministic per-embodiment safety shield filters inadmissible candidates; (5) a fallback ladder handles empty-admissible-set cases. The learned ranking only reorders admissible candidates; enforcement guarantees come from the deterministic shield and fallback ladder.
  We evaluate with a pre-registered protocol in simulation (LIBERO-Long). In 600-episode configurations the full framework improved success over a shield-only baseline and reduced collision false negatives at matched recall. Deployment-efficiency measurements on target on-robot and edge accelerators are included. Real-robot experiments and an offline reranking significance test remain future work; see the paper for disclosures.

</details>


### [155] [Embodied-Navigator: Point, Think, Memorize, and Align for Efficient Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17512)
*Hongyan Feng,Sunlai Chen,Xuanyu Liu,Miao Pan,Yangfan Xie,Yuxiang Cui,Zhongxiang Zhou,Rong Xiong,Wenqi Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Yueting Zhuang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAMP-Nav是一个统一的具身导航框架，通过Pixel-to-3D动作公式（Point）、选择性推理与锚轨迹记忆机制（Think and Memorize）以及两层级对齐范式（Align）解决VLMs在具身导航中动作空间不匹配、推理调度僵硬和记忆管理低效的问题，在R2R-CE上达到66.2%的SR，仅需9万条训练轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）在具身导航中直接部署面临三大挑战：现有方法强制VLMs进入与其2D预训练先验不匹配的非自然动作空间；推理调度机制过于僵化，缺乏动态触发；记忆管理效率低下。作者希望解决这些核心局限以实现高效具身导航。

Method: TAMP-Nav包含三个关键组件：(1) Pixel-to-3D Action Formulation（Point）：将导航重新表述为2D视觉提示，VLM仅选择2D像素，再投影为3D坐标交由底层SLAM控制器执行，使具身执行与VLM固有的2D视觉能力自然对齐；(2) Selective Reasoning and Anchor-Trajectory Memory mechanism（Think and Memorize）：动态触发思维链推理，仅在关键节点保留高保真记忆，将冗余轨迹压缩为轻量级时空指示符；(3) Two-Level Alignment Paradigm（Align）：基于GRPO，叠加全局结果奖励与细粒度过程奖励，提供密集监督以紧密对齐智能体的认知规划与物理环境反馈。

Result: 实验表明TAMP-Nav在R2R-CE基准上达到66.2%的SR，实现最先进性能，同时具有高运行效率和样本效率（仅需90k训练轨迹）。

Conclusion: 通过将动作空间与VLM的2D先验对齐、引入动态推理与高效记忆机制以及密集两层级奖励对齐，TAMP-Nav有效解决了VLM具身导航中的核心瓶颈，实现了更高效、更具适应性的导航能力。

Abstract: Although Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced embodied navigation, their direct deployment remains challenging, as existing methods often force VLMs into unnatural action spaces that misalign with their 2D pre-training priors, compounded by rigid reasoning schedules and inefficient memory management. To overcome these limitations, we propose TAMP-Nav, a unified framework for efficient embodied navigation. First, we introduce a Pixel-to-3D Action Formulation (Point) that reformulates navigation into 2D visual prompting. Specifically, the VLM merely selects 2D pixels, which are then projected into 3D coordinates for a low-level SLAM controller. This design naturally aligns embodied execution with the VLM's inherent 2D visual capabilities. Second, we propose an integrated Selective Reasoning and Anchor-Trajectory Memory mechanism (Think and Memorize), which dynamically triggers Chain-of-Thought and retains high-fidelity memory only at critical nodes, compressing redundant trajectories into lightweight Space-Time Indicators, thereby preserving critical historical information and enhancing spatio-temporal perception. Finally, we design an efficient Two-Level Alignment Paradigm (Align) via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). By superimposing global outcome rewards with fine-grained process rewards, this dense supervision tightly aligns the agent's cognitive planning with physical environmental feedback, endowing the model with adaptive reasoning capabilities. Experiments demonstrate that TAMP-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance (e.g., 66.2% SR on R2R-CE) with high runtime and sample efficiency (requiring only 90k training trajectories).

</details>


### [156] [Scalix: Uncertainty-Aware Scale-Consistent Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17553)
*Sebastian Barbas Laina,Tianyi Zhang,Panagiotis Petropoulakis,Simon Schaefer,Simon Boche,Jaehyung Jung,Cedric Le Gentil,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Scalix是一个实时单目SLAM框架，通过将学习到的深度线索集成到概率因子图优化中，实现度量尺度状态估计，解决了单目SLAM固有的尺度模糊问题，在室外和室内大规模环境中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目SLAM虽然配置简单但存在尺度模糊问题；视觉惯性系统虽然能观测尺度，但在机器人恒速运动（常见场景）下尺度不可观测；现有深度学习深度图虽有希望但噪声大且跨帧尺度不一致。

Method: Scalix将单目深度模型的预测与逐像素深度不确定性和逐帧尺度不确定性相结合，将尺度预测作为优化中的独立测量值，嵌入概率因子图框架中，并通过多视图数据关联提升尺度一致性。

Result: 在大规模室外和室内数据集上的实验表明，Scalix在度量基准和尺度归一化基准上均达到最先进性能，同时保持实时运行能力和良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过概率方式集成学习深度先验并显式建模不确定性，可以可靠地恢复单目SLAM的度量尺度，在保持实时性的同时获得优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Cameras are ubiquitous sensors in robotics due to their compact form factor and the perceptual richness captured through visual information. Monocular SLAM enables robots to understand the environment with a minimum setup, however, it inherently suffers from scale ambiguity. A common solution is to provide multi-modal sensor configurations, such as visual-inertial systems, where scale is observable unless the robot navigates under a constant-velocity motion, a common scenario in mobile robotics. With the advent of deep-learning, geometric foundation models have been used to address this problem, but the depths maps are often noisy and scale-inconsistent across frames. In this paper, we propose Scalix, a real-time monocular SLAM framework that achieves metric-scale state estimation by integrating learned depth cues into a probabilistic factor-graph formulation. By augmenting existing monocular depth models with both per-pixel depth uncertainty and per-frame scale uncertainty, Scalix treats scale predictions as independent measurements within its optimization, leading to improved scale consistency through multi-view data associations. Experiments in large-scale outdoor and indoor environments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both metric and up-to-scale benchmarks while maintaining real-time operation and generalization.

</details>


### [157] [HODAgent: Towards On-Demand, Responsive Humanoids for Physical World Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17584)
*Wang Warren Chen,Jiahao Zhang,Zhenjiang Li,Mingxu Wang,Lei Yi,Yuchen Kang,Shuo Sun,Ziping Chen,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HODAgent是一个面向服务场景人形机器人的System-2具身智能体，采用半双工架构，整合环境交互器、规划器、执行器和分层记忆，在仿真与实体机器人上均优于基线，实现跨虚实环境的自适应服务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务场景中人形机器人需要处理情境意图、响应式执行、任务修订和结果验证等多方面挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的System-2架构来同时维护交互、规划与任务状态，难以应对服务过程中出现的新请求和动态变化。

Method: 提出了HODAgent半双工架构，包含Env-Interactor、Planner、Executor和分层Memory四大模块。半双工设计允许在运动过程中处理新请求、保留进度、修订动作，并通过执行结果验证闭环。共享接口统一仿真与实体机器人（Unitree G1）的控制，隔离平台特定实现。

Result: 交互仿真测试164个案例中，在两种VLM骨干下Joint Success分别达到84.8%和91.5%，比基线高9.8和18.9个百分点。实体机器人上原子任务通过率92%、复合任务72%、完整任务63.3%。多个具身基准上较基线提升0.7-9.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 统一的System-2智能体架构能够使能人形机器人在仿真和现实环境中具备自适应服务能力，验证了该设计范式的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: We propose HODAgent, a System-2 embodied agent for humanoid robots in service settings, addressing situated intent, responsive execution, task revision, and outcome verification. Its semi-duplex architecture integrates an Env-Interactor, Planner, Executor, and hierarchical Memory to maintain coherent interaction, planning, and task state during service episodes. This allows handling new requests during motion, retaining progress, revising actions, and grounding closure in execution outcomes. A shared interface connects simulation and physical robots (Unitree G1), isolating platform-specific control. In an interactive simulation with 164 cases, HODAgent achieves 84.8% and 91.5% Joint Success under two VLM backbones, outperforming baselines by 9.8 and 18.9 points. On physical robots, pass rates are 92% (atomic), 72% (composite), and 63.3% (complete tasks). On multiple embodied benchmarks, it improves over baselines by 0.7-9.0 points. Results show a unified System-2 agent enables adaptive humanoid service across simulation and reality.

</details>


### [158] [tinyDSM: A Framework for Skill Modeling and Development for Resource-Constrained Millirobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17596)
*Markus D. Kobelrausch,Michael Miedler,Axel Jantsch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究资源受限的小型毫机器人如何在生命周期内自主探索、学习和适应能力，提出了tinyDSM框架，将内在动机与适应性评估结合，用强化学习驱动技能习得，实现从最小先验知识出发的开放式技能发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘米级毫机器人等资源受限系统面临计算、存储和能量限制，传统方法难以在其上实现自主学习和适应能力。现有方法通常需要大量先验知识和资源，无法满足小型系统在真实环境中的开放式发展需求。

Method: 提出tinyDSM框架，核心包括：(a)基于内在动机的发展机制，(b)认知架构（知识、推理、学习），(c)最小资源利用。采用层级知识图谱和运动学推理器建模并评估运动技能，结合强化学习算法驱动技能获取和适应，仅编码最小先验通用知识。

Result: 在36 cm³体积、使用Raspberry Pi Pico RP2040（仅9 kB资源）的毫机器人上验证，机器人从最基础电机技能开始，在15分钟内自主发展为复杂的几何运动模式。补充仿真分析支持跨学习算法和内在动机参数的系统比较。
