Conclusion: 该研究证明即使资源极度受限的小型系统，也能通过最小先验知识和内在动机机制实现自主的开放式技能发展，为小型机器人认知架构设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate developmental mechanisms that enable small, resource-constrained systems such as cm-sized millirobots to autonomously explore, learn, and adapt their capabilities throughout their lifespan. Reinforcement learning algorithms guide the agent's skill acquisition and adaptation through the interplay of our proposed tinyDSM, which integrates intrinsic motivation and fitness-based assessment. We strive for minimal, hard-wired skills while encouraging the open-ended development of new skills. A key emphasis in our approach is to encode minimal a-priori general knowledge, which serves as a foundational starting point for the system as it further learns system-specific dependencies from the initial knowledge provided. Thus, by design, our approach attempts to cover very generic application domains. The methodology is based on (a) developmental mechanism with intrinsic motivation, and (b) a cognitive architecture (knowledge, reasoning, learning), while (c) utilizing minimal resources. It uses a hierarchical knowledge graph and kinematic reasoners to model and evaluate simple and advanced motion related skills. In our experiments, we use a resource-constrained millirobot with a volume of 36 cm^3 with a Raspberry Pi Pico 32-bit microcontroller (RP2040) that integrates all described features and capabilities except the camera system in 9 kB. Starting with learning the most elementary motor skills the millirobot autonomously progresses from simple linear and angular movements to complex geometric patterns within 15 minutes. To complement the physical experiments, we perform a simulation-based analysis that enables systematic comparisons across learning algorithms and intrinsic motivation parameters.

</details>


### [159] [LIBERO-VIFO: Benchmarking the Capability and Safety of Visual Cue Following in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17600)
*Zhengyan Qian,Rui Yan,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Jinhui Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBERO-VIFO基准，系统评估VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型中视觉线索跟随的能力与安全性。该基准定义了8个视觉线索家族和4个协议（分两部分），分别测试授权线索理解与跟随能力，以及无授权情况下视觉线索跟随的安全风险。评估7个VLA模型发现：视觉线索理解与执行之间存在差距，且当前VLA模型能够在无语言指令时执行线索指示的任务，暴露了未授权视觉线索跟随的新兴风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅覆盖范围狭窄的线索形式，仅关注最终任务成功率，缺乏对线索跟随能力的细致评估；同时将所有视觉线索视为授权线索，未探索未授权跟随的安全风险。需要统一的基准来同时评估VLA模型视觉线索跟随的能力与安全性。

Method: 构建LIBERO-VIFO基准：定义8个多样化的视觉线索家族；设计4个协议分为两部分——第一部分（Part I）测试线索理解与授权跟随能力，第二部分（Part II）在语言线索冲突和空洞语言条件下评估未授权视觉线索跟随。在7个VLA模型上进行评估，并扩展实验覆盖场景实例化线索、安全关键设置和真实机器人部署。

Result: 视觉线索理解并不能可靠转化为执行能力；当前VLA模型在无语言指令情况下能够执行线索指示的任务，暴露未授权视觉线索跟随的安全风险。扩展实验进一步佐证了这些发现。

Conclusion: LIBERO-VIFO将视觉线索跟随的能力与安全性纳入系统评估框架，为VLA社区确立了以视觉为中心的安全视角，为后续研究提供了新的评估维度和方向。

Abstract: Visual cues are increasingly adopted to guide robot learning, but whether Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can reliably follow authorized cues while disregarding unauthorized ones remains unclear. Existing work covers only a narrow range of cue forms and focuses on final task success, providing only a coarse assessment of cue-following capability. Treating all visual cues as authorized also leaves safety risks of unauthorized following unexplored. To address these gaps, we introduce LIBERO-VIFO, a benchmark to evaluate both the capability and safety of visual cue following in VLA models. LIBERO-VIFO defines eight visual cue families spanning diverse forms. A total of four protocols in two parts are defined: Part I tests cue understanding and authorized following, while Part II evaluates unauthorized visual cue following under language-cue conflict and empty language conditions. Evaluating seven VLA models reveals that although visual cue understanding does not reliably translate into execution, current VLAs are able to execute cue-indicated tasks without language instruction, exposing an emerging risk of unauthorized visual cue following. Extended experiments on scene-instantiated cues, safety-critical settings, and real-robot deployment corroborate these findings. LIBERO-VIFO brings both the capability and safety of visual cue following into systematic evaluation, establishing visual-centric safety as a new perspective for the VLA community.

</details>


### [160] [Physics-Informed Sliding-Window Particle Filtering for Tactile-Only In-Hand 6-DoF Object Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17601)
*Lingjun Shao,Ying Zhang,Xiangfei Li,Xiangyang Li,Huan Zhao,Zhenyu Wang,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息粒子滤波器，用于在视觉不可用或被严重遮挡的静态及短准静态手内操作场景中，仅依靠密集全手触觉测量实现6自由度物体位姿精修与信念维护，并在Allegro Hand V5上的五个物体实验中获得优于多种基线方法的归一化ADD-S指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉-仅位姿估计的核心难点在于触觉部分可观测性：全手税觉(手指触觉)接触点稀疏、间歇且存在歧义，尤其在激励受限和物体对称性条件下。现有方法未充分利用整个手掌的密集触觉信息进行鲁棒的位置信念更新。

Method: (1) 在SE(3)上构建物理信息粒子滤波器；(2) 似然函数融合主动接触符号距离一致性、法向力对齐、摩擦锥可行性、零力负证据及可选的可行性保护；(3) 采用滑窗对数似然融合近期触觉帧以降低单帧歧义；(4) 势场引导的提议分布将粒子从手-物体穿透区域移开；(5) 对称感知重采样保留多个可行模式。

Result: 在Allegro Hand V5上对五个物体的实验表明，该方法得到的归一化ADD-S低于触觉-仅几何方法、粒子滤波基线和学习方法基线；消融实验证实了时间融合、势场引导和模式保留的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过融合多模态物理约束的粒子滤波框架能够在视觉缺失条件下实现鲁棒的触觉-仅位姿精修与信念维护，其关键设计（时间融合、势场引导、对称感知重采样）均对性能有显著贡献。

Abstract: This paper studies tactile-only 6-DoF pose refinement and belief maintenance for grasped objects in static and short quasi-static in-hand configurations where vision is unavailable or heavily occluded. The key difficulty is tactile partial observability: whole-hand taxel contacts are sparse, intermittent, and ambiguous under limited excitation and object symmetries. We propose a physics-informed particle filter on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ that updates pose beliefs from dense whole-hand tactile measurements. The likelihood combines active-contact signed-distance consistency, force-normal alignment, friction-cone feasibility, zero-force negative evidence, and optional feasibility guards. A sliding-window log-likelihood fuses recent tactile frames to reduce single-frame ambiguity, while a potential-field-guided proposal steers particles away from hand--object penetration. Symmetry-aware resampling preserves multiple plausible modes. Experiments on an Allegro Hand V5 with five objects show lower normalized ADD-S than tactile-only geometric, particle-filter, and learning baselines, and ablations confirm the benefits of temporal fusion, potential guidance, and mode preservation.

</details>


### [161] [Iterative Grasp Pose Refinement: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for 2D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17628)
*Amir Arsalan Nematollahi,Shayan Ahmadi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的机器人抓取精炼框架，结合关键点对象表示和深度Q网络（DQN），利用2D俯视图像生成并迭代精炼抓取候选，在Dex-Net数据集的300个对象上实现100%成功率（对几何算法原本无法抓取的对象），并通过Delta并联机器人的物理实验验证了sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让机器人具备理解和操作物体的能力，需要紧凑、可解释且可泛化的对象表征。传统的几何方法在复杂抓取场景中往往失效，而强化学习为解决接触丰富的操作任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的抓取精炼框架：1) 利用2D俯视图像中基于关键点的对象表征；2) 采用深度Q网络（DQN）作为学习算法；3) 通过几何算法先生成初始抓取候选；4) 框架迭代精炼抓取候选，将失败的抓取转变为成功的抓取；5) 在模拟环境中使用UR5机械臂进行训练和评估，并在Delta并联机器人上进行物理验证。

Result: 在Dex-Net数据集的300个对象上使用UR5机械臂进行实验，框架在几何方法认为不可抓取的对象上实现了100%的成功率；物理实验进一步验证了框架的sim-to-real迁移能力，精炼后的抓取成功操作了之前无法抓取的对象。

Conclusion: 研究表明强化学习在解决机器人抓取挑战中的有效性，为接触丰富的操作任务提供了可扩展和自适应的解决方案，并具备从模拟到现实的可迁移性。

Abstract: Developing robots capable of understanding and manipulating objects requires compact, interpretable, and generalizable representations. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based framework for robotic grasp refinement, integrating keypoint-based object representations with a Deep Q-Network (DQN). Using 2D overhead images captured in a simulated environment, a geometric-based algorithm generates initial grasp candidates, which are iteratively refined by the proposed framework, transforming failed grasps into successful ones. Experiments conducted on 300 objects from the Dex-Net dataset using a UR5 manipulator demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, achieving a 100% success rate on objects previously deemed ungraspable by geometrical methods. The framework's sim-to-real transferability is further validated through physical experiments on a Delta parallel robot, where a refined grasp successfully manipulates an object that was previously ungraspable. The findings underscore the effectiveness of reinforcement learning in addressing challenges in robotic grasping, offering a scalable and adaptable solution for contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [162] [OVIP-SG: Open-Vocabulary Instance-Preserving Scene Graphs for Mapping and Retrieval of Small, Fine-Grained Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17633)
*Tianjing Hao,Haiyu Lan,Angsong Li,Cheng Chen,Enyu Li,Jiarui Yang,Yuning Su,Peiwen Lin,Wang Chuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OVIP-SG，一个统一的开放词汇物体级3D场景图构建框架，解决开放词汇感知在3D场景图中的实例一致性问题，实现实例级语义建图、功能场景划分和小型细粒度物体检索，在Replica数据集上显著优于ConceptGraphs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇感知引入3D场景图是一把双刃剑：虽然视觉-语言检测器能恢复闭集模型遗漏的长尾类别和小型细粒度物体，但也会碎片化大表面、将小物体合并到大物体中，损害实例一致性并降低建图保真度。此外，现有方法难以检索先前未建图的目标或判断查询物体是否真正缺失，阻碍了鲁棒的具身开放世界导航与探索。

Method: OVIP-SG包含多个核心组件：(1) 利用视觉语言模型(VLM)枚举场景特定类别以进行鲁棒的开放世界检测；(2) 通过对称3D IoU关联和面积加权特征融合保留小型独立实例；(3) 利用VLM推断的物体功能将场景划分为紧凑的功能搜索区域；(4) 四阶段级联检索流水线，结合体素投票并基于探索覆盖率判断目标缺失。

Result: 在Replica的统一评估协议下，OVIP-SG较ConceptGraphs在类别平均准确率(mAcc)上提升6.31分，频率加权mIoU(F-mIoU)提升5.15分，类别无关原生实例全景质量(PQ)达0.398；搜索区域缩小至室内地面空间的21.8%，物体存在性分类的平衡准确率达0.773；真实机器人实验验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: OVIP-SG通过实例保持语义建图、功能场景分区和级联检索流水线的统一框架，有效解决了开放词汇感知引入的实例不一致问题，并支持缺失物体判定，为具身开放世界导航与探索提供了更鲁棒的3D场景理解方案。

Abstract: Integrating open-vocabulary perception into object-level 3D scene graphs is a double-edged sword. While vision-language detectors recover long-tail categories and small, fine-grained objects overlooked by closed-set models, they also tend to fragment large surfaces and merge small objects into larger neighboring objects, compromising instance-level consistency and undermining mapping fidelity. Moreover, existing methods struggle to retrieve previously unmapped targets or determine whether a queried object is absent, hindering robust embodied open-world navigation and exploration. We present OVIP-SG, a unified framework for instance-preserving semantic mapping, functional scene partitioning, and language-guided small, fine-grained object retrieval. OVIP-SG uses a vision-language model (VLM) to enumerate scene-specific categories for robust open-world detection. Symmetric 3D Intersection over Union (IoU) association and area-weighted feature fusion preserve small independent instances, while VLM-inferred object functions partition scenes into compact functional search regions. A four-stage cascaded retrieval pipeline further incorporates voxel voting and determines target absence from exploration coverage. Under a unified evaluation protocol on Replica, OVIP-SG outperforms ConceptGraphs by 6.31 points in class-mean accuracy (mAcc) and 5.15 points in frequency-weighted mIoU (F-mIoU) while achieving a class-agnostic native-instance Panoptic Quality (PQ) of 0.398. It reduces the search area to 21.8% of the indoor floor space and reaches 0.773 balanced accuracy for object-presence classification. Real-world robotic experiments further demonstrate its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [163] [Collective Ranking of Environmental Signals through Gaussian Belief Propagation in a Patrolling Robot Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17690)
*Zachary R. Madin,Connor York,Jonathan Lawry,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将多机器人巡逻图同时用作信念传播的因子图，利用高斯信念传播（GBP）在巡逻过程中对多个位置的信号进行集体排序，在仿真和四台Leo Rovers真实硬件实验中均优于简单平均和访问次数加权平均基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人巡逻通常以最小化空闲时间（idleness）为目标，但在安全和环境监测应用中，还需要对所有巡逻位置按某一测量信号进行集体排序，这是best-of-n问题到多选项连续值域的推广。已有方法缺乏对这一联合问题的有效解决框架。

Method: 利用巡逻图的双重身份——既是控制智能体移动的拓扑结构，又作为空间信念传播的因子图。在已访问节点上构建单变量测量因子，在巡逻边线上构建成对平滑因子，使用高斯信念传播（GBP）进行集体排序，并与简单平均和访问次数加权平均两种基线方法对比。

Result: 在多种传感器噪声条件下的仿真中，GBP在排序准确率、均方误差和收敛时间上均优于两种基线方法。随着噪声增大，GBP性能优雅退化，而两种平均方法性能显著恶化。在四台Leo Rovers对办公大厅中传播无线电信号的真实跟踪实验中，硬件结果复现了相同的性能排序。

Conclusion: 将巡逻图作为因子图进行高斯信念传播，能够有效利用空间平滑性先验，在巡逻任务中同时实现位置信号的高质量集体排序，且在高噪声条件下具有更强的鲁棒性，仿真与硬件实验结果一致，验证了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Multi-robot patrolling requires a team to visit all areas of an environment at regular intervals, typically minimising idleness. A practical extension, motivated by security and environmental monitoring, is to additionally form a collective ranking of all patrol locations by some measured signal, a generalisation of the best-of-n problem to the many-option, continuous-valued regime. We observe that the patrol graph admits a natural dual interpretation: it is simultaneously the topology that dictates agent movement and a factor graph over which spatial beliefs can be propagated. Exploiting this equivalence, we apply Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP), a graph-based algorithm, to collective ranking using unary measurement factors at visited nodes and pairwise smoothness factors along patrol edges. We compare GBP against simple and visit-count-weighted averaging across a range of sensor-noise conditions in simulation, and validate the approach on four Leo Rovers tracking a propagating radio signal in an office lobby. GBP outperforms both baselines on ranking accuracy, mean squared error, and time to consensus. We find that as noise increases and the task becomes harder, GBP degrades gracefully in simulation while both averaging methods degrade substantially. Hardware trials reproduce the same performance ordering on a real propagating radio signal, supporting the practical relevance of the simulated results.

</details>


### [164] [Force-Based Offset Estimation for Keyed Peg-in-Hole Assembly Using Local Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17691)
*Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Abilash Philip Madavath,Augustin Raju,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于力觉的偏移估计方法用于键槽销孔装配任务，嵌入"感知-验证-插入"流水线中，通过KNN-高斯过程混合回归器从腕部力/力矩测量中估计残余错位，将接触分为硬碰撞和导向倒角插入两种模式并分别建模，最终将插入成功率从67%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 键槽销孔装配任务对几何约束要求极为严格，且在不确定环境下对抓取位姿偏差高度敏感，导致装配失败率较高，需要一种能够从力觉数据中准确估计并补偿错位的方法。

Method: 提出感知-验证-插入(perception-validation-insertion)三阶段流水线：1) 感知阶段采用关键点检测的抓取与放置；2) 验证阶段利用抓取后单目视觉验证结果辅助确定性搜索；3) 插入阶段利用局部KNN-高斯过程混合回归器直接从腕部力/力矩测量估计残余错位。方法通过接触窗口持续时间阈值区分两种接触模式（硬碰撞与导向倒角插入），并为每种模式分配专用推理模型。

Result: 在协作机器人臂的集成力/力矩传感器实验中，应用该流水线后插入成功率从67%提高到87%，并在倒角销插入任务中实现了准确的径向偏移估计。

Conclusion: KNN结合基于单目视觉验证结果的确定性搜索可提升回归器精度，接触模式分类路由策略有效增强了力觉估计的鲁棒性，为键槽销孔类高精度装配任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Key-keyway assembly tasks impose strict geometric constraints and are highly sensitive to grasp pose deviations in uncertain environments. This work presents a force-based offset estimation method for keyed peg-in-hole assembly, embedded within a perception-validation-insertion pipeline. Residual misalignment is estimated directly from wrist force/torque measurements using a local KNN-Gaussian Process hybrid regressor. The framework distinguishes between two contact regimes, hard collision and guided chamfer insertion, and routes inference to a dedicated model for each. Regime classification is achieved via a contact-window duration threshold. KNN combined with a deterministic search using the results of a post-grasp monocular visual validation contributes to an increased accuracy of the regressor model. This approach achieves accurate radial offset estimation in chamfered peg insertion, during a keypoint detection-based pick and place application. Experiments using the integrated force/torque sensor of a collaborative robot arm showed an increase in insertion success rate from 67% to 87% after the pipeline was applied.

</details>


### [165] [Dijkstra as an Oracle for Online Stochastic Shortest Path Navigation with Provable Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17703)
*Mansur M. Arief,Ali Akarma,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明Dijkstra算法在比文献中常用的因果条件更弱的条件下（即确定化地图上的非负缩减代价）仍可作为精确规划引擎，并据此提出在线学习算法DORA，在保持安全约束的同时大幅降低规划计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在未知真实穿越代价和随机执行误差下需要低成本、安全到达目标。求解随机最短路径问题的精确方法（如值迭代）计算量随地图直径增长；Dijkstra算法虽快，但通常被认为在随机转移下不精确。现有结果缺乏对Dijkstra算法适用条件的精确定位。

Method: （1）理论分析：证明Dijkstra算法在"确定化地图上的非负缩减代价"这一弱于因果条件的条件下仍能精确求解。该条件比文献中常见的因果条件（causality condition）更弱。（2）算法设计：提出DORA（Dijkstra Oracle Reduced-cost Algorithm），采用固定次数的最短路径oracle调用，不估计转移核；在动态障碍物撞击概率需保持在预算内时，添加对数生存权重。（3）实验验证：在网格世界导航、定向钻井、无人机监视三个基准上对比测试。

Result: DORA在所有基准上都与获得真实转移核的乐观值迭代性能相当，但规划工作量减少4.5至19.3倍；相比determinize-and-replan方法，学习过程中的接触次数减少17倍；接触率保持在跨越两个数量级的预算范围内。

Conclusion: 最短路径搜索可以被用作安全和高效的在线导航与路径规划引擎。Dijkstra算法在弱化条件下仍能保证精确性，为随机环境中的机器人导航提供了计算效率与安全性兼顾的实用方案。

Abstract: Mobile robots that operate in side by side with humans and critical facilities must reach their goals at low cost, despite often unknown true traversal costs of the map apriori and imperfect actuation. Planners that solve the underlying stochastic shortest path problem exactly, such as value iteration, require computation that grows with the diameter of the map, whereas Dijkstra's algorithm is fast but is usually considered inexact once transitions are stochastic. This study shows that Dijkstra's algorithm can remain an exact planning engine under a condition that is much weaker than the causality condition often invoked in the literature, namely nonnegativity of a reduced cost defined on the determinized map. Building on this characterization, an online learner DORA (Dijkstra Oracle Reduced-cost Algorithm) is proposed for robot navigation that calls a shortest path oracle a fixed number of times per episode, never estimates a transition kernel, and adds a logarithmic survival weight when the probability of contact with a dynamic obstacle must stay within a budget. In the numerical experiments involving three other benchmarks that cover grid world navigation, directional drilling, and drone surveillance, the learner matches optimistic value iteration that is given the true transition kernel while performing 4.5 to 19.3 times less planner work, reduces contacts during learning by a factor of seventeen relative to determinize and replan, and keeps the contact rate within budgets that span two orders of magnitude. These results indicate that shortest path search supports safe and efficient online navigation and path planning tasks.

</details>


### [166] [CompCPZ: Preserving Multi-Modal Intent in Language-Guided Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17717)
*Zhen Zhang,Ahmad Hafez,Peng Xie,Yanliang Huang,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CompCPZ，一种代数层编译器，使语言条件机器人策略能够在语言解析树上递归组合原始约束多项式Zonotope包络，从而恢复多模态析取指令的表示能力。在ManiSkill3基准上以1,900/1,918配对胜利显著优于基线方法，并零调参迁移至真实机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件机器人策略存在结构性缺陷：将析取指令（如"红盘或蓝盘附近"）坍缩为单一连通集会导致语义静默失败——机器人看起来几何上成功，实际上并未满足指令的任一分支。此外，运行时模式不确定性使提前提交单一动作的规划器性能退化。

Method: CompCPZ是一种代数层编译器，语言条件学习系统可将其包裹以恢复多模态析取表示。具体而言，它沿语言解析树递归组合每基元的约束多项式Zonotope包络，采用无分布共形覆盖确保覆盖率保证，并以亚毫秒级运行时完成计算。

Result: 在ManiSkill3桌面操作闭环基准上，CompCPZ以1,900/1,918的配对胜利（p << 10^-30）显著超越凸集基线、多峰解码器和零样本视觉-语言-动作模型。同一编译器无需调参即可迁移至Unitree Go2四足机器人上的平面真实机器人实验（动作捕捉环境下）。

Conclusion: 组合语言接地应不仅评估是否到达解码目标，还应评估所表示的可行集是否保留用户意图的连通分量结构。CompCPZ证明了在语言解析树层面进行代数组合可以避免语义坍缩，并为语言条件策略提供了可验证的多模态保证。

Abstract: A robot asked to "place the cup near the red plate or the blue plate" may reach the centroid between them and appear geometrically successful, while satisfying neither disjunct of the instruction. This silent semantic failure exposes a structural limitation of language-conditioned robot policies: representations that collapse a disjunctive instruction into a single connected set cannot preserve all feasible modes, and planners that commit to one action degrade under run-time mode uncertainty. We address this limitation with CompCPZ, a sound algebraic layer that language-conditioned learning systems wrap to recover multi-modal disjunctive representation, recursively composing per-primitive constrained polynomial zonotope enclosures along the language parse tree with distribution-free conformal coverage and sub-millisecond runtime. On a closed-loop ManiSkill3 tabletop-manipulation benchmark, CompCPZ outperforms convex set baselines, multi-peak decoders, and a zero-shot vision-language-action model (1,900/1,918 paired wins, p << 10^(-30)); the same compiler also transfers without retuning to planar real-robot trials on a Unitree Go2 quadruped under motion capture. These results suggest that compositional language grounding should be evaluated not only by reaching a decoded target, but by whether the represented feasibility set preserves the connected-component structure of the user's intent.

</details>


### [167] [Stability Control for Real World Testing in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17779)
*Phillip Pitschi,Simon Sagmeister,Frederik Werner,Markus Lienkamp,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套综合稳定性控制系统，包括电子稳定控制（ESC）、滑移控制（SC）和反向转向系统（CS），用于保障自动驾驶车辆在动力学极限下的安全运行。系统通过自适应调整运动控制器的转向和制动指令来维持车辆稳定性，并在仿真与全尺寸实车上进行了验证，同时提供了开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车辆动力学极限（如自主赛车场景）下控制自动驾驶车辆极具挑战性。外部因素（如路况或天气）易导致车辆失稳，而大多数控制算法假设车辆行为稳定，在临界情况下可能失效。尤其在没有安全驾驶员操作的昂贵车辆（如自动驾驶赛车）中，这一问题尤为严峻，亟需一套保障车辆稳定运行的安全控制系统。

Method: 提出包含三个子系统的综合稳定性控制方案：电子稳定控制（ESC）负责维持车辆整体稳定性，滑移控制（SC）管理车轮滑移率，反向转向系统（CS）在车辆侧滑时施加反向转向指令。三个子系统协同工作，对上层运动控制器的转向和制动指令进行适应性地修改，以将车辆维持在稳定工作区域内。

Result: 通过在仿真环境和真实全尺寸车辆上的实验验证，结果表明该稳定性控制系统能够在临界情况下维持车辆稳定性，并扩大了车辆的可操作可行区域。

Conclusion: 该稳定性控制系统有效保障了自动驾驶车辆在动力学极限下的安全运行，并作为开源项目发布，便于其他研究者和工程实践集成使用。

Abstract: Controlling an autonomous vehicle at the limits of handling is a challenging task. Due to external influences, such as road conditions or weather, a vehicle can easily become unstable. Since most control algorithms assume stable vehicle behavior, they might fail in these situations. Especially when operating expensive vehicles without a safety driver on board, as in autonomous racing, this poses a significant challenge. To enable safe operation at the vehicle's dynamic limits, we present a comprehensive stability control system that safeguards motion control algorithms in autonomous driving. The proposed system consists of an electronic stability control (ESC), a slip control (SC), and a countersteer system (CS), which collectively adapt steering and brake commands from the motion controller to maintain vehicle stability. We validate our approach through both simulation and experiments on a real-world, full-scale vehicle. The results show that the stability control system maintains vehicle stability in critical situations and extends the operational feasible region. To simplify integration, we provide an open-source implementation at github.com/TUMFTM/tam-stability-control.

</details>


### [168] [Effector-Centric NMPC of Tiltable-Multirotors for Offset-Free Omnidirectional Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17819)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Johannes Kübel,Haokun Liu,Junichiro Sugihara,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对可倾转多旋翼在空中操作中的应用，提出了一种基于力/力矩（wrench）的控制框架，并验证了四旋翼可倾转配置在穿越奇异位形和执行全方位操作中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作（aerial manipulation）需要将机器人操作能力扩展到空中环境。与传统的带机械臂的空中系统相比，可倾转多旋翼可以直接通过飞行基座产生六自由度力/力矩，兼具高效运动和全方位操作能力。本文旨在解决该平台在设计中（螺旋桨尺寸、悬停效率、末端执行器布局）和控制中（奇异位形穿越、扰动补偿）的关键问题。

Method: 本文首先进行设计分析，论证四旋翼可倾转构型在无干涉螺旋桨尺寸与悬停效率间的平衡，并利用零空间冗余穿越奇异位形；提出向上布置末端执行器以兼顾几何间隙与可用力/力矩。控制上提出双策略扰动补偿：改进积分项处理模型误差，基于加速度的估计器处理外部力/力矩。在此基础上构建以末端执行器为中心的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，统一集成设计选择、奇异位形处理和扰动补偿。

Result: 所提框架在自建的可倾转四旋翼上以100 Hz全机载实时运行。真实世界实验包括90度阶跃翻转、白板推压和连续360度阀门旋转，验证了在每臂单自由度（one-DoF-per-arm）的可倾转四旋翼上实现基于力/力矩的全方位操作及奇异穿越的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文通过设计分析、控制框架构建和真实实验验证，证明可倾转四旋翼在无需额外机械臂的情况下即可实现全方位空中操作，为空中操作平台的设计与控制提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation extends robotic operations to previously inaccessible aerial environments. Unlike arm-equipped aerial systems, tiltable-multirotors can directly generate six-degree-of-freedom wrenches through their flight bases, enabling both efficient movement and omnidirectional operation by tilting the thrust direction.
  This work presents a design analysis and a wrench-based control framework for tiltable-multirotors in aerial manipulation. We show that a four-rotor tiltable configuration provides a balance between interference-free propeller sizing and hovering efficiency across different attitudes, and its null-space redundancy is crucial for traversing singular configurations under physical constraints. We further show that an upward end-effector placement yields a favorable trade-off between geometric clearance and available wrench. To address disturbances, we propose a dual strategy consisting of a modified integral term for model error and an acceleration-based estimator for external wrenches. Building on these insights, we develop an effector-centric nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework that integrates design choices, singularity handling, and disturbance compensation into a unified formulation.
  The proposed framework runs fully onboard at 100 Hz on a custom-built tiltable-quadrotor. Real-world experiments, including a 90-deg step cartwheel rotation, whiteboard pushing, and continuous 360-deg valve turning, demonstrate the feasibility of wrench-based omnidirectional manipulation with singularity traversal on a one-DoF-per-arm tiltable-quadrotor.

</details>


### [169] [Jetson-ORB-SLAM3: Accuracy-Preserving GPU Implementation for Edge Computing Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17874)
*Rajat Roy,Aditya Arun Kumar Yadav,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上精度保持的GPU版ORB-SLAM3实现。GPU ORB前端与参考CPU检测器算法一致（94.7%关键点精确匹配、99.9%描述子位一致），并通过原生TensorRT实现CNN回环检测的边缘端可行化。在EuRoC、TUM-VI和KITTI数据集上，GPU管线与CPU参考轨迹误差差异在0.10cm以内，证明了加速不损失精度；回环推理加速180倍至2.2ms，单目惯性模式下在7W设备上维持32FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低功耗边缘平台上，视觉惯性SLAM受到稠密特征提取和回环检测成本制约。先前的ORB-SLAM GPU移植通过近似ORB检测器来换取速度，改变了特征集合从而影响了估计轨迹的精度。因此需要一种既能在边缘端GPU上加速又不损失精度的ORB-SLAM实现。

Method: 将视觉前端（特征提取）卸载到GPU，后端（建图与优化）保留在CPU，实现计算与硬件特性匹配。GPU ORB前端算法级复现参考CPU检测器；回环检测采用CosPlace ResNet-50，通过原生libnvinfer FP16引擎（而非通用的ONNX-Runtime executors）在边缘端实现TensorRT加速。在Jetson Orin Nano和桌面平台上分别运行GPU管线与CPU参考，四组配置交叉对比验证精度。

Result: GPU ORB前端达到94.7%关键点精确一致性和99.9%描述子位一致性。EuRoC数据集上四种配置（GPU/CPU × Jetson/Desktop）的平均绝对轨迹误差（SE(3)）差异在0.10cm以内；TUM-VI六个房间序列中五个达到亚厘米精度；KITTI十一个序列中九个相对平移误差低于1%。回环查询推理2.2ms，较ONNX-Runtime加速180倍。单目惯性模式在11个EuRoC序列上维持32FPS平均值。

Conclusion: 该实现证明GPU移植和硬件变更均不会偏移估计轨迹，加速是精度保持的而非近似性的。原生TensorRT引擎使CNN回环检测能够在7W设备上与跟踪并发运行，整体系统在低功耗边缘平台上具备实用性和竞争力。

Abstract: Visual-inertial SLAM on low-power edge platforms is constrained by the cost of dense feature extraction and loop closure. Prior GPU ports of ORB-SLAM trade accuracy for speed by approximating the ORB detector, altering the feature set and therefore the estimated trajectory. We present an accuracy-preserving GPU implementation of ORB-SLAM3 for the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, whose GPU ORB front end reproduces the reference CPU detector algorithmically to 94.7% exact keypoint agreement and 99.9% descriptor bit agreement. This work also makes CNN-based loop closure edge-viable through native TensorRT. The visual front end (feature extraction) is offloaded to the GPU while the mapping and optimization back end is kept on the CPU, matching each computation to the hardware it suits. The accuracy is verified by comparing four configurations: the GPU pipeline and the unmodified CPU reference, each run on both the Jetson Orin Nano and a desktop. On EuRoC dataset, all four agree to within 0.10cm in mean absolute trajectory error (SE(3)), so neither the GPU port nor the change of hardware shifts the estimated trajectory. The GPU-versus-CPU comparison is reproducible on TUM-VI and KITTI datasets, so the acceleration is accuracy-preserving rather than approximate. The proposed implementation is competitive with published ORB-SLAM3 on EuRoC, attains sub-centimeter accuracy on five of the six TUM-VI room sequences, and reaches sub-1% relative translation error on nine of eleven KITTI sequences. For loop closure, the generic ONNX-Runtime CUDA/TensorRT execution providers are unusable with our CosPlace ResNet-50 on the embedded platform, whereas a native libnvinfer FP16 engine reduces per-query inference to 2.2ms, a 180x speedup. Learned place recognition therefore runs concurrently with tracking on a 7W device. In monocular-inertial mode the system sustains 32FPS mean over the eleven EuRoC sequences.

</details>


### [170] [ControlledShifts: Towards Standardizing Robustness Evaluation in Trajectory Prediction Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17882)
*Ingrid navarro,Pablo Ortega-Kral,Yutong Duan,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlledShifts框架与基准套件，通过统一的"特征化-划分"公式系统性地将现有轨迹数据集重新划分为分布内（seen）与分布外（unseen）部分，包含三个针对关键拓扑与行为分布偏移的基准，并提出统一鲁棒性分数从预测质量与预测稳定性两个互补维度评估模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型轨迹预测器在面对训练数据代表性不足的场景时会严重退化。现有方法虽尝试通过数据驱动或测试时自适应缓解分布偏移退化，但多沿碎片化的泛化轴线验证，缺乏标准化的跨偏移鲁棒性比较框架。

Method: 提出ControlledShifts框架，通过特征化函数（固定基准探测的偏移轴线）与划分函数（固定该轴线尾部如何被隐藏）统一定义分布内与分布外划分；构建三个覆盖关键拓扑和行为分布偏移的基准；提出统一鲁棒性分数，从预测质量（相对性能增益）和预测稳定性（偏移下性能保持）两个维度聚合多维性能指标。

Result: 通过基准测试主流的Transformer架构，揭示了不同容量模型在处理潜在相关性与环境结构时的关键差异。

Conclusion: ControlledShifts提供了一个标准化、可复现的轨迹预测鲁棒性评估框架，使跨分布偏移维度的模型比较成为可能，并揭示了模型容量与鲁棒性之间的深层关联。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is central to safety in autonomous driving, yet learning-based predictors tend to degrade sharply when encountering scenarios poorly represented by their training data. Many methods attempt to mitigate distribution shift degradation through data-centric or test-time adaptation approaches; however, they are typically validated along fragmented axes of generalization, leaving the field without a standardized way to compare robustness across shifts a model may encounter.
  To address this, we introduce ControlledShifts, a framework and benchmark suite that systematically re-splits existing trajectory datasets into in-distribution (seen) and out-of-distribution (unseen) partitions, via a shared characterization-and-splitting formulation, in which a characterization function fixes the axis of variation a benchmark probes and a splitting function fixes how the tail of that axis is withheld. The suite comprises three benchmarks targeting key topological and behavioral distribution shifts. Furthermore, to aggregate multi-dimensional performance metrics across these benchmarks, we propose a unified robustness score that evaluates models along two complementary dimensions: prediction quality (relative performance gain) and prediction stability (performance preservation under shift). We showcase ControlledShifts by benchmarking prominent transformer-based architectures, exposing critical differences in how models of varying capacities handle latent relevance and environmental structure.

</details>


### [171] [PRISM: Precision and contact-rich Real-world Industrial Skill dataset with Multimodal sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17962)
*Tengbo Yu,Jiahao Wu,Hanning Wang,Rui Chen,Chuanhou Liu,Chuang Sun,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个面向接触丰富型工业操作的大规模多模态数据集，包含25+操作任务、5000+轨迹（共45小时遥操作演示），以多视角RGB-D、力/力矩、触觉和机器人状态等同步多模态数据为特征，旨在填补现有机器人学习数据集在短时程、低接触任务上的空白，为高精度工业环境下的多模态感知与控制提供现实基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集大多聚焦于短时程、低接触任务（如抓取放置），缺乏对工业装配所需的精密控制、力/力矩或触觉调控以及多模态反馈的支持，因此亟需一个面向接触丰富型工业操作的大规模数据集。

Method: 构建PRISM数据集，涵盖25+种操作任务（如电子元件插拔、传送带分拣等），收集5000+条轨迹共45小时遥操作演示，并同步记录多视角RGB-D、力/力矩、触觉及机器人状态等多模态数据，覆盖多样的机械约束场景。

Result: PRISM提供了在真实制造环境中高精密工业约束下的多模态感知与控制基准，弥补了现有数据集在接触丰富型操作上的不足，可作为接触丰富、可泛化操作的开放基础数据集。

Conclusion: PRISM作为首个大规模接触丰富型工业操作多模态数据集，为真实制造环境下的机器人多模态感知与精密集控研究提供了开放、可复现的基准平台，进一步推动了接触丰富型、可泛化操作在工业场景中的落地应用。

Abstract: Recent progress in robotic learning has been fueled by large-scale datasets collected in everyday environments. However, most existing datasets emphasize short-horizon, low-contact tasks such as pick-and-place, and therefore do not capture the precision control, force/torque or tactile regulation, and multimodal feedback required for industrial assembly. To address this gap, we introduce PRISM, a large-scale multimodal dataset for contact-rich industrial operations. The dataset spans more than 25 manipulation tasks (e.g., electronic components plug/unplug, conveyor-based sorting) and covers diverse mechanical constraints. PRISM includes more than 5,000 trajectories totaling 45 hours of teleoperated demonstrations, recorded using synchronized multi-view RGB-D, force/torque, tactile, and robot-state measurements. In contrast to datasets collected in household or laboratory settings, PRISM provides a realistic benchmark for multimodal perception and control under high-precision industrial constraints, and serves as a foundation for contact-rich, generalizable manipulation in real-world manufacturing environments. The dataset is open-sourced at: https://tengbo-yu.github.io/PRISM/

</details>


### [172] [Hydra-0: Action Flow for Generalist World Modeling and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18077)
*Hongyu Li,Bowen Wen,Xinghao Zhu,Yixuan Wang,Yilun Du,Yunzhu Li,George Konidaris,Stan Birchfield,Soha Pouya,Chenran Li,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Hydra-0，一种以动作流（将机器人动作表示为像素运动）为条件的新型通才世界模型。该模型通过共享视觉接口实现跨具身、任务、环境和视频生成骨干的通用世界建模与控制，在机器人运动误差和物体运动误差上分别比动作条件基线降低90.4%和60.2%，支持零样本组合与数据高效适配。此外，该接口还涌现出逆模式，即从人类演示的物体流预测机器人动作，无需任务特定的专家演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通才世界模型往往以动作作为条件输入，但动作表示在不同具身形态、任务和环境中缺乏统一的接口，难以实现跨域泛化和共享学习。作者希望找到一种能够连接异构训练数据、开放式策略评估与机器人控制的统一视觉接口。

Method: Hydra-0将机器人动作表示为像素运动（action flow），以此作为世界模型的共享条件接口。具体包括：（1）以动作流为条件的通才世界模型，学习跨具身、任务、环境和多种视频生成骨干的动作后果；（2）零样本组合和数据高效的适配机制；（3）利用该接口的逆模式，将从人类演示中提取的期望物体流映射为兼容的机器人动作，再通过训练好的动作头将潜在特征转换为可执行动作。

Result: 相比动作条件基线，Hydra-0在机器人运动误差上降低90.4%，物体运动误差上降低60.2%。在RoboLab基准上，重放成功率与参考成功率之间的Pearson相关系数达到r=0.96。同时支持零样本组合和数据高效的域适配。

Conclusion: 动作流作为一种共享控制接口，能够有效连接异构训练数据、开放式策略评估与机器人控制。Hydra-0证明了该接口在通才世界建模与可控机器人操作中的巨大潜力，并揭示了从物体运动到机器人动作的逆涌现能力。

Abstract: We introduce Hydra-0, a generalist world model conditioned on action flow, which represents robot actions as pixel motion. This shared visual interface enables generalist world modeling and control by learning action consequences across embodiments, tasks, environments, and video-generation backbones. Our best configuration achieves 90.4% lower robot-motion error and 60.2% lower object-motion error than our action-conditioned baseline, while supporting zero-shot composition and data-efficient adaptation. On the RoboLab benchmark, Hydra-0 achieves a Pearson correlation of r=0.96 between replayed and reference success rates. Finally, we uncover an emergent inverse mode of this interface: a world action model that predicts compatible robot motion from desired object flow transferred from a human demonstration. A trained action head maps the resulting latent features to executable actions without requiring task-specific expert robot demonstrations. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of action flow as a shared control interface connecting heterogeneous training data, open-loop policy evaluation, and robot control.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Brief Announcement: Fair Binding for Hidden-State Authorization in Byzantine SMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17349)
*Arnab Mallick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究在拜占庭领导者下，对隐藏的消耗性资源进行安全活性的分配问题。作者将问题拆分为两个独立需求：一是正确副本的到达顺序必须约束提交顺序（公平排序）；二是已提交的首次请求必须绑定后续有效性（使冲突的后续请求无效）。通过显式的授权见证接口，作者给出了一个满足授权安全性和首次到达活性两方面的公平预约/使用协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可验证拜占庭容错（Byzantine SMR）假设副本能够评估有序命令的有效性。然而在代理授权（agent authorization）场景下，命令的有效性仅相对于一个已提交的策略状态，而验证者无法从日志中重构该状态。此时一个动作在旧状态被授权的证明只是历史性证明，本身并不能为后续使用保留隐藏资源。因此需要对这种隐藏消耗性资源的安全分配进行严格刻画。

Method: 作者隔离出两个独立的必要条件：(1) 公平排序——正确副本的到达顺序必须约束提交顺序；(2) 首次请求绑定——已提交的首次请求必须使后续冲突请求无效，而非仅仅记录首次请求曾被授权。作者定义了授权见证接口，并在一次性预约模型下给出公平预约/使用协议。

Result: 证明在隐藏且非前缀可恢复的策略状态下，两个需求是互不蕴含的独立条件。所提出的公平预约/使用协议同时满足授权安全性与首次到达活性。在可信FIFO准入条件下，两个需求合二为一，因为准入与执行是原子的；而在拜占庭SMR中，请求提交与使用在时间上是分离的。

Conclusion: 对于隐藏消耗性资源的拜占庭领导者安全分配，必须同时满足公平排序（到达顺序约束提交顺序）和首次请求绑定（冲突请求被使无效）两个独立条件。该框架将授权安全与首次到达活性统一在一个协议中，揭示了CSMR与拜占庭SMR在处理此类资源分配时的本质差异。

Abstract: Validated Byzantine SMR assumes that replicas can evaluate the validity of an ordered command. Agent authorization creates a different regime: a command may be valid only relative to a committed policy state that validators cannot reconstruct from the log. A proof that an action was authorized at an old commitment is then only a historical attestation, it does not by itself reserve the hidden resource for later use.
  We isolate two independent requirements for safe live allocation of a hidden consumable resource under a Byzantine leader. First, arrival order at correct replicas must constrain commit order, the gap addressed by fair-ordering protocols. Second, a committed first request must bind later validity: it must make conflicting later requests invalid, not merely record that the first request was once authorized. The second requirement is non-vacuous precisely because the current policy state is hidden and not prefix-recoverable. Using an explicit authorization-witness interface, we characterize the two distinct obligations in this one-shot reservation model and give a fair reserve/use protocol satisfying both authorization safety and first-arrival liveness. Under trusted FIFO admission the two requirements collapse because admission and execution are atomic, Byzantine SMR separates request commitment from use.

</details>


### [174] [Generalizing and accelerating consistency checking for non-transactional distributed storage systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17388)
*Kotikala Raghav,Aman Hassan,Brian Sajeev Kattikat,Patel Jay,RSRS Santhosh,Abhilash Jindal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文推广了Wing-Gong线性一致性检查算法，使其能够检查非事务性一致性保证（如ZooKeeper的有序顺序一致性）以及系统特定的一致性保证。实验表明该方法易于实现、减少测试假阴性、帮助调试、速度可提升370倍、可扩展更多并发客户端，并报告了6个新的一致性违规bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性一致性检查器基于Wing-Gong算法，只能检查线性一致性这一种一致性保证。然而分布式存储系统（如ZooKeeper）提供的是其他类型的一致性保证（如有序顺序一致性），且不同系统还有其特定的额外排序约束。使用线性一致性检查器来测试这些系统会产生误报（false negatives）和漏报，需要更通用的检查工具。

Method: 将Wing-Gong算法推广为更通用的形式：通过定义操作间的偏序关系（ordering constraints），将线性一致性中全局顺序的要求泛化为对任意指定的偏序约束进行可满足性检查。这样既可以覆盖传统线性一致性、有序顺序一致性等非事务性一致性保证，也可以根据系统规范自定义额外的排序约束。

Result: 在8个分布式存储系统上的实验表明：这种推广易于实现；减少了测试中的假阴性；有助于调试一致性违规；检查速度可提升最多370倍；在相同时间预算内可扩展到更多并发客户端。此外发现了6个新的一致性违规bug，其中5个无法被现有的一致性检查器发现。

Conclusion: 推广后的WG算法使一致性检查器更具通用性和灵活性，能够适应不同分布式存储系统的实际一致性语义，显著提升了测试效率和bug发现能力。

Abstract: Linearizability checkers check if an operation history, observed by concurrent clients, is linearizable. They are used in testing distributed storage systems, and use the classic Wing-Gong (WG) linearizability checking algorithm.
  In this paper, we generalize the WG algorithm to make linearizability checkers more versatile: we can check other non-transactional consistency guarantees, like ordered sequential consistency provided by Zookeeper. Equipped with this generalization, we can also check for system-specific consistency guarantees that introduce additional ordering constraints over operations in a history, as per the system's specification.
  Our experiments with 8 distributed storage systems show that checking for system-specific consistency guarantees is easy to realize, reduces false negatives in testing, helps debug consistency violations, can be up to 370x faster, and can scale to more concurrent clients within the same checking time budget. We report 6 new consistency violation bugs, out of which 5 could not be found with existing consistency checkers.

</details>


### [175] [Optimal Adaptive Multi-Valued Byzantine Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17552)
*Marc Dufay,Anton Paramonov,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了拜占庭协议（BA）从二进制值到L比特值的研究，通过新颖技术将消息复杂度和轮数复杂度与拜占庭实际数量f解耦，在同步、部分同步和异步三种设置下均实现了更优的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拜占庭协议具有线性延迟和二次消息复杂度，在大规模场景下不实用。Constantinescu等人的近期工作通过考虑实际拜占庭方数量f≤t来解耦n和t的依赖，但该工作严格限制了二元值的拜占庭协议。本文旨在将这些结果扩展到L比特值的拜占庭协议。

Method: 基于Constantinescu等人的框架，结合新颖技术，在最优弹性（同步t<n/2，否则t<n/3）下设计不同设置下的协议：同步下采用确定性协议，约简为O(f+log n)轮；同步和部分同步下采用确定性协议保持O(f)轮；异步下采用期望O(1)延迟的协议。

Result: 在同步设置下获得O(n·(L+f·κ))比特复杂度和O(f+log n)轮复杂度的确定性协议；在同步和部分同步下获得Õ(n·κ+t·(L+f·κ))比特复杂度和O(f)轮复杂度的确定性协议；在异步下获得Õ(n·κ+t·(L+t·κ))期望比特复杂度和期望O(1)延迟的协议。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将拜占庭协议从二元值扩展至L比特值，在三种网络假设下均实现了与实际拜占庭方数量f相关的复杂度优化，显著推进了大规模拜占庭协议的实际可行性。

Abstract: In Byzantine Agreement (BA), $n$ parties, out of which $t$ can be Byzantine, run a distributed protocol to agree on a common valid input. Traditionally, these protocols have a linear latency and quadratic message complexity, making them impractical at a large scale. In their recent work, Constantinescu, Dufay, Paramonov, and Wattenhofer consider the actual number of byzantine parties $f \leq t$ and work toward decoupling the dependency on $n$ and $t$ in the complexity. They obtain a BA protocol with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + t\cdot f)$ message complexity and $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(f)$ round complexity.
  However, their results are strictly limited to agreement on a binary value. Using the framework given by their work along with novel techniques, we extend these results for BA on an $L$-bit value. With $κ$ being a security parameter, and with optimal resiliency ($t < n/2$ in the synchronous setting or $t < n/3$ otherwise), we obtain:
  - In synchrony, a deterministic protocol with $\mathcal{O}(n\cdot (L + f \cdot κ))$ bit complexity and $\mathcal{O}(f + \log n)$ round complexity.
  - In synchrony and partial synchrony, deterministic protocols with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n \cdot κ+ t\cdot (L + f \cdot κ))$ bit complexity and $\mathcal{O}(f)$ round complexity.
  - In asynchrony, a protocol with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n \cdot κ+ t\cdot(L + t \cdot κ))$ expected bit complexity and expected $\mathcal{O}(1)$ latency.

</details>


### [176] [Counting in Population Protocols on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17590)
*Petra Berenbrink,Robert Elsässer,Tom Friedetzky,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Dominik Kaaser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在种群协议中计算节点数量的协议，使用O(n)阶状态，在高概率下于O(B(G)·log²n + L(G)·logn)次交互内稳定，其中B(G)为广播时间，L(G)为负载均衡时间。协议基于新型随机比特采样协议和以O(log log n)加性误差近似log n的协议，并给出了终止性均匀种群协议的计算不可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种群协议中节点是匿名且无标识的，计算顶点的数量（大小计数）是一个基础而重要的问题。现有工作主要针对完全图（完整交互图）设计，而本文旨在处理任意底层图G=(V,E)的种群协议，并在此更一般的设定下分析其复杂度。

Method: 本文的方法包括：(1) 提出在给定调度器确定发起者和响应者的条件下，采样独立随机比特的新协议；(2) 提出近似log n的协议，仅用O(poly log n)状态和O(B(G)·log²n)次交互，误差为O(log log n)，高概率成立；(3) 基于以上两个子协议，设计精确计数的协议，需要存在唯一领导者，使用O(n)阶状态。所有协议不依赖图G的任何知识。

Result: 精确计数协议在O(B(G)·log²n + L(G)·logn)次交互内高概率稳定，使用O(n)状态。近似log n的协议使用O(poly log n)状态和O(B(G)·log²n)次交互。此外，本文给出终止性均匀种群协议（无论有无领导者）计算图规模性质（如节点计数或奇偶性判定）的不可能性结果。

Conclusion: 本文在任意底层图的一般性设定下解决了种群协议中的大小计数问题，其协议复杂度以广播时间和负载均衡时间为参数。两个子协议（随机比特采样和log n近似）可独立使用。终止性不可能性结果刻画了均匀终止协议在计算图规模属性上的能力边界。核心协议需要唯一领导者，而两个子协议不需要。

Abstract: We consider the problem of counting the number of agents in a population protocol where the agents are connected by an underlying graph $G=(V,E)$ with $|V|=n$ nodes. In each step, a random scheduler selects an edge uniformly at random, and the incident nodes make a state transition. As per standard assumptions, agents are identical and anonymous, that is, have no identifiers. To break symmetry, in each interaction one of the agents is declared as the initiator uniformly at random. Our size counting protocol uses $\tilde O(n)$ states and stabilizes in $O( B(G) \cdot \log^2(n) + L(G) \cdot \log(n))$ interactions with high probability, where $B(G)$ is the broadcast time and $L(G)$ is the load balancing time. Our protocol is based on novel protocols for sampling independent random bits (given that the scheduler determines an initiator and responder) and approximating $\log n$ up to an additive error of $O(\log \log n)$ with high probability. The latter uses $O(poly\log(n))$ states and $O(B(G)\cdot\log^2 n)$ interactions. Both results may be of independent interest. The main protocol for exact counting requires the presence of a unique leader, the other two do not. None of the protocols requires any knowledge about the graph $G$. We conclude with impossibility results for terminating uniform population protocols that compute graph-size properties (like counting nodes or determining parity) with and without a leader.

</details>


### [177] [Bounded-State Restoration: Decoupling Local Restore Capacity from External LLM State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17826)
*Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出受限状态恢复（BSR）方法，将KV-cache系统中的完整发现与本地驻留分离，通过最多W个chunk的可复用窗口安装状态，使恢复工作集（RWS）从数十GiB压缩到500.75 MiB/rank（外部状态达31.277 GiB/rank时），同时保持总传输和安装工作量仍为Θ(|S|)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化KV-cache系统可以将长上下文LLM执行状态保留在GPU内存之外，但保留容量本身并不决定使状态重新可执行所需的本地内存量。论文指出需要隔离研究这一第二资源——恢复工作集（RWS），即恢复过程中生命周期重叠的峰值本地暂存状态。在已有流水线中，恢复大状态需要与状态大小相当的L1内存，这是资源瓶颈。

Method: BSR将完整发现与本地驻留分离：首先在不于L1物化全部命中状态的情况下探测完整可复用前缀，然后通过最多W个chunk的可复用窗口安装确认状态。在限定辅助状态条件下，峰值恢复容量为O(W)，而总传输与安装工作量保持Θ(|S|)。由于可复用状态跨异构分配组和张量并行rank，BSR采用请求级提交规则：部分安装永不作为有效可复用前缀暴露；失败会失效已公布前缀并回退到较低有效层级或确定性重计算。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash、TP=2、跨两个DGX Spark节点的实验中，外部状态从1.956增长到31.277 GiB/rank，而W=32时测量到的L1 RWS精确保持500.75 MiB/rank，实现63.959倍的外部状态与驻留暂存之比。524K token的全新运行重复验证了最大状态接受结果。层级和rank非对称故障仅暴露完全复用或回退前零外部复用。匹配的SSD优化将512K恢复TTFT从43.1秒降至17.6秒且不改变RWS。

Conclusion: BSR证明可以解耦KV-cache保留容量与本地恢复内存需求，用O(W)的有界驻留实现Θ(|S|)的总工作量，在保持恢复正确性和故障安全性的同时，将恢复内存需求压缩两个数量级以上。

Abstract: Hierarchical KV-cache systems can retain long-context LLM execution state beyond GPU memory, but retention capacity does not determine the local memory required to make that state executable again. We isolate this second resource as the restoration working set (RWS): the peak local staging state whose lifetimes overlap during restoration. In the pinned upstream LMCache whole-plan path, measured full-reuse points for 1.956, 7.823, and 15.646 GiB/rank states first succeed at 2, 8, and 16 GiB L1 rungs, with successful L1 peaks of 1.956, 7.824, and 15.648 GiB/rank.
  We introduce Bounded-State Restoration (BSR), which separates complete discovery from local residency. BSR probes the complete reusable prefix without materializing the whole hit in L1, then installs confirmed state through a reusable window of at most $W$ chunks. Under bounded auxiliary state, peak restoration capacity is $O(W)$ while total transfer and installation work remains $Θ(|S|)$. Because reusable state spans heterogeneous allocator groups and tensor-parallel ranks, BSR uses a request-level commit rule: partial installation is never exposed as a valid reusable prefix; failures invalidate the advertised prefix and fall back to a lower valid tier or deterministic recomputation.
  On DeepSeek-V4-Flash with TP=2 across two DGX Spark nodes, a clean no-resume sweep grows external state from 1.956 to 31.277 GiB/rank while measured L1 RWS remains exactly 500.75 MiB/rank at $W=32$, a 63.959x largest-state external-to-live-staging ratio. A second fresh 524K-token run repeats the largest-state acceptance result. Evaluated tier and rank-asymmetric failures expose either complete reuse or zero external reuse before fallback. A matched SSD optimization reduces 512K restore TTFT from 43.1 to 17.6 seconds without changing RWS.

</details>


### [178] [Multivalued Consensus: General Adversaries Require More Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17998)
*Mose Mizrahi,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究非阈值（一般）对手模型下的多方容错共识问题，基于有限射影几何构造了一族Q^d满足的对手结构，证明其在多种协议（交互一致性、拜占庭协议、广播、核心集合协商）中引发通信复杂度的下界，并给出匹配的紧致上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的容错共识下界大多针对阈值对手（如拜占庭故障数t<n/3），对一般（非阈值）对手结构的通信复杂度下界研究不足。本文旨在基于有限射影几何构造具体的对手结构，推导出在这些结构下的通信复杂度下界，并探索这些下界是否依赖于协议的终止特性。

Method: 1) 基于有限射影几何构造Z_proj^{n,d}和Z_2proj^{n,d}对手结构家族，满足Q^d条件；2) 利用"法定人数外部终止"论证建立下界；3) 设计非终止型容错可靠广播协议作为反例，证明不要求终止时下界不成立；4) 结合上界协议证明下界紧致。

Result: 1) 在Z_proj^{n,d}下，无错误R轮交互一致性协议需Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})位通信，拜占庭协议和广播需Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})位；2) 异步网络中可靠广播和拜占庭协议需Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})位；3) 核心集合协商需Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})位；4) 非终止协议仅需约(Ln + O(δn²log n))位，证明终止性是下界的关键；5) 给出了O(Ln^{1+1/d}+n²log n)位上界协议，证明下界紧致。

Conclusion: 本文通过有限射影几何构造的对手结构，系统性地建立了非阈值对手模型下各类共识协议的通信复杂度下界，并揭示了终止（停止发送消息）要求在产生高通信开销中的关键作用。这些结果在异步和同步网络中均成立，即使使用密码学也有效，并完成了下界的紧致性证明。

Abstract: We study $n$-party fault-tolerant consensus against general (non-threshold) adversaries. We describe an infinite family $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ of $Q^d$-satisfying $n$-party adversary structures based on finite projective geometry which cause error-free $R$-round protocols for interactive consistency on $L$-bit inputs to require $Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})$ bits of expected communication. Likewise, $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ causes error-free byzantine agreement and broadcast to cost $Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})$ bits. In every case, the lower bound is $Ω(L_{\mathsf{out}} \cdot n^{1+1/d})$ bits, where $L_{\mathsf{out}}$ is the output length.
  The family $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ also causes reliable broadcast and byzantine agreement to cost $Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})$ bits of expected communication in asynchronous networks. Moreover, there exists a related family $Z_\mathsf{2\textsf-proj}^{n,d}$ of $Q^d$-satisfying adversary structures that make core set agreement cost $Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})$ bits. These asynchronous lower bounds hold against send-omission adversaries, even if the protocol uses cryptography. Their basis is that if a quorum of non-faulty parties agree on an output and terminate, then the messages they sent before terminating must suffice for the parties outside the quorum to also terminate with the same output. Surprisingly, if we do not require the parties to terminate (stop sending messages) after they output, then these bounds no longer hold. We show this by designing a non-terminating omission-tolerant reliable broadcast protocol that can for any parameter $δ> 1$ be tuned to cost $(1 + \frac{1}{δ- 1})Ln + O(δn^2\log(δn))$ bits, which is of independent interest. Lastly, we show how to get termination with $O(Ln^{1+1/d} + n^2\log n)$ bits (assuming the $Q^d$ condition), and thus prove our asynchronous lower bounds tight.

</details>


### [179] [Minimizing Commit Rules for DAG-based Atomic Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18029)
*Petr Kuznetsov,Maxence Perion,Sara Tucci-Piergiovanni*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在基于DAG的拜占庭容错原子广播协议领域，定义了无认证轮次DAG构建上的提交规则及子规则关系，并据此分别确定了最终同步模型和异步模型下的最小提交规则。作者提出了新协议Minnow，可在最终同步（S-Minnow）和异步（A-Minnow）网络中实例化，这两者是首个依赖最小提交规则的DAG原子广播协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DAG-based拜占庭容错原子广播协议依赖各种提交规则来决定哪些顶点可以被全序投递。直观上，对DAG施加条件更少的提交规则意味着更低的投递延迟和更高的效率。然而，此前缺乏一个系统性的框架来比较和确定在给定模型下所需条件最少的提交规则，因此无法从理论上界定各协议提交规则的"最小性"。

Method: 本文在无认证（uncertified）的轮次（round-based）DAG构建基础上定义提交规则，并引入子规则（sub-rule）关系：若规则cr1提交的顶点集合总是包含规则cr2提交的顶点集合，则cr1是cr2的子规则。利用该偏序关系，分别在最终同步模型和异步模型中推导出所需条件最少的安全最小提交规则。基于这些最小提交规则，设计并实现了Minnow协议，包含两个变体：面向最终同步网络的S-Minnow和面向异步网络的A-Minnow。

Result: 作者识别出了最终同步模型下的一个最小提交规则和异步模型下的一个最小提交规则，并证明了它们的安全性。基于这些最小规则设计了Minnow协议（S-Minnow和A-Minnow），据作者所知，这是首个依赖此类最小提交规则的DAG-based原子广播协议。

Conclusion: 通过形式化提交规则的子规则关系并识别各模型下的最小提交规则，本文为DAG-based原子广播协议的效率提供了理论下界。Minnow协议验证了最小提交规则在实际协议设计中的可行性，表明其能够在保证安全性的同时减少DAG提交所需的条件，从而降低广播消息的投递延迟。

Abstract: A popular class of Byzantine fault-tolerant atomic broadcast protocols rely on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that represent causal relations between broadcast messages. Each process applies a protocol-specific \emph{commit rule} on its local DAG to determine which vertices can be delivered in a total order. Intuitively, commit rules that impose fewer conditions on the DAG to order its vertices imply more efficient protocols, as the broadcast messages are delivered with lower latency.
  In this paper, we define commit rules on an uncertified round-based DAG construction, and introduce a sub-rule relation between them: a commit rule $cr_1$ is a sub-rule of a commit rule $cr_2$ if, for any DAG, the set of vertices committed by $cr_1$ contains all vertices committed by $cr_2$. We use the sub-rule relation to determine a minimal commit rule in an eventually synchronous model and a minimal commit rule in an asynchronous model, namely, two commit rules that require the least conditions on the DAG to safely commit any set of vertices in their respective models. To the best of our knowledge, no existing DAG-based atomic broadcast protocol relies on such minimal commit rules: we introduce \emph{Minnow}, a new protocol for DAG-based atomic broadcast, which can be instantiated in both eventually synchronous (S-Minnow) and asynchronous networks (A-Minnow).

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Quantum-Safe Web Service Architecture Using Time-Based One-Time Passwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16961)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于后量子密码学（PQC）的量子安全Web服务架构开发，通过TLS/HTTPS实现传输安全，并利用HMAC驱动的TOTP实现Web服务安全。实验评估了SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3算法的计算性能，分析了不同硬件资源条件下的计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着OTP在多因素认证中的广泛应用，以及自动化连接和系统间互操作场景中对TOTP的需求日益增加，现有的Web服务安全架构需要适应后量子时代的安全要求，因此需要探索量子安全的Web服务架构。

Method: 采用PQC实现TLS和HTTPS传输安全管理，构建HMAC驱动的TOTP实现Web服务安全管理；在实验环境中对比评估SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3四种哈希算法的计算性能，并分析不同硬件资源条件下的计算时间。

Result: 研究提供了四种哈希算法在不同硬件资源条件下的计算性能对比数据，为未来Web服务部署中的算法选择和资源规划提供了参考依据。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了结合PQC与HMAC-TOTP的量子安全Web服务架构方案，并通过性能对比分析为实际部署提供了技术选型和资源规划方面的指导。

Abstract: One-Time Passwords (OTPs) have become a common option for multi-factor authentication in several applications. For instance, during website login processes, OTPs are often used in conjunction with traditional text-based usernames and passwords to verify whether the access request originates from a legitimate human user rather than an automated agent. However, in scenarios involving automated connections and system-to-system interoperability, Time-Based One-Time Passwords (TOTPs) may be required to establish secure connections and access Web Services (WSs). Therefore, this study focuses on exploring the development of a quantum-safe web service architecture. The proposed approach achieves transmission security management by implementing Transport Layer Security (TLS) and HyperText Transfer Protocol Secure (HTTPS) based on Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Furthermore, web service security management is realized through the construction of keyed-Hash Message Authentication Code (HMAC)-driven TOTPs. Within the experimental environment, this study evaluates and compares the computational performance of the Secure Hash Algorithm-2 (SHA-2), SHA-3, Ascon-Hash256, and SM3. The required computation time under different hardware resource conditions is analyzed for future web service deployment.

</details>


### [181] [Probing the Prefill: Detecting Code Vulnerabilities via Latent Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16970)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在读取C/C++代码时，其隐藏激活是否已包含漏洞信号。通过在4个LLM上提取最后一层prefill token的激活并训练MLP探针，在4个C/C++漏洞基准上平均F1达41.7%，其中在Devign上最佳探针（68.8% F1）接近微调分类器SOTA（67.9%），表明模型自身对代码漏洞的表征具有诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对LLM生成代码的安全防御都是事后性的（如静态分析器、微调分类器或用LLM作评审器筛查已完成的代码），忽略了生成模型自身的内部状态。作者试图验证一个更直接、可测量的假设：LLM读取代码时，其隐藏激活是否已包含该代码漏洞状态的信息，从而为模型原生的轻量级漏洞筛查提供依据。

Method: 从4个LLM（Granite-4.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Qwen3.6-27B、Gemma-4-12B，覆盖3个模型家族）中提取最后一个prefill token的激活，用这些激活训练MLP探针；在4个函数级C/C++基准（Devign、Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul）上评估探针的漏洞检测性能。

Result: 探针以13.4–16.0M参数（不到基础模型规模的0.2%）实现41.7%的平均F1。在Devign上，Qwen3.5-9B的最佳探针达到68.8%的F1，匹配了已发表的微调分类器SOTA（67.9%）；但在更困难且类别更不平衡的基准（Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul）上，探针与SOTA差距显著。

Conclusion: 这是早期证据表明编码LLM对任意代码自身的表征含有该代码漏洞状态的有用信息，激励未来朝轻量级、模型原生的漏洞筛查方向进一步研究。但该方法目前仅在部分数据集上达到SOTA，在困难基准上仍落后，尚不能替代现有防御手段。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation is now embedded in mission-critical pipelines, but defenses against vulnerable output remain post-hoc -- static analyzers, fine-tuned classifiers, or an LLM judge that screen completed code, ignoring the generating model's own internal state. We test a narrower, directly measurable question: when an LLM reads a piece of C/C++ code as context, do its hidden activations already carry a signal about that code's vulnerability status? We extract last prefill token activations from four LLMs (Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B) across three model families and train MLP probes on these activations. We evaluate them on four function-level C/C++ benchmarks (Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul). Our probes achieve 41.7\% average F1 using 13.4--16.0M-parameter probes -- under 0.2\% of base-model size. On Devign, the best probe (Qwen3.5-9B, 68.8\% F1) matches the published fine-tuned-classifier SOTA (67.9\%) despite reading only a frozen, general-purpose LLM's activations; on the harder, more imbalanced benchmarks (Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul) probes trail SOTA substantially. This is early evidence that a coding LLM's own representation of arbitrary code is informative about that code's vulnerability status, motivating further work toward lightweight, model-native vulnerability screening.

</details>


### [182] [Remote-Timer-as-a-Service: Efficient Microarchitectural Leakage in the Cloud with Remote Timers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17043)
*Martin Schwarzl,Haocheng Xiao,Albert Pedersen,Sam Ainsworth,Nigel Topham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Edge computing solutions have become a crucial part of the industry, delivering fast, flexible and scalable applications close to the end users, with typical use cases including dynamic content creation, image resizing and chatbots. Cloudflare Workers is one such framework, which handles millions of HTTP requests per second worldwide. To reduce start-up latency, Cloudflare Workers removes process-isolation boundaries between multiple tenants and leverages language-level isolation. This architecture poses the risk of Spectre attacks. To mitigate these, Cloudflare Workers previously introduced several countermeasures such as restricted timer measurements, no shared memory, no multithreading and Dynamic Process Isolation (DyPrIs), detecting potential attacks and process-isolating potentially malicious scripts.
  We demonstrate that the production implementation of DyPrIs was insufficient. We adopt microarchitectural amplification techniques and discover various possibilities to measure time in the production environment of Cloudflare Workers. Given these techniques, we show that freezing and coarsening timers in the Cloudflare Workers security model is insufficient. Leveraging both timing amplification and remote timers, we demonstrate a remote Spectre attack that leaks a JWT token from a co-located victim worker in the Cloudflare Workers production environment. We outperform the existing attack by orders of magnitude, going from 2 bit/min to up to 12 bit/s at an accuracy of 99.16%, posing an immediate risk to customer data. Following our end-to-end attack, Cloudflare Workers mitigated it in a coordinated effort by integrating the V8 Sandbox limiting transient access to 64-bit pointers, improving the detection capabilities of DyPrIs, and deploying hardware-assisted MPK-based in-process isolation to confine each tenant heap under a dedicated memory-protection key.

</details>


### [183] [CUSTOS: Toward Forensic-Ready Zero Trust at the Capture-Containment Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17068)
*Avinash Srinivasan,John Paramadilok*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CUSTOS，一个取证就绪的零信任参考架构，以取证管理点(FMP)为核心协调分层捕获、身份关联重建和遥测编排。实验显示始终在线的决策记录在网关以1.9-3.0%吞吐量代价实现哈希链上溯源性；反应式检查点约65ms但无法对抗直接SIGKILL约9ms的故意自毁（取证粉碎效应）；并发捕获+SIGKILL在1000次试验中0次恢复密钥，而FMP屏障后的顺序SIGKILL实现1000/1000恢复。身份导向遥测填充64-75%的决策记录模式，优于网络导向的18-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构以持续验证取代隐式信任，但mTLS、临时工作负载、身份中心控制与自动化修复导致载荷可见性降低、基于IP的归因弱化、易失性证据获取窗口缩小，使传统取证方法失效。

Method: 提出CUSTOS参考架构，引入取证管理点(FMP)协调分层捕获、身份与策略关联重建、遥测编排及ZT控制的取证访问。构建组件级原型，包含实时强制执行网关及独立运行时与编排器实验。

Result: 始终在线决策记录以1.9-3.0%吞吐量代价在网关哈希链上实现；反应式检查点约65ms但因约9ms的直接SIGKILL和内核级强制及自毁机制失效；并发捕获+SIGKILL恢复率为0/1000，FMP屏障后顺序SIGKILL恢复率为1000/1000；身份导向遥测填充64-75%模式，优于网络导向的18-30%。

Conclusion: 取证就绪的ZT需要同时具备始终在线的证据基础和受控的反应式捕获，同时须明确识别仍无法恢复的易失性证据域。

Abstract: Zero Trust (ZT) replaces implicit trust with continuous verification, but mutual TLS, ephemeral workloads, identity-centric control, and automated remediation reduce payload visibility, weaken IP-based attribution, and shrink the window for acquiring volatile evidence. We propose CUSTOS, a forensic-ready ZT reference architecture centered on a Forensic Management Point (FMP) that coordinates tiered capture, identity- and policy-linked reconstruction, telemetry orchestration, and ZT-controlled investigative access. We evaluate a composed, component-level prototype using a live enforcement gateway plus separate runtime and orchestrator experiments. An always-on decision record is captured and hash-chained on the gateway at a 1.9-3.0\% throughput cost on in-process policy engines, preserving decision provenance outside the monitored workload under stated trust assumptions. Reactive checkpointing (about 65 ms) precedes seconds-scale defender-routed eviction but loses to unsequenced direct SIGKILL (about 9 ms), in-kernel enforcement, and adversarial self-destruction, producing the forensic shredder effect. On a real container, concurrent capture and SIGKILL recovered the planted secret in 0/1000 trials; sequencing SIGKILL behind the FMP barrier recovered it in 1000/1000. The primary integrated single-node Kubernetes race checkpoints an FMP-controlled process; container-memory capture is evaluated separately and was unavailable in the managed-Kubernetes configuration. Across five public benchmark datasets and a synthetic schema reference, identity-oriented telemetry populates 64-75\% of the decision-record schema against 18-30\% for network-oriented, while rate limiting bounds the full-memory admission ceiling. These results show that forensic-ready ZT requires both an always-on evidentiary floor and bounded reactive capture, while identifying where volatile evidence remains unrecoverable.

</details>


### [184] [SentryBus: A Multi-Vantage Observability Model and Validated Instrument for I2C Sensor-Interface Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17082)
*Sandesh More,Elton Batista,Karla Daley,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究医疗物联网、无人机和网络物理系统中传感器接口被攻击者操纵的问题。SentryBus通过被动监控I2C传感器总线的事务时序、读写序列等特征，建立采集行为模型，尝试检测接口操纵。文中报道了三项测量结果：内联拦截器在采集服务时间上的边界为0.842%，稳态采集可持续6304秒，数据转换特征不可跨会话迁移。目前尚无受控攻击试验的检测率数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器驱动系统在数据到达嵌入式处理器后就信任测量值，而位于传感器与处理器之间的数字接口的攻击者可以注入看似合理的数据并被固件接受。因此需要一种被动、宿主机侧的方法来检测此类操纵，而不是在数据层面依赖事后校验。

Method: SentryBus对I2C传感器总线上的采集行为进行建模，特征包括事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器与FIFO状态访问以及原始数据转换。攻击者模型涵盖内联拦截器、并行控制器、传感器替换和受侵主机。采用双面测试台同时捕获两条总线、宿主机内存和遥测数据，检测器仅使用面向宿主机的总线数据，其余视角作为地面真值。

Result: 三个测量结果：(1) 内联拦截器在采集服务时间上被界定在0.842%以内，同时保持采集调度和负载内容不变；(2) 遥测视角的稳态采集在6304秒内稳定，无时钟回归；(3) 数据转换特征编码了会话特定的信号统计量，不可跨捕获会话迁移（阴性结果）。此外，低成本分析器可截断捕获且不产生内核可见错误。

Conclusion: SentryBus确立了被动宿主机侧监控可测量地刻画采集边界能力的前提验证，但受控攻击试验尚未完成，因此尚不宣称具体检测率。工作验证了方法可行性与测量基础设施的有效性。

Abstract: Sensor-driven systems in medical Internet of Things devices, drones, and cyber-physical systems commonly trust a measurement once it reaches the embedded processor. An adversary on the digital interface between sensor and processor can supply a plausible value that correct firmware accepts and reports as ordinary telemetry. The hypothesis is that sensor interface manipulation leaves observable evidence on the acquisition path, that the evidence appears at different vantages depending on attacker position, and that a passive host-side monitor therefore has a measurable boundary beyond which manipulation becomes indistinguishable from legitimate acquisition. SentryBus models acquisition behavior on the I2C sensor bus using transaction timing, read and write sequences, transfer lengths, address behavior, register and FIFO state access, and raw data transitions. The adversary is modeled as an inline interposer, parallel controller, sensor replacement, or compromised host, because a commodity target-only sensor cannot initiate transfers or stretch, reorder, or delay bus transactions. A dual sided testbed captures both busses, host memory, and telemetry, and the detector consumes the host facing bus alone while the remaining vantages serve as ground truth. A physiological instantiation reports three measured results: an inline interposer bounded at 0.842 percent of acquisition service time while preserving acquisition schedule and payload content, clean acquisition stability sustained over 6304 seconds at the telemetry vantage with no clock regression, and a negative result establishing that data-transition features encode session specific signal statistics and do not transfer across capture sessions. Instrument characterization shows that a low-cost analyzer can truncate captures without kernel visible error. Controlled attack trials are still outstanding, so no detection rate is claimed.

</details>


### [185] [Structured Driving-State Narratives for Small Language Model-Based GNSS Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17092)
*Abyad Enan,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的GNSS欺骗攻击检测与分类框架，通过将GNSS与多源传感数据转化为结构化语义叙事，输入SLM进行实时检测与分类。实验表明SLM在精度上与大型语言模型（LLM）相当（平均准确率96.99%，F1分数97.18%），且推理延迟和GPU内存占用更低，适用于资源受限的车载计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆依赖GNSS定位，但欺骗信号可诱导车辆产生看似合理实则错误的行驶状态。现有的欺骗检测方法需要高计算资源，难以在车载嵌入式平台上实时部署，因此需要一种计算高效且可靠的检测框架。

Method: 将GNSS与其他传感源的独立驾驶状态数据转化为结构化语义叙事（structured semantic narratives），输入小语言模型（SLM）进行欺骗检测与攻击分类。框架覆盖五类场景：无攻击、超调攻击、静止攻击、逐向攻击和错误转向攻击。性能与在相同训练数据上微调的LLM进行对比，并在地理未见过的实地数据（南卡罗来纳州克莱姆森）上验证。

Result: SLM在检测精度上与LLM相当：平均准确率96.99%，精确率99.05%，召回率95.59%，F1分数97.18%。在推理延迟和GPU内存占用（微调与推理阶段）方面，SLM均优于LLM。地理未见数据上的评估也验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该SLM框架能够在较低计算和内存资源下实现实时的GNSS欺骗攻击检测与分类，适合部署在资源受限的车载计算平台上。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) depend on reliable Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning. However, spoofed GNSS signals can induce plausible but incorrect vehicle states. This study develops a small language model (SLM)-based framework for detecting and classifying GNSS spoofing attacks by comparing vehicle behaviors independently derived from GNSS and other sensing sources. The framework converts independent driving states from GNSS and other sensing sources into structured semantic narratives that are provided to an SLM for spoofing detection and attack classification. The performance of the SLM-based framework is compared with large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on identical training data and evaluated on the same test set. The evaluation considers five classes: no attack, overshoot attack, stopped attack, turn-by-turn attack, and wrong-turn attack. The framework is also evaluated with geographically unseen field data collected in Clemson, South Carolina, United States. Experimental results indicate that the evaluated SLMs achieve performance similar to the LLMs, achieving an average accuracy of 96.99%, precision of 99.05%, recall of 95.59%, and F1-score of 97.18%. In terms of computational efficiency and resource utilization, the SLMs demonstrate advantages over the LLMs by requiring lower inference latency and less GPU memory during both fine-tuning and inference. Evaluation using field data collected in a geographically distinct location further demonstrated its efficacy. The presented framework can detect and classify GNSS spoofing attacks in real-time while requiring relatively low computational and memory resources, and is therefore suitable for deployment on resource-constrained vehicular computing platforms.

</details>


### [186] [Digital Twin-Based Intrusion Detection for Vehicle Powertrain CAN Bus Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17093)
*Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生（DT）的汽车控制器局域网（CAN）入侵检测系统，通过共享编码器LSTM联合建模17个解码后的动力总成信号，利用预测与观测之间的残差检测保持正常通信模式的隐蔽载荷攻击。在72%以上检测率下，对连续漂移和伪装攻击分别达到94.6%和89.2%的检测率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN入侵检测系统主要针对消息时序、频率或序列的异常，无法检测保持这些属性但操纵载荷payload的攻击。数字孪生此前仅用于模拟CAN流量和生成攻击场景，尚未被用于入侵检测本身。

Method: 构建共享编码器LSTM数字孪生模型，在22步窗口（原文为24步，按原文）内联合预测7个数值信号和2个分类挡位信号，共使用17个来自真实Hyundai/Kia CAN日志的解码信号。检测机制为：当残差超过校准阈值时标记该时间步为异常，并采用自适应滚动策略防止持续污染损坏孪生模型的输入历史。

Result: 针对四种攻击（平台攻击、连续漂移、伪装攻击、挡位伪装攻击）进行评估，DT模型在所有攻击类型上均优于基于范围和合理性的基线方法。连续漂移检测率达94.6%，伪装攻击达89.2%，而基线方法几乎无法检测任何伪造载荷攻击。但误报率高达39.6%，表明在持续攻击下鲁棒性仍需改进。

Conclusion: 学习耦合的车辆动力学特征能够检测保持正常CAN通信模式的隐蔽载荷操纵攻击，DT-based IDS展示了在行为级网络安全方面的应用潜力。然而误报率过高的问题需要在未来工作中解决，以提升其在持续攻击场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing automotive intrusion detection systems (IDSs) for the Controller Area Network (CAN) largely target discrepancies in message timing, frequency, or sequencing and cannot detect attacks that preserve these properties while manipulating the payload. Digital twins (DTs) have been used to emulate CAN traffic and generate attack scenarios for IDS evaluation, but their use for intrusion detection remains unexplored. This study develops a DT-based IDS that jointly models physical relationships among decoded powertrain signals and identifies attacks through residuals between predicted and observed behavior. A shared-encoder LSTM DT was trained on 17 decoded signals from a real Hyundai/Kia CAN log to jointly predict seven numeric and two categorical gear signals over a 24-step window. A timestep is flagged when a residual exceeds a calibrated threshold, while adaptive rollout protects the twin's input history from sustained contamination. Four attacks (plateau, continuous drift, masquerade, and gear masquerade) were evaluated against the twin and a range-and-plausibility baseline. The DT outperformed the baseline across all attacks, achieving detection rates of 94.6% for continuous drift and 89.2% for masquerade, while the baseline detected almost none of the fabricated payload attacks. These results demonstrate that learning coupled vehicle dynamics enables detection of stealthy payload manipulations that preserve normal CAN communication patterns. False positive rates reached 39.6%, highlighting the need for improved robustness under sustained attacks. The DT-based IDS shows promise for detecting stealthy payload-level CAN attacks that preserve normal communication patterns, supporting behavior-based cybersecurity for connected and automated vehicles.

</details>


### [187] [Protocol-Embedded Compliance for Privacy-Preserving, Non-Custodial Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17145)
*Santiago De Simone,Geoffrey Goodell,Georgios Samakovitis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非托管、类似现金的数字资产参考架构和核心协议规则，通过将合规数据以加密签名方式嵌入资产状态，在协议层面实现合规执行，同时保留用户隐私和强审计能力，并可对接现有支付系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为支付基础设施必须采用托管式、基于账户的模型才能保证交易完整性、可审计性和合规验证（特别是AML）。本文挑战这一假设，主张非托管、现金式数字资产同样能具备这些能力，且可能更安全。

Method: 基于Goodell等人提出的不可伪造、有状态、不经意（USO）资产模型，构建参考架构和核心协议规则。合规数据作为由独立实体签发的加密签名认证直接嵌入资产状态中；交易仅在满足合规谓词并将结果签名包含到资产状态后才有效。合规执行在协议层完成，而非通过机构托管或基于身份的账户控制。

Result: 本文提出的模型能够成功与现有支付系统交互，使得非托管、合规验证的交易可以集成到传统金融基础设施中，在保留有意义用户隐私的同时实现强审计性。

Conclusion: 非托管、现金式数字资产可以通过协议级合规执行替代机构托管，实现隐私保护与可审计性的兼顾，并可无缝对接传统支付基础设施。

Abstract: Received wisdom on payments infrastructure strongly supports the custodial, account-based model as a necessity for transaction integrity, auditability and verification; the set of fundamental primitives for regulated digital money exchange, the argument goes, necessitates designated identifiable entities that store and process credentials, perform KYC, and ultimately act as the 'single version of the truth' for compliance remediation and, most important, AML. In this paper, we propose this is not the case, by arguing that non-custodial, cash-like digital assets can embody such capabilities, in an arguably more secure manner.
  To that end, we present a reference architecture and core protocol rules for digital-value-exchange systems that preserve meaningful user privacy while enabling strong auditability. The protocol defines the conditions under which digital asset creation, transfer, and redemption are valid. The architecture specifies the allocation of actors, roles and components through which these rules operate, enabling independent verification of transaction compliance with applicable norms. Building upon the Unforgeable, Stateful, Oblivious (USO) asset model of Goodell et al., regulatory compliance data are embedded directly into the asset state as cryptographically signed attestations issued by independent entities. A transfer is valid only upon satisfaction of applicable compliance predicates and inclusion of the resulting signature within the asset state. Compliance enforcement is thus performed at the protocol level rather than through institutional custody or identity-based account control. We conclude that our proposed model can successfully interface with existing payment systems, making it possible to integrate non-custodial, compliance-verified transactions with legacy financial infrastructure.

</details>


### [188] [Picture the Epsilon: Pursuing Identity-Level Privacy Guarantees for Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17147)
*Arman Zareian Jahromi,Vishnu Bondalakunta,Mohammad Akbar Bin Shah,Naimul Haque,Shuangqing Wei,George T. Amariucai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种用于审计预训练黑盒人脸生成器身份级差分隐私（DP）的ε估计方法进行了比较研究：GaussMech、KDE-LR、MMD-TV和ROC-HT。实验在FaceFusion和InstantID上开展，发现四种方法均显示显著的个体可区分性，但给出的ε估计差异较大，且在高可区分性区间内无法可靠地对这些方法进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到图像的人脸生成器被广泛使用，其输出与源图像之间的视觉不相似性有时被当作隐私保护的证据。然而，要正式审计这些系统是否满足身份级(ε, δ)-差分隐私，需要在多种将嵌入空间观测转化为DP参数ε估计的路径之间做出选择。目前缺乏对这些不同审计方法的系统比较，因此需要明确各方法的假设、局限性和适用区间。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的身份级审计框架，比较了四种适用于预训练黑盒人脸生成器的审计方法：(1) GaussMech：基于高斯机制的逐身份敏感性估计；(2) KDE-LR：基于逐维度核密度估计的log-ratio并通过基本组合聚合；(3) MMD-TV：通过最大均值差异经全变差距离推导纯DP ε的解析种群级下界；(4) ROC-HT：基于交叉验证分类器out-of-fold ROC的假设检验评估。论文对每种方法明确了其假设、超参数依赖、有限样本局限性和ε估计的有效区间。

Result: 在FaceFusion和InstantID上跨多种身份编码器和参考数据集进行实验，四种审计方法均一致揭示了显著的个体可区分性，但报告的ε估计值差异显著，反映了各方法不同的假设和有限样本处理方式。在高可区分性区间内，实验数据不支持对四种方法进行可靠排序。

Conclusion: 四种审计方法的相对权衡应在部分隐私机制上进行评估，这被确定为下一步的自然研究方向。该框架将各审计方法置于共享的身份级审计场景中，阐明了它们的假设和有限样本处理如何塑造最终的差分隐私估计结果。

Abstract: Image-to-image face generators are widely used, and visual dissimilarity between their outputs and source images is sometimes treated as evidence of privacy. Auditing whether these systems satisfy formal identity-level (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy requires choosing among several distinct routes for converting embedding-space observations into estimates or bounds on the differential privacy parameter epsilon. We present a comparative study of four such audits applicable to pre-trained, black-box face generators: a Gaussian-mechanism reading of per-identity sensitivity (GaussMech); a per-dimension kernel-density log-ratio aggregated by basic composition (KDE-LR); an analytical population-level lower bound on pure-DP epsilon derived from the maximum mean discrepancy via the total variation distance (MMD-TV); and a hypothesis-testing evaluation of a cross-validated classifier's out-of-fold ROC (ROC-HT). For each method we make explicit its assumptions, hyperparameter dependence, finite-sample limitations, and the regime in which its epsilon estimate is informative. Applied to FaceFusion and InstantID across multiple identity encoders and reference datasets, the audits consistently reveal substantial identity distinguishability while reporting markedly different epsilon estimates that reflect each method's distinct assumptions and finite-sample treatment. In this high-distinguishability regime, the experiments do not support a reliable ranking of the four methods. Their relative trade-offs should be evaluated on partially private mechanisms, which we identify as the natural next study. The resulting framework places these audits in a shared identity-level audit setting and clarifies how their assumptions and finite-sample treatments shape the resulting differential privacy estimates.

</details>


### [189] [Authorization Before Context: A Model-Neutral Audience Boundary Against Cross-Audience Memory Leakage in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17148)
*Sibo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在个人语言代理的"记忆→上下文"转换阶段强制执行"授权边界（authorization before context）"的方法：采用单一的、反单调的受众成员规则，确保任何事实只有在当前所有观看者原本就属于该记忆项的受众时才会被组装进上下文，从而从机制上排除跨受众泄密与污染记忆扩权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人语言代理会从某一受众处学习事实，并可能随后将其放入为另一受众组装的提示词中。这一"记忆→上下文"的转换是一个攻击面：模糊或不一致的渠道、跨受众窥探以及被投毒的记忆，都可能导致系统组装出"与查询相关但当前观看者无权查看"的上下文。需要一个独立于模型行为的、确定性的机制边界来保证授权。

Method: 在记忆→上下文转换阶段引入单独的、反单调的受众成员资格规则（anti-monotone audience-membership rule）。具体做法：每条记忆条目携带其记录时的受众；当前观看者集合从通道元数据读取，元数据模糊时回退为公开；仅当每个当前观看者原本已属于该条目的受众时，条目才被允许进入上下文。该规则通过"排除"而非"模型行为"来保证授权，构成一个模型无关的不变量：被禁止的事实必须在模型被调用之前就缺失于最终组装的上下文中。

Result: 在合成的Contextual-Integrity测试集上，所提出的边界组装出的上下文中没有任何被禁止的事实进入，而无范围限制的基线方法则按构造必然包含此类事实；进一步审计确认每条读取路径均以失败关闭（fail closed）。证据为初步且合成性质。

Conclusion: 该"授权先于上下文"的规则为参与者在跨渠道场景下提供完整的回忆能力，同时通过排除机制保证：为更狭窄受众记录的内容不会到达更广泛的受众，且被投毒的记忆无法自行扩大其受众范围。该边界位于"模型被调用之前"，因此是模型无关的、可证明的上下文组装不变量。仍需更广泛、更真实的验证。

Abstract: A personal language agent learns a fact from one audience and may later place it in the prompt it assembles for another. This memory-to-context step is an attack surface: ambiguous or inconsistent channels, cross-audience prying, and poisoned memory can each cause the system to assemble context containing a fact relevant to the query yet unauthorized for the current viewers. We introduce authorization before context: a single, anti-monotone audience-membership rule applied at the memory-to-context transition. Each item carries the audience present when it was recorded; the current viewer set is read from channel metadata and falls back to public when ambiguous; and the item is admitted only when every current viewer already belonged to its audience. We prove that this rule gives every participant cross-channel recall while ensuring, by exclusion rather than by model behavior, that nothing recorded for a narrower audience reaches a broader one and that poisoned memory cannot widen its own audience. The boundary is a model-neutral invariant on the exact assembled context: a forbidden fact must be absent before the model is called. On a synthetic Contextual-Integrity suite, no forbidden fact entered the context our boundary assembled, whereas unscoped baselines included such facts by construction; we further audit that every read path fails closed. The evidence is preliminary and synthetic.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond the Hype: Evaluating LLM Integration and Practical Limitations in Security Operation Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17154)
*Elnaz Rabieinejad,Ali Dehghantanha,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Sarina Dastgerdy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对20名SOC从业者的半结构化访谈，揭示了LLM在安全运营中心的应用现状：在低风险、可快速验证的任务中能带来时间节省，但从业者普遍将LLM输出视为初稿而非决策级结论；由于输出不可靠和推理不透明，LLM在高风险安全决策中的信任度有限。作者据此提出了LLM集成成熟度评估框架，并呼吁关注可审计性和透明解释机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在SOC中的探索日益增多，但从业者对幻觉、推理不透明和验证成本等核心运营顾虑缺乏系统的实证研究。现有文献多关注技术能力，而忽视了实际使用者对LLM输出可信度和工作流适配的真实看法，亟需从业者视角来指导安全采用。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方法，对20名SOC从业者（包括一线分析师、SOC管理层和工具开发者）进行深入定性研究，通过访谈获取从业者对LLM在SOC工作流中实际使用体验、信任评估、验证实践的第一手描述，并在此基础上归纳提炼成熟度评估框架和研究议程。

Result: 研究发现：1) 从业者报告LLM在低风险、快速可验证任务（如日志摘要、初步调查线索）上有感知时间节省；2) 从业者一致将LLM输出定位为初步草稿或建议，而非决策级结论；3) 由于输出不可靠和推理不清晰，对高风险安全决策的信任有限；4) 依赖临时性验证规范和持续人工监督，而非标准化缓解流程；5) 基于访谈提出LLM集成成熟度评估框架及强调可审计性和透明解释的研究议程。

Conclusion: LLM在SOC中的当前应用价值主要局限于低风险辅助性任务，距离成为决策级工具仍有较大差距。实现更安全采用的关键在于：建立标准化的验证与缓解机制（替代现有临时性做法），开发可审计和可解释的模型推理机制，并通过成熟度框架引导组织分阶段推进LLM集成。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored within Security Operation Centers (SOCs) to support text-heavy analytical work such as alert contextualization, incident summarization, and drafting investigative artifacts.
