Despite this interest, practitioners describe critical operational concerns, most notably hallucinations (plausible but incorrect outputs), opaque reasoning, and the verification effort required to safely use model-generated content in security workflows. In this paper, we present findings from semi-structured interviews with 20 SOC practitioners spanning frontline analysts, SOC managers, and tool developers. Participants report perceived time savings for low-stakes tasks that are quickly verifiable (e.g., summarizing logs or drafting initial investigative leads), but they consistently frame LLM outputs as preliminary drafts and suggestions rather than decision-grade conclusions. Participants also describe limited trust in LLMs for high-stakes security decisions due to unreliable outputs and unclear model reasoning, and they report relying primarily on ad-hoc verification norms and continuous human oversight rather than standardized mitigation procedures. Based on these interview-grounded accounts, we introduce a maturity rubric to characterize readiness for LLM integration and outline a research agenda emphasizing auditability and transparent explanation mechanisms to support safer adoption in SOC workflows.

</details>


### [191] [The Acknowledgment Point Is the System: Durable Policy-Decision Receipts for AI Audit Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17176)
*Neeraj Kumar Singh Beshane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文围绕AI审计记录的持久性与信任边界约束，重建了RuntimeGuard-AI原型系统。该系统将每个确定性策略决策绑定到精确的策略来源，在调用方选择的同步边界提交隐私最小化记录，并返回带Ed25519签名的收据以表明边界是否完成。重启后引擎验证帧记录、清单、分片放置、序列连续性和重放身份；独立的证明路径将已提交记录分组为链式签名的Merkle时代供审计员验证。在Apple M4 Pro上测试，缓冲签名证据达到27,193请求/秒、141.9微秒中位延迟；全同步模式下吞吐降至约242请求/秒、中位延迟升至16.0ms。结果呈现了实测的持久性-延迟权衡，而非"免费"的异步审计路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审计记录只有在持久性和信任边界明确的情况下才有用。在持久写入前返回受保护的决策能最小化延迟，但无法保证在立即崩溃时证据能够存活。作者需要重建系统来明确这种权衡。

Method: 将每个确定性策略决策绑定到精确策略源，在调用方选择的同步边界提交隐私最小化记录，返回Ed25519签名收据表明边界完成状态。重启后通过帧记录、清单、分片放置、序列连续性和重放身份验证进行恢复；通过链式签名的Merkle时代（epoch）提供独立的证明路径。

Result: 在Apple M4 Pro上（4个工作线程、2048字节提示）：缓冲签名证据达27,193请求/秒，中位延迟141.9微秒；逐记录数据与全同步将吞吐降至约242请求/秒，中位延迟升至16.0ms；签署含100,000条记录的epoch需97.0ms。

Conclusion: 获得的是实测的持久性-延迟权衡数据，而非"免费"的异步审计路径。该原型不证明模型执行、不能防止被攻陷的签名者分叉历史，也不建立法律合规性。

Abstract: An AI audit record is useful only if its durability and trust boundary are explicit. Returning a guarded decision before any durable write minimizes latency, but it cannot guarantee that evidence survives an immediate crash. We rebuild RuntimeGuard-AI around this constraint. The resulting research prototype binds each deterministic policy decision to the exact policy source, commits a privacy-minimizing record at a caller-selected synchronization boundary, and returns an Ed25519-signed receipt that states whether that boundary completed. After restart, the engine validates framed records, manifests, shard placement, sequence continuity, and replay identity. A separate attestation path groups committed records into chained, signed Merkle epochs that an auditor verifies with an externally obtained key. On an Apple M4 Pro at four worker threads and 2,048-byte prompts, buffered signed evidence reaches 27,193 requests/s with 141.9 microseconds median latency. Per-record data and full synchronization reduce throughput to approximately 242 requests/s and raise median latency to 16.0 ms. Sealing a 100,000-record signed epoch takes 97.0 ms. The result is a measured durability-latency trade-off, not a "free" asynchronous audit path. The prototype does not prove model execution, prevent a compromised signer from forking history, or establish legal conformity.

</details>


### [192] [PACE: Policy-Attested Contract Execution for Safe AI Agents in Decentralized Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17220)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Richard Williamson,Hemanth Hm,Prakhar Mehrotra,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACE是一个在LLM智能体与链上执行之间引入的交易级授权框架，通过类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器和签名的策略决策记录（PDR）将审批与确切执行字节绑定，在确定性沙箱中实现了0.00%的不安全执行率和0.00%误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体正成为DeFi交易的接口，但它们依赖LLM规划交易，继承了LLM对提示注入的脆弱性，且缺乏将验证者审批与最终链上提交的确切交易绑定的机制。

Method: PACE引入类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器和签名PDR，以密码学方式将已批准的意图、策略和模拟报告绑定到确切执行字节，并具备重放和过期保护；Solidity智能账户在链上强制执行PDR签名。

Result: 在40个任务、四类攻击类别加良性效用、2800次试验的确定性沙箱中，PACE达到0.00不安全执行率和0.00误报率（未受保护基线为0.80）。链上开销为29,826-31,822 gas。消融实验发现宽松策略设置（+57.5个百分点）和触及合约允许列表（+12.5个百分点）是主导安全组件。

Conclusion: PACE在可复现基准内实现逻辑级安全，而非部署就绪的DeFi安全；其确定性地平线需通过额外的三模型实时LLM评估和主网分叉测试来进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are emerging as interfaces for decentralized finance (DeFi) actions such as swaps, lending operations, and yield management. Because these agents rely on large language models (LLMs) to plan transactions, they inherit the LLM's susceptibility to prompt injection and lack of mechanisms to bind a verifier's approval to the exact transaction ultimately submitted on-chain. We present PACE (Policy-Attested Contract Execution), a transaction-level authorization framework that interposes between an LLM-based agent and on-chain execution. PACE introduces typed transaction intents, a deterministic policy verifier, and signed Policy Decision Records (PDRs) that cryptographically bind the approved intent, policy, and simulation report to the exact execution bytes, with replay and expiration protection. A Solidity smart account enforces PDR signatures on-chain with a measured overhead of 29,826-31,822 gas. We evaluate PACE against six baselines on 40 tasks spanning four attack categories plus benign utility (2,800 trials, 10 seeds). In our deterministic sandbox, PACE achieves a 0.00 unsafe execution rate and 0.00 false-positive rate on benign tasks, compared to 0.80 for the unguarded baseline. Ablation studies identify permissive policy settings (+57.5 pp) and the touched-contract allowlist (+12.5 pp) as the dominant safety components. To test whether the same deterministic floor holds for real model outputs, the artifact additionally provides a three-model live-LLM evaluation over the full task suite with repeated runs. A mainnet-fork harness is included for archive-RPC deployments, but fork results are reported only when the corresponding artifacts are generated. These auxiliary studies are separate from, and never substitute for, the deterministic benchmark. We frame our claims as logic-level safety within a reproducible benchmark rather than deployment-ready DeFi security.

</details>


### [193] [COMIC: Reference-Aware Safety Gating for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17234)
*Md Abdullahil Oaphy,Anhao Xiang,Zongxing Xie,Huayue Gu,Chenyu Wang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出COMIC（Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier），一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的引用感知预生成安全门控机制。核心发现是多模态越狱攻击中，提示和图像单独均无害，但模型将良性操作（如摘要、翻译）绑定到局部视觉目标时才产生不安全行为。现有防御对整对prompt-image进行审核，忽视了操作-目标对才是真正的安全单元。COMIC通过推断操作和引用类型、构建候选目标、进行接地解析，并基于显式操作-目标对进行风险评估，采用最大风险聚合和质量感知路由来保守处理歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理截图、扫描文档、图表等视觉输入时面临新的安全风险：许多多模态越狱攻击中，提示和图像在孤立状态下均无害，不安全行为仅在模型将看似良性的操作（如摘要、翻译、遵循指令）绑定到局部视觉目标时才出现。现有防御将prompt-image对作为整体进行审核，忽略了真正安全相关的单元是接地后的操作-目标对，导致在有害语义局部化且依赖视觉引用解析时防御能力显著下降。

Method: COMIC采用引用感知的预生成安全门控流程，具体包括：1）推断请求的操作类型和引用类型；2）从OCR和开放词汇提议中构建候选目标；3）将引用接地到合理的视觉目标；4）对显式的操作-目标对进行安全性评估。为保守处理歧义，COMIC结合最大风险聚合与质量感知路由，在决定转发或阻断请求前进行综合判断。

Result: COMIC在多个开源多模态大语言模型、局部及更广泛的多模态越狱基准、以及良性的引用敏感场景上进行了评估。结果表明COMIC在持续提升鲁棒性的同时，保持了良性的实用性（utility）和实际执行效率。

Conclusion: 多模态安全无法仅通过整体审核prompt-image对来可靠实现，必须显式建模请求的操作、该操作所应用的视觉目标以及该目标接地的置信度（confidence of grounding）。这一发现为多模态安全的后续研究提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interact with screenshots, scanned documents, diagrams, and other visually grounded inputs. This shift introduces a new safety risk: in many multimodal jailbreaks, neither the prompt nor the image is harmful in isolation. Unsafe behavior emerges only when the model binds an apparently benign operation, such as summarizing, translating, or following, to a localized visual target. This reveals a structural weakness in current multimodal defenses, which largely moderate the prompt-image pair as a whole even though the true security-relevant unit is the grounded operation-target pair produced during dereference. In this work, we identify and analyze this reference-dependent failure mode and show that existing defenses degrade when harmful semantics are localized, activated only after grounding, and dependent on visual reference resolution. To address this problem, we propose COMIC (Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier), a reference-aware pre-generation safety gate for MLLMs. COMIC first infers the requested operation and reference type, constructs candidate targets from OCR and open-vocabulary proposals, grounds plausible referents, and evaluates safety over explicit operation-target pairs. To handle ambiguity conservatively, COMIC combines max-risk aggregation with quality-aware routing before deciding whether to forward or block a request. We evaluate COMIC across multiple open-source MLLMs, localized and broader multimodal jailbreak benchmarks, and benign reference-sensitive settings. The results show that COMIC consistently improves robustness while preserving benign utility and practical efficiency. More broadly, our findings suggest that multimodal safety cannot be enforced reliably without modeling the requested operation, the visual target to which it applies, and the confidence of that grounding.

</details>


### [194] [ADAPTD: Adaptive Detection and Proactive Threat Defense for Autonomous APT attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17251)
*Yeongwoo Kim,Quanyan Zhu,György Dán*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPTD，一个通信和计算高效的决策理论框架，用于在企业分段网络中检测和遏制高级持续性威胁（APT）的横向传播，通过紧凑kill chains、即时阻断和预测性驱逐策略的集成，在全局态势感知与通信开销之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击者越来越多地采用复杂技术在企业分段网络中横向传播。及时检测和防御依赖于跨子网络协调，但维护全局态势感知会产生大量通信开销。因此需要灵活的监控和适应性遏制机制来管理这种权衡。

Method: ADAPTD框架包含三个核心组件：(i) 紧凑kill chains用于识别多样化攻击向量；(ii) 即时阻断机制用于及时遏制；(iii) 预测性驱逐策略用于恢复系统安全。该框架基于决策理论，采用去中心化信念更新方案。

Result: 实验验证了ADAPTD在多种威胁场景下的有效性：去中心化信念更新方案优于先进的扩散HMM；相比基于Transformer的检测大幅减少误驱逐；在噪声环境下自适应阻断能遏制攻击者同时最小化不必要干扰；消融研究证实结合两种防御动作显著降低防御者总成本。

Conclusion: ADAPTD通过将即时阻断与预测性驱逐两种防御动作相结合，在通信效率和计算效率上都表现优异，能够在保持低通信开销的同时实现有效的APT检测与遏制，显著降低了防御者的总成本。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) actors increasingly employ sophisticated techniques to propagate laterally through segmented enterprise networks. Timely detection and defense depend on cross-subnetwork coordination, yet maintaining global situational awareness generates substantial communication overhead. To manage this tradeoff, flexible monitoring and adaptable containment are imperative. This paper presents ADAPTD, a communication- and computation-efficient, decision-theoretic framework integrating: (i) compact kill chains for identifying diverse attack vectors, (ii) an immediate blocking mechanism for timely containment, and (iii) a predictive eviction strategy to restore system security. Our experiments validate ADAPTD's effectiveness across diverse threat scenarios. First, our decentralized belief update scheme outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion HMM. Second, ADAPTD substantially reduces false evictions compared to transformer-based detection. Third, under noisy environments, adaptive blocking contains attackers while minimizing unnecessary disruption. Lastly, the ablation study confirms that combining two defensive actions significantly reduces the defender's total cost.

</details>


### [195] [When Agents Act on Web3: An Attack-Surface Survey of MCP, Skills, and Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17275)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Nour Diallo,Wujie Xiong,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI智能体通过MCP生态在公共区块链上行使工具权限时的安全威胁模型，指出区块链执行层的四个核心属性（不可逆性、签名权威、持续自主性、序列级组合）从本质上改变了攻击威胁模型，使可恢复的通用智能体安全问题转变为永久性不可逆损失，并提出了攻击面分类法、Web3风险映射矩阵及防御综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正从"读取"转向"执行"，MCP生态中修改外部状态的工具占比已从27%上升至65%。当智能体在公共区块链上行使其权限时，攻击后果由区块链执行层而非传统软件假设所决定，现有智能体安全研究未充分认识这一范式差异，且MCP安全文献高度碎片化。

Method: 采用调查综述方法：(1) 论证区块链执行层四个属性（不可逆性、签名权威、持续自主性、序列级组合）如何质变威胁模型；(2) 将碎片化的MCP安全文献整合为攻击面分类法；(3) 构建Web3风险映射矩阵，将每类攻击关联到放大影响、责任放大因素、代表性缓解措施及残余缺口；(4) 综合防御机制（含新兴的基于区块链的机制）并评估其有效性。

Result: 研究发现现有防御措施虽在改进但严重不足：已测防护措施只能阻止不到30%的攻击，模型级安全拒绝率不足3%。这表明当前防御体系远不能应对区块链不可逆执行带来的放大风险。

Conclusion: 区块链执行层从根本上改变了AI智能体的安全威胁模型，现有防御远不足以应对。作者基于风险矩阵中的空白单元推导出未来研究议程，并将研究定位于相邻调查之上。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act rather than merely read: across the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem, the share of deployed tools that modify external state has risen from 27% to 65% of tool use. When agents exercise this authority on public blockchains through MCP, skills, and tool calling, the consequences of an attack are governed by the blockchain execution layer rather than by conventional software assumptions. This survey argues that four properties of that layer (irreversibility, signing authority, continuous autonomy, and sequence-level composition) qualitatively change the threat model, turning the recoverable failures of generic agent security into a standing, irreversible loss. We organize the fragmented MCP-security literature into an attack-surface taxonomy, then contribute a Web3 risk-mapping matrix that ties each attack class to its amplified impact, the responsible amplifiers, a representative mitigation, and the residual gap. We synthesize defenses, including emerging blockchain-based mechanisms, and find them improving but insufficient: measured protections stop fewer than 30% of attacks, and model-level safety refuses fewer than 3%. We close by positioning the work against adjacent surveys and deriving a research agenda from the matrix's open cells.

</details>


### [196] [FlowShield: cryptocurrency anti-money laundering with transaction semantics parsing and fund flow tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17355)
*Qishuang Fu,Andreas Deppeler,Joseph K. Liu,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowShield是一个面向加密货币反洗钱（Crypto AML）的框架，通过恢复行为级语义、重构资金流子图、融合LLM文本语义与GCN图结构，实现交易级洗钱检测并生成可读的嫌疑活动报告（SARs）。该框架在自建的公开多链洗钱数据集BybitML及两个公开数据集上取得平均F1分数98.0%的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Crypto AML方法面临三大挑战：(1) 复杂洗钱行为快速将被盗资产碎片化，跨越多种语义和多个区块链；(2) 现有方法常常简化交易语义、依赖拓扑中心信号或仅输出孤立的检测标签；(3) 多链场景下数据稀缺，缺乏公开的多链洗钱数据集。这些局限使得洗钱意图不明确、检测能力不足。

Method: FlowShield包含四个核心模块：(1) 从可观测关系中恢复行为级语义，使洗钱意图显式化；(2) 从三个互补视角重构资金流子图，追踪价值来源与再分配；(3) 采用文本-结构融合机制，结合LLM编码的语义信息和流文本与GCN编码的结构信息；(4) 生成可读的嫌疑活动报告（SARs），提供简明摘要和可解释的红旗标记。此外，构建并开源了首个公开多链洗钱数据集BybitML。

Result: 在BybitML和两个公开洗钱数据集上，FlowShield实现了最佳整体性能，平均F1分数达98.0%。行为分析和SAR分析表明，FlowShield能够揭示多样化的洗钱策略，并为调查复杂多跳资金流生成可读报告。

Conclusion: FlowShield通过语义恢复、多视角资金流重构、文本-结构融合以及SAR生成，实现了精准的交易级洗钱检测和可解释的调查辅助，同时通过开源BybitML数据集缓解了多链场景下数据稀缺问题，为Crypto AML领域提供了新的研究基准。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency anti-money laundering (Crypto AML) is increasingly challenged by sophisticated laundering behaviors that rapidly fragment stolen assets through diverse semantics and across multiple blockchains. Existing Crypto AML methods often simplify transaction semantics, rely on topology-centric signals, or output isolated detection labels. In this paper, we present \textsc{FlowShield}, a Crypto AML framework for transaction-level laundering detection and investigator-facing report generation. \textsc{FlowShield} first recovers behavior-level semantics from observable relations, making laundering intents explicit. To trace value provenance and redistribution, \textsc{FlowShield} reconstructs fund-flow subgraphs from three complementary perspectives. It then employs a text--structure fusion mechanism, enabling the interplay between large language model (LLM)-encoded semantics and flow texts with graph convolutional network (GCN)-encoded structure. Beyond mere detection, \textsc{FlowShield} further generates readable suspicious activity reports (SARs), offering investigators concise summaries and explainable red flags. To address the data scarcity in multi-chain detection, we construct and open-source \textit{BybitML}, the first public multi-chain laundering dataset. We evaluate \textsc{FlowShield} on \textit{BybitML} and two public laundering datasets and experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} achieves the best overall performance, with an average F1 score of 98.0\%. Further behavior and SAR analyses demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} can reveal diverse laundering strategies and produce readable reports for investigating complex multi-hop fund flows.

</details>


### [197] [Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17360)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Jiajia Li,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Fair-ASR评估协议，以共享目标调用预算B作为黑盒越狱攻击的可比评估基准，并据此重新评估11种攻击方法；同时提出ReCode组合式攻击方法，在20次目标调用预算下对GPT-5实现85%攻击成功率且平均仅需7.19次攻击者调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击评估仅依赖攻击成功率(ASR)，忽略了其对攻击预算的依赖，导致不同方法之间的比较不公平；现有的计算感知评估将异构资源折算为FLOPs，对黑盒模型难以估计且无法反映资源特定约束。

Method: 引入Fair-ASR协议：以目标调用次数作为可直接观测且与方法无关的比较轴，在共享目标调用预算B下评估攻击；同时单独追踪攻击者调用次数用于效率分析。基于Fair-ASR发现的效率差距，提出ReCode，将去敏化重写与两种低成本原语组合，实现预算高效的组合式攻击。

Result: 在Fair-ASR协议下重新评估11种攻击发现：攻击排名在不同目标调用预算下发生显著变化；简单随机扰动和手工模板在同等目标访问权限下仍具强竞争力；没有评估的LLM驱动方法能同时在目标调用和攻击者调用两个维度上保持高效。ReCode在20次目标调用预算下对GPT-5达到85% ASR，平均每请求仅需7.19次攻击者调用。

Conclusion: Fair-ASR为黑盒越狱评估提供了公平可比的基础，揭示现有方法排名对预算依赖的严重性；ReCode证明了组合低成本原语可以在不过度消耗资源的前提下实现高效攻击，为预算约束下越狱攻击的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.

</details>


### [198] [Trusted Workflow Relays:Cross-Tenant Email Abuse and Composable Red Team Initial-Access Primitives in Multi-Tenant Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17361)
*Priyank Nigam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种新型跨租户通知滥用模式："受信任工作流中继（trusted workflow relay）"。在三个已负责任披露并修复的跨租户通知工作流中，认证攻击者能够通过供应商托管的邮件身份向租户边界外的收件人发送消息并部分控制内容。该模式类似于经典的未认证SMTP开放中继，但失败已上移到应用层：攻击者已认证、供应商是合法发件人，但应用层授权谓词未能约束"谁可以触发向谁发送什么内容"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云应用通过供应商运营的邮件身份发送通知可改善投递率，但分离了提供通知参数的参与方与发起消息的服务主体。这种分离引入了安全漏洞：认证参与者可能绕过租户边界向任意收件人发送供应商可信服务投递的消息。研究者希望在供应商/多租户环境中系统化描述并防御这类工作流级通知滥用。

Method: 研究者通过负责任披露的方式分析并修复了三个真实跨租户通知工作流漏洞：(1) 后端请求绕过UI长度限制，原始HTML/CSS存活到最终消息中；(2) 缺少收件人租户验证且subject和HTML字段受攻击者控制；(3) 审批应用存在弱访问控制、顺序对象标识符、缺失操作授权和不完整token验证。基于这些案例提出"受信任工作流中继"定义、测试矩阵，并映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术及RFC 8628设备码钓鱼。

Result: 三个漏洞均被负责任披露并修复。研究发现SPF、DKIM和DMARC能认证消息本身，但无法证明应用层发送已获授权。论文提供了针对通知管道的测试矩阵，将primitive映射到无附件钓鱼的MITRE ATT&CK技术，并与设备码钓鱼（RFC 8628）建立关联。

Conclusion: 应对策略包括：租户绑定（tenant binding）、类型化模板（typed templates）、对象级授权（object-level authorization）、令牌受众验证（token audience validation）以及身份遥测（identity telemetry）。这些控制措施旨在防止认证参与者滥用供应商可信通知管道进行跨租户消息投递。

Abstract: Cloud applications routinely send notifications through provider-operated mail identities, which improves deliverability but separates the actor who supplies notification parameters from the service principal that originates the message. In three responsibly disclosed and remediated cross-tenant notification workflows, an authenticated actor could reach recipients across tenant boundaries and, to varying degrees, control content that a trusted provider service delivered. In the first, backend requests bypassed a UI length limit, raw HTML and CSS survived into the delivered message, attacker links rendered, and CSS could hide service-controlled text; iframes and non-web URI schemes were rejected. The second combined missing recipient-tenant validation with attacker-controlled subject and HTML fields. The third, an approval application, added weak access control, sequential object identifiers, missing action authorization, and incomplete token validation, composing notification abuse with authorization failures.
  The pattern is analogous to a classical unauthenticated SMTP open relay, but the failure has moved up the stack: the actor is authenticated and the provider is the legitimate sender, yet application-layer authorization still fails to constrain who may cause it to send what to whom. We define a trusted workflow relay as a delivered, service-authentic message for which the application-level send-authorization predicate is false. We give a test matrix for notification pipelines, map the primitive to MITRE ATT&CK techniques for attachment-free phishing, and link it to device-code phishing (RFC 8628). SPF, DKIM, and DMARC can authenticate a message yet cannot establish that an application-level send was authorized. We conclude with controls for tenant binding, typed templates, object-level authorization, token audience validation, and identity telemetry.

</details>


### [199] [FESC: Remodeling Long-Context Private Inference with Encrypted State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17442)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FESC是一个混合FHE-MPC系统，用于私有长上下文选择性状态空间模型（SSM）推理。通过因子化扫描-收缩方法，避免了现有加密SSM方案中线性乘法深度、全序列状态驻留或密集FHE-MPC转换的问题，首次在单GPU上完成了L≥1,024的原生长文档端到端私有推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有推理系统针对Transformer注意力进行了优化，但注意力的二次方token-pair计算随序列长度增长成为瓶颈。选择性SSM提供线性时间递归，但其直接加密实现面临线性乘法深度、序列级状态驻留和密集FHE-MPC转换三大问题，亟需一种高效的私有SSM推理方案。

Method: 提出FESC（Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract），一种混合FHE-MPC系统。其核心是因子化扫描-收缩方法：在转换边界保持输入相关转换紧凑，无需密集展开即可组合转换，按需流式传输状态块，并在转换前收缩输出。该系统实现了GPU优化的CKKS内核用于线性计算，以及针对SiLU、softplus、指数和RMSNorm的MPC协议，并结合近似感知微调。

Result: FESC是首个在单GPU上完成L≥1,024原生端到端私有长文档推理的系统。在L=2,048时，12层Mamba-base模型在单张A100 GPU上77.3分钟完成推理，峰值内存占用32.7GB，且在长文档任务上保持接近明文精度。

Conclusion: FESC证明了通过因子化扫描-收缩策略可以高效地将选择性SSM架构映射到混合FHE-MPC框架，在保持精度的同时实现实际可行的私有长文档推理，为大规模机密文档处理提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Processing long, sensitive documents with machine-learning models requires efficient, privacy-preserving long-context inference. Prior private inference systems optimize or distribute encrypted Transformer attention, but its quadratic token-pair work remains the bottleneck as sequence length grows. Selective state-space models (SSMs) offer linear-time recurrence, yet direct encrypted implementation incurs linear multiplicative depth, sequence-wide state residency, or dense FHE-MPC conversion. We present Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract (FESC), a hybrid FHE-MPC system for private long-context selective SSM inference. Its factorized scan-contract keeps input-dependent transitions compact across conversion boundaries, composes them without dense expansion, streams state chunks on demand, and contracts outputs before conversion. We demonstrate interface compatibility of the scan-contract implementation across invariant and selective SSM architectures. For our Mamba-2 instantiation, we design GPU-optimized CKKS kernels for linear computations, MPC protocols for SiLU, softplus, exponential, and RMSNorm, with approximation-aware fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FESC is the first private long-document inference system to complete native end-to-end execution at $L \geq 1{,}024$ on a single GPU. At $L = 2{,}048$, a 12-layer Mamba-base model completes inference in 77.3 minutes on one A100 GPU with a peak memory footprint of 32.7 GB, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on the evaluated long-document tasks.

</details>


### [200] [Decomposition Attacks Across Unlinkable Identities: Limits of Stateful Defenses for LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17445)
*Bowen Sun,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在无法可靠关联攻击者身份的情况下，分解攻击能否被阻止。理论证明，防御效果完全取决于良性请求的分组特征：持久可识别的分组允许有效防御，而新鲜不可区分的分组则无法防御。实验表明，在严格的可用性预算下，所有十种测试策略（包括拥有精确请求-操作映射的特权策略）均无法在阻止攻击和满足预算间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型服务多采用无状态防御，仅对当前请求进行判断，容易被分解攻击（将有害任务拆分为多个单独合法请求并组合答案）绕过。针对此问题，需要状态化监控器来关联请求，但攻击者可以使用不可关联的身份并在其他平台组合答案，使得缺乏可靠的分组信号。作者希望探究在这种严苛设定下分解攻击是否仍能被阻止。

Method: 理论分析：针对固定攻击策略（无重试）证明安全性与可用性的权衡完全由良性请求的分组结构决定，分别分析持久可识别分组与新鲜不可区分分组两种情形；实验验证：在91个可执行任务和11,393个能力匹配的良性请求上测试十种防御策略，包括一种具有精确请求到操作映射的特权策略，在1%良性请求拒绝上限和0.5%背景流量拒绝上限下评估攻击阻断率和预算达标情况；同时测试模型在未见过的任务族上的泛化表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，在1%良性请求拒绝上限和0.5%背景流量拒绝上限下，所有十种测试策略均无法在阻止分解攻击和满足可用性预算之间取得平衡；对于防御未见过的任务族，攻击在首次尝试后成功率至少为99%，第二次尝试后达到100%。

Conclusion: 在攻击者可使用不可关联身份且可在外部组合答案的条件下，仅依赖请求分组的状态化防御无法有效阻止分解攻击。有效的防御必须引入与分组相关的额外证据或机制，例如可靠的身份关联、新鲜身份的成本（如身份验证费用）、或对答案使用的控制（如限制输出集成）。

Abstract: Most large language model services use stateless defenses, which judge only the current request, to refuse harmful tasks. Decomposition attacks exploit this limitation by splitting a harmful task into individually permissible requests and combining their answers. Defending against them therefore requires a stateful monitor that considers requests together. If it can group all requests for one attacker task, it can stop the attack. However, attackers can use unlinkable identities and combine answers elsewhere, leaving no reliable grouping signal. We ask whether decomposition attacks can still be stopped under this setting. For a fixed attack strategy without retries, we prove that the achievable security and utility tradeoff depends entirely on how benign requests for the same capabilities are grouped. Persistent, recognizable groups permit a useful defense; fresh, indistinguishable groups do not. When attackers can retry and learn from Allow/Block decisions, this useful operating point disappears: the feedback reveals what passes but not whether a block was correct. Experiments on 91 executable tasks and 11,393 capability-matched benign requests support these results. Under a 1% denial cap for these requests and a 0.5% cap for unrelated background traffic, all ten tested policies, including one privileged policy with an exact request-to-operation map, either fail to stop attacks or exceed the budget. On defense-unseen task families, attack success is at least 99% after one attempt and 100% after two. Effective defenses therefore require additional evidence or mechanisms tied to grouping, such as reliable identity linkage, costs for fresh identities, or control over answer use.

</details>


### [201] [KeyPooling: Measuring Where LLM API Relay Paths Collapse Prompt Cache Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17485)
*Bowen Sun,Yixi Cai,Xiaogeng Liu,Zhengyue Zhao,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KeyPooling测量方法，揭示通过LLM API中继（relay）转发请求时，使用共享提供者凭据会导致不同客户观察到彼此的提示缓存状态，造成跨客户数据泄露。测试发现OpenAI和Anthropic的五种开源网关默认均未将客户绑定到上游凭据，全部暴露跨客户缓存读取漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究虽然表明部分端点存在缓存共享问题，但未明确究竟是哪个凭据、池、适配器或嵌套跳转决定了最终缓存身份。作者旨在精确定位控制缓存身份归属的转换环节，并评估跨账户缓存泄露的实际影响范围。

Method: KeyPooling通过缓存查找和写入追踪客户身份流，验证运行时转换，并每次只测试一个预测的身份组件。在五个开源网关与OpenAI和Anthropic的连接场景中，系统性地测试主体验证与命名空间拆分、池关联、适配器及嵌套中继的对比，以定位控制转换的关键环节。

Result: 默认情况下五个网关均未将客户绑定到上游凭据；共享凭据下所有五个网关在两个提供者上都暴露了跨客户缓存读取。在OpenRouter环境中，测试覆盖了80.5%的合格token量，发现28个标签中12个存在跨账户读取，占33.7%的流量。生产路由上可连续恢复八个目标位置。缓存粒度、路由、速率限制、归因和预算并非安全控制，而是逐token恢复的条件。在可复用公共前缀后放置拆分可在1.7-2.5%成本增幅内保留大部分模型复用。

Conclusion: 作者推导出防御契约：每个客户必须进入提供者强制执行的域，或源自认证身份的命名空间必须存活于每次最终缓存查找和写入。该防御措施能以极小的成本代价在保持缓存复用效益的同时消除跨客户泄露。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) API relays authenticate customers separately but often forward requests through shared provider credentials. Providers scope prompt caches to upstream principals and namespaces, so relay customers mapped to one cache identity can observe each other's cache state. Prior work showed cache sharing at selected endpoints but did not identify which credential, pool, adapter, or nested hop controls the finalidentity. We present KeyPooling, a measurement method that traces customer identity through cache lookup and write, verifies runtime transformations, and tests one predicted identity component at a time. Across five open-source gateways connected to OpenAI and Anthropic, none bound customers to upstream credentials by default; under a shared credential, all five exposed cross-customer cache reads for both providers. Principal and namespace splits, pool associations, and adapter and nested-relay contrasts localized the controlling transformations. In an outcome-independent weekly OpenRouter frame, tests covered 80.5% of eligible token volume and found cross-account reads for 12 of 28 labels carrying 33.7% of volume. On one production route, a controlled procedure recovered eight consecutive target positions without target access. Broader tests identify cache granularity, routing, rate limits, attribution, and budget as conditions for token-by-token recovery, not security controls. We derive a defense contract: every customer must enter a provider-enforced domain, or a namespace derived from authenticated identity must survive every final cache lookup and write. Placing this split after reusable public prefixes preserved most modeled reuse at a 1.7-2.5% cost increase.

</details>


### [202] [Cross-Domain Joint DDoS Detection in Multi-Controller SDN via Confidence-Based Entropy Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17507)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shen Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对多控制器SDN中DDoS检测的"聚合偏差"问题，提出了一种跨域置信度融合框架，通过轻量级边缘侧消息校准聚合控制器的决策，在不共享原始流量数据的前提下，将聚合控制器误报率从8.87%降至1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升至96.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多控制器SDN中DDoS攻击呈现"分散源、集中目标"的跨域模式。基于熵的DDoS检测器在单控制器场景有效，但直接应用于多控制器时存在被忽视的异常：攻击转换阶段聚合控制器持续产生大量误报，而边缘控制器已恢复正常。作者将这一现象归因于OpenFlow统计延迟与无约束动态阈值漂移的耦合效应。

Method: 提出一个跨域置信度融合框架，利用轻量级边缘侧消息来校准聚合控制器的决策，无需共享原始流量数据。该框架具有非侵入性、通信高效和可增量部署的特点。

Result: 在三控制器线性Mininet测试平台（24台主机，10次运行）上进行实验，该方法在保持边缘控制器性能的同时，将聚合控制器误报率从8.87%降至1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升至96.89%。

Conclusion: 该跨域置信度融合框架有效解决了多控制器SDN中DDoS检测的聚合偏差问题，验证了轻量级跨域协作信息在改进分布式网络异常检测中的价值。

Abstract: In multi-controller Software-Defined Networking (SDN), Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks exhibit a "dispersed source, concentrated target" pattern across domains, i.e., attack traffic originates from multiple edge-controller domains but converges on a victim in a single aggregation controller domain. While entropy-based DDoS detectors are effective in single-controller settings, their direct application in multi-controller SDN reveals a previously overlooked anomaly. Through systematic experiments, we identify an aggregation bias: during the post-attack transition phase, the aggregation controller continues to generate excessive false positives, while edge controllers have already returned to normal. We attribute this phenomenon to the coupled effects of OpenFlow statistics lag and unconstrained dynamic-threshold drift. To address this issue, we propose a cross-domain confidence-fusion framework that leverages lightweight edge-side messages to calibrate aggregation-controller decisions without sharing raw traffic data. The framework is non-intrusive, communication-efficient, and incrementally deployable. Experiments on a three-controller linear Mininet testbed with 24 hosts over 10 runs show that the method preserves edge-controller performance while reducing the aggregation false positive rate from 8.87% to 1.96% and increasing the F1 score from 89.04% to 96.89%.

</details>


### [203] [CryptDough: A Unified Analytics Engine for Secure Multiparty Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17529)
*Muhammad Faisal,Alessandra Lanz,Sam Buxbaum,Adam Godel,Vasiliki Kalavri,Mayank Varia,John Liagouris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CryptDough是一个统一的安全多方计算（MPC）分析引擎，支持跨域分析（关系型、时间序列、ML推理）和多种威胁模型，通过分层系统设计和虚拟向量概念，在保持竞争力的同时常优于现有系统2倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MPC解决方案通常只支持单一威胁模型或单一工作负载类型，缺乏能够统一支持跨域分析（关系型、时间序列、ML推理）及多种威胁模型的系统。

Method: (i) 分层系统设计，通过渐进式抽象降低实现模块化和可扩展性；(ii) 虚拟向量概念，允许用户在所有软件栈层编写单线程代码，将通信、并行化和内存管理的复杂性下推到执行引擎。

Result: CryptDough泛化了最先进MPC系统的功能，在所支持的各类分析任务上保持竞争力，且通常比现有系统性能提升超过2倍。

Conclusion: CryptDough证明了一个统一的MPC引擎可以在相同运行时内支持多种威胁模型和多种分析工作负载，同时保持甚至超越专用系统的性能。

Abstract: We present CryptDough, a unified analytics engine for secure multiparty computation (MPC). CryptDough enables multiple distrusting parties to jointly execute a data analysis pipeline on their private inputs and learn nothing beyond the result (e.g., aggregate statistics). Unlike existing MPC solutions that support a single threat model or workload type, CryptDough provides built-in support for cross-domain analytics (relational, time series, ML inference) under various threat models, all within the same system runtime.
  CryptDough contributes (i) a hierarchical system design that facilitates modularity and extensibility through progressive lowering of abstractions, and (ii) the concept of virtual vectors that enable users to write single-threaded code across all layers of the software stack, while pushing the complexity of communication, parallelization, and memory management down to the execution engine. We show that CryptDough generalizes the functionality of state-of-the-art MPC systems and remains competitive on the analytics they support, often outperforming them by more than $2\times$.

</details>


### [204] [SoK: Cross-Chain Transaction Identification and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17532)
*Hang Zheng,Qishuang Fu,Joseph Liu,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项关于跨链交易识别与匹配的系统化知识综述（SoK），将存取款识别方法分为四类、交易匹配方法分为三类机制，评估了现有数据集与工件的可得性，并提炼出四大开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥、即时加密货币交易所和中心化跨账本平台在多链生态中频繁转移资产，但反复成为高价值攻击目标和跨链洗钱渠道。跨链交易比单链交易难以分析：没有任何单一账本记录完整跨链转账，证据分散在源链、目标链和链下系统，且各类系统间证据的可用性和可靠性差异巨大。

Method: 本文采用系统化知识（SoK）方法，首先对存款/取款识别方法进行四类分类，并将交易匹配方法归为三种机制：确定性标识符匹配、字段约束启发式和模型辅助匹配；随后评估现有数据集与工件的可得性并归纳失败模式。

Result: 研究发现跨链匹配方法的适用性和报告性能主要受底层系统所暴露的证据决定；现有数据集中不到一半仍然可获取，并提炼出三种工件失效模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出四项开放挑战，旨在推动可审计、可复现且可操作的跨链分析。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges, instant cryptocurrency exchanges, and centralized cross-ledger platforms move assets across an increasingly multi-chain ecosystem. However, these systems have repeatedly become targets of high-value attacks and channels for cross-chain money laundering. Cross-chain transactions are substantially harder to analyze than single-chain transactions: no single ledger records an entire cross-chain transfer, its evidence is scattered across the source chain, the destination chain, and off-chain systems, and the availability and reliability of that evidence vary widely across systems. In this paper, we present a systematization of knowledge (SoK) on cross-chain transaction identification and matching. First, we classify deposit and withdrawal identification methods into four approaches and transaction matching methods into three mechanisms: deterministic identifier matching, field-constraint heuristics, and model-assisted matching. We find that their applicability and reported performance are shaped mainly by the evidence the underlying system exposes, and we further examine how matched pairs support downstream attack detection and fund tracing. Second, we assess the availability of existing datasets and artifacts, finding that fewer than half remain obtainable, and distill three artifact failure modes. Finally, we outline four open challenges toward auditable, reproducible, and actionable cross-chain analysis.

</details>


### [205] [TENET: Telegram Mini App (in)security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17538)
*Andrea Ciccotelli,Federico Zappone,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究Telegram Mini Apps生态中的客户端存储安全漏洞。作者开发了专用审计工具TENET，对61个Mini Apps进行分层抽样筛查，发现37个可分析应用中30个存在安全缺陷，包括明文存储、可恢复加密和可重放令牌三种严重等级。连Telegram官方Wallet也存在可能导致账户完全被攻陷的严重漏洞。经过负责任披露后，Telegram已实现两个新的安全存储API并修复了Wallet漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为在客户端内运行的Web应用，缺乏平台级安全审查、无存储访问限制，且用户群体涉及加密货币实时资产，构成"WebView环境+金融动机+无安全审查"的独特危险组合，其客户端存储安全问题亟待系统性研究。

Method: 提出TENET审计工具，设计决策（模式选择、熵阈值、字符集验证）基于目标秘密的结构属性并在ground-truth数据集上实证验证。采用基于流行度的分层加权抽样策略筛查61个Mini Apps，对满足处理条件的37个进行深入分析。

Result: 37个可分析应用中30个存在安全缺陷，分为三个严重等级：明文存储、可恢复加密、可重放令牌。Telegram官方Wallet暴露恢复助记词明文，可能导致账户完全被攻陷。负责任披露后，Telegram新增两个安全存储API，验证确认官方Wallet已修复。最终提出平台开发者与第三方Mini App开发者的缓解措施与最佳实践。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态系统存在普遍而严重的客户端存储安全漏洞，官方应用亦未能幸免。这些缺陷源于平台无安全审查、无存储限制、WebView保护薄弱与金融敏感性用户群的叠加。通过负责任披露和平台级API改进，问题可以得到缓解，但仍需系统性安全治理。

Abstract: Telegram, with over 450 million daily active users, has introduced Mini Apps---web-based applications running directly within its client. However, this integration introduces notable security risks. As we demonstrate, many Mini Apps store authentication materials---such as session tokens and wallet mnemonic phrases---in plaintext on client devices, exposing users to unauthorized access, impersonation, and financial exploitation. While insecure client-side storage is a known risk in web applications, the Telegram Mini App ecosystem presents a uniquely dangerous combination of factors absent from prior work: no platform-level security review, no storage access restrictions, a financially motivated user base handling live cryptocurrency assets, and a WebView environment that offers weaker protections than standalone browsers. To investigate this threat, we present TENET, a purpose-built auditing tool whose design decisions---pattern selection, entropy thresholds, and charset validation---are grounded in the structural properties of the secrets targeted and empirically validated against a ground-truth dataset. We screened 61 Mini Apps using a stratified, popularity-weighted sampling strategy based on popularity. Of the 37 applications that met our processing criteria and were analyzed, 30 exhibited security flaws, which we classify into three severity tiers: plaintext storage, recoverable encryption, and replayable tokens. Notably, even Telegram's official Wallet exhibits a severe vulnerability that may lead to full account compromise. Following our responsible disclosure, Telegram implemented two new secure-storage APIs, and our post-remediation verification confirmed that its official Wallet no longer exposes the recovery mnemonic in plaintext. Finally, we propose mitigation measures and best practices for both Telegram platform developers and third-party Mini App creators.

</details>


### [206] [Software Defined Networks Key Relay for Large-Scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17539)
*Stephan Laschet,Gergely Lendvay,Thomas Lorünser,Paul James,Luca Torresetti,Alessandro Colombo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用软件定义网络(SDN)对大规模量子密钥分发网络(QKDN)进行编排，提出基于Dijkstra和最大最小容量算法的多路径选择方案，并引入隐式多方协议实现跨域中继路径选择，以提升量子安全网络的可扩展性、鲁棒性和互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模QKDN需要高效的编排和管理机制，而传统方法难以满足动态路径选择和负载均衡的需求。SDN能够集中控制网络并提供全局视图，因此研究如何利用SDN来编排QKDN，同时解决多域场景下的隐私保护问题，是该工作的出发点。

Method: 基于ETSI和ITU规范构建SDN控制的QKDN架构，SDN控制器聚合全网KPI并据此选择最优路径。提出了基于Dijkstra和最大最小容量算法的多路径选择算法，内置负载均衡机制。此外，设计了隐式多方协议用于多域场景下的中继路径选择，避免各提供商泄露敏感网络信息。还讨论了查询批处理、多路径选择和组密钥等关键能力。

Result: 通过仿真测试验证了所提算法的性能并分析了各算法的权衡取舍。算法在路径选择和负载均衡方面表现良好，隐式多方协议能够在保护各域隐私的前提下完成跨域中继路径选择。

Conclusion: 这些贡献旨在增强量子安全网络基础设施的可扩展性、鲁棒性和互操作性，为大规模QKDN的SDN编排提供了系统性框架和实用算法。

Abstract: This work addresses the orchestration of large-scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks (QKDNs) using Software Defined Networking (SDN). Building on ETSI and ITU specifications, common best practices and architectures are outlined. The main task of the SDN Controller is to aggregate technical key performance indicators (KPI) from the network and, based on these, select the optimal path. Multiple path selection algorithms, based on Dijkstra or a maximum-minimum capacity algorithm, with built-in load balancing are presented. The algorithms were tested in simulations and their performances, and tradeoffs, are discussed. Additional critical aspects related to SDN controlled QKDNs are discussed, such as query batching, multi-path selection and group key capabilities. An oblivious multi-party protocol is proposed for relay path selection in a multi-domain scenario, so providers don't have to disclose sensitive information about their QKDN. These contributions aim to enhance scalability, resilience and interoperability in quantum-secure network infrastructures.

</details>


### [207] [Reflex-Guard: A Low-Latency Guardrail for LLM Prompt Safety Using Dense Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17556)
*Istiaque Ahmed,Afia Anjum Borsha,Ranat Das Prangon,Abu-fuad Ahmad,Thi Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Reflex-Guard是一个轻量级的本地防护模型，通过越狱感知预处理、紧凑句子变换器嵌入和七个快速二分类器，在仅37.6毫秒端到端延迟下达到95.9%的有害提示召回率，性能显著优于Llama Guard 2和SafeDecoding等现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护方法（如LLM-as-a-judge和云安全API）虽然能检测不安全内容，但每次请求会引入250-900毫秒的延迟，远超实时应用所需的小于100毫秒响应要求；此外，将用户提示路由到外部审核端点还会带来严重的数据隐私问题。

Method: Reflex-Guard采用本地运行架构，包含三个核心组件：（1）越狱感知的预处理模块，（2）紧凑的句子变换器嵌入，以及（3）七个快速的二分类器。在基于五个互补来源构建的30,568个样本的战略平衡数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: Reflex-Guard在37.6毫秒端到端延迟下实现95.9%的有害提示召回率；相比Llama Guard 2（255毫秒）和SafeDecoding（723毫秒）大幅提速；默认阈值下能检测100%的GCG后缀攻击和Base64编码提示；DrAttack结构化提示需将阈值降至0.03以获得最佳检测；达到16.79的Reflex效率分数（RES），显著优于Llama Guard 2（11.90）和SafeDecoding（9.80）。

Conclusion: Reflex-Guard在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低延迟并增强隐私保护，适合实时应用场景。研究还发现不同类型攻击在嵌入概率空间中占据不同区域，为实际部署提供了针对性建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world applications often face the risks of specially crafted prompts designed to bypass the safety controls. Existing guardrail methods, such as LLM-as-a-judge and cloud-based safety APIs are able to detect unsafe content. However, they often add a delay of about 250-900 ms to each request. This delay is too high for real-time applications, when the system usually needs to respond in less than 100 ms. Furthermore, routing user prompts through external moderation endpoints raises significant data privacy concerns. This paper introduces Reflex-Guard, a lightweight guardrail that runs locally. It uses jailbreak-aware preprocessing, compact sentence-transformer embeddings, and seven fast binary classifiers. Together, these components enable high-accuracy prompt safety filtering with much lower latency than existing solutions. Through systematic evaluation on a strategically balanced dataset of 30,568 samples drawn from five complementary sources, we demonstrate that Reflex-Guard achieves 95.9% recall on harmful prompts at 37.6 ms end-to-end latency. It is faster than existing baselines, including Llama Guard 2 at 255 ms and SafeDecoding at 723 ms. It can detect 100% of GCG suffix attacks and Base64-encoded prompts using the default threshold. However, DrAttack structured prompts required lowering the threshold to 0.03 for optimal detection, as they produced a distinct probability distribution. Reflex-Guard achieves Reflex Efficiency Score (RES) scores up to 16.79, significantly outperforming Llama Guard 2 (11.90) and SafeDecoding (9.80). This analysis offers practical deployment advice and shows that different attack types occupy distinct regions in the embedding probability space.

</details>


### [208] [HarnessRisk: A Lifecycle-Oriented Benchmark for Agent Harness Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17597)
*Yajing Bai,Jinhao Duan,Jie Peng,Xianfeng Wu,Sijia Liu,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HarnessRisk，一个面向智能体（agent）框架安全的生命周期导向基准，将框架安全组织为六个运维阶段，包含128个沙盒化测试用例，并在三个框架、六个语言模型和14种配置下进行评估，发现框架配置阶段最为脆弱，且显式风险识别并不能可靠地导向安全行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准大多针对单一攻击机制或有限的运维设置子集，难以比较不同框架职责下安全故障如何涌现。随着大语言模型越来越多地通过agent框架部署（管理工具、扩展、持久状态、权限和外部动作），需要一个统一、生命周期导向的评估体系来全面衡量agent框架安全性。

Method: HarnessRisk将agent框架安全组织为六个运维阶段：框架配置（Harness Configuration）、能力扩展（Capability Extension）、运行时操作（Runtime Operation）、状态持久化（State Persistence）、动作控制（Action Control）和事故恢复（Incident Recovery）。基准包含128个沙盒化用例，每个用例将一个良性用户目标与嵌入不可信工作流工件中的对抗性指令配对。评估采用四个指标：Utility（效用）、Attack Success Rate（攻击成功率）、Persistence（持久性）和Detection（检测率）。实验覆盖三个框架、六个语言模型和14种模型与框架配置。

Result: 攻击成功率范围为12.6%至80.9%，而Utility保持在75.0%至97.6%之间。框架配置阶段在所有三个框架中都是最脆弱的阶段，表明攻击可以通过在原本授权的工作流中篡改安全敏感参数来成功。此外，显式风险识别并不能可靠地导致安全行动——某些配置在超过90%的运行中检测到风险，却仍保持较高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 需要在部署的模型和框架配置层面、跨多个框架职责来评估agent安全。结果显示，仅依靠风险识别能力不足以防范攻击，必须评估模型在检测到风险后的实际安全行动；同时框架配置阶段应作为安全加固的重点关注对象。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed through agent harnesses that manage tools, extensions, persistent state, permissions, and external actions. Existing safety benchmarks mainly target individual attack mechanisms or a limited subset of operational settings, making it difficult to compare how safety failures emerge across different harness responsibilities. We present HarnessRisk, a lifecycle oriented benchmark that organizes agent harness safety into six operational phases including Harness Configuration, Capability Extension, Runtime Operation, State Persistence, Action Control, and Incident Recovery. HarnessRisk contains 128 sandboxed cases, each pairing a benign user objective with an adversarial instruction embedded in an untrusted workflow artifact. We evaluate each trajectory using Utility, Attack Success Rate, Persistence, and Detection. Across three harnesses, six language models, and 14 model and harness configurations, attack success ranges from 12.6% to 80.9%, while Utility remains between 75.0% and 97.6%. Harness Configuration is the most vulnerable phase across all three harnesses, showing that attacks can succeed by altering security sensitive parameters within otherwise authorized workflows. We also find that explicit risk recognition does not reliably lead to safe action, as some configurations detect risks in more than 90% of runs while retaining substantial attack success. These results highlight the need to evaluate agent safety across multiple harness responsibilities and at the level of the deployed model and harness configuration.

</details>


### [209] [An Emulation Anchored Digital Twin Testbed for Cyberattack and Defense Analysis in Hospital IT OT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17650)
*Prashant Rawat,Ravi Kumar Bairagi,Arunima,Geeta Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个医院IT/OT网络安全测试平台，结合数字孪生技术，用于监控、实验和验证防御机制。该测试平台模拟中央服务器、电子健康记录系统、SCADA基础设施以及分段IT、OT和DMZ网络，支持受控网络攻击执行、软件补丁评估和基于强化学习的防御智能体训练。实验表明其计算开销低（平均CPU利用率低于0.4%），且能捕获多阶段SSH攻击传播过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医院日益依赖IT与OT融合的基础设施，但这种融合扩大了网络攻击面，且难以在真实系统上安全验证防御机制。现有测试平台往往只聚焦于孤立的IT或OT环境，无法捕捉现实中的跨域医疗交互场景，亟需一个能真实反映医院IT-OT融合环境的测试平台。

Method: 构建一个医院IT/OT网络安全测试平台，采用容器化方式模拟中央服务器、EHR系统、基于SCADA的基础设施及分段网络（IT、OT、DMZ）。平台支持受控攻击执行、补丁评估和RL防御智能体训练。进一步扩展为数字孪生，利用日志和网络统计建模实时环境状态，通过命令执行和容器生命周期编排实现双向交互。采用Modbus/TCP和FHIR/HL7协议以支持医疗与工业组件间的真实通信。

Result: 实验评估显示计算开销极低：每容器平均标准化CPU利用率低于0.4%，大多数轻量级服务低于0.01%。OpenPLC Modbus TCP操作的中位往返延迟为0.901毫秒。测试平台成功捕获了从DMZ传播至IT和PLC网络的多阶段SSH攻击，验证了其安全防御验证能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为将模拟环境扩展到硬件启用的医院数字孪生提供了基础，为医疗IT/OT融合环境中的防御机制安全验证和RL防御智能体训练提供了可行平台。

Abstract: Modern hospitals increasingly rely on integrated Information Technology (IT) and Operational Technology (OT) infrastructures to support critical healthcare services. However, this convergence expands the cybersecurity attack surface and makes safe validation of defensive mechanisms difficult on live systems. Existing testbeds often focus on isolated IT or OT environments and do not capture realistic cross-domain healthcare interactions. This work presents a hospital IT and OT cybersecurity testbed coupled with a digital twin for monitoring, experimentation, and validation of countermeasures. The testbed emulates a central server, Electronic Health Record (EHR) systems, SCADA-based infrastructure, and segmented IT, OT, and DMZ networks. It supports controlled cyberattack execution, software-patch evaluation, and training of RL-based defense agents. The testbed is further extended to a digital twin that models the real-time state of the environment using log and network statistics and enables bidirectional interaction through command execution and container lifecycle orchestration. Modbus/TCP and FHIR/HL7 support realistic communication across healthcare and industrial components. Experimental evaluation shows low computation overhead, with average normalized CPU utilization below 0.4 % per container and most lightweight services operating below 0.01%. OpenPLC Modbus TCP operations achieve a median round-trip latency of 0.901 ms. The testbed also captures a multi-stage SSH-based attack propagating from the DMZ to the IT and PLC networks. The framework provides a foundation for extending the emulated environment toward a hardware-enabled hospital digital twin.

</details>


### [210] [MobileWorldSafety: Benchmarking GUI Agent Safety Against Environmental Injection Attacks in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17659)
*Sujin Chen,Lijun Li,Tianyi Du,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobileWorldSafety，一个基于真实Android应用的基准测试，包含142个风险任务，用于系统评估移动端GUI代理在环境注入攻击下的安全性。通过在六个代理上的评估，发现所有代理的攻击成功率介于40.4%到66.9%之间，表明当前代理在面对伪装成普通移动上下文的对抗内容时普遍无法维持安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的GUI代理正从研究原型向实际部署过渡，但由于其处理不可信环境内容，极易受到环境注入攻击（包括间接提示注入和对抗性指令）。现有基准未能捕捉日常用户场景，缺乏对移动设备上GUI代理在环境注入攻击下的系统性评估。

Method: 构建MobileWorldSafety基准：包含142个基于真实Android应用的风险任务。每个任务定义了一个可通过编程验证的风险指标（基于最终系统状态），并采用两阶段评估管道：基于规则的验证处理无歧义情况，LLM法官裁定模糊情况，以区分安全性失败与能力失败，实现客观可复现的评估。

Result: 对六个代理（包括通用代理和专用GUI代理）的评估显示，所有代理仍高度脆弱，攻击成功率在40.4%到66.9%之间。结果表明当前代理在对抗性内容以普通移动上下文形式呈现时，常常无法维持安全对齐。

Conclusion: MobileWorldSafety为量化这些安全漏洞提供了基础，并推进了鲁棒移动GUI代理的研究方向。该基准能够客观区分安全性失败与能力失败，为评估和提升移动GUI代理在环境注入攻击下的安全性提供了可复现的评估框架。

Abstract: LLM-powered GUI agents that autonomously operate smartphones are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to early real-world deployment. However, because these agents routinely process untrusted environmental content, they are highly vulnerable to environmental injection attacks, which include indirect prompt injections and adversarial instructions. Such attacks can manipulate the behavior of agents without user awareness through diverse channels encountered in everyday mobile use. Despite these risks, existing benchmarks often fail to capture everyday user scenarios, lacking a systematic evaluation of GUI agents under environmental injection attacks on mobile devices. To address this gap, we introduce MobileWorldSafety, a benchmark of 142 risk tasks built on real Android applications. For each task, we define a programmatically verifiable risk indicator over the final system state and evaluate outcomes with a two-stage pipeline: rule-based verification handles unambiguous cases, while an LLM judge adjudicates ambiguous ones. This distinguishes safety failures from capability failures and enables objective and reproducible assessment. Evaluations on six agents, including both general agents and specialized GUI agents, demonstrate that all agents remain highly vulnerable, with attack success rates ranging from 40.4% to 66.9%. These findings indicate that current agents often fail to maintain safety alignment when adversarial content is presented as ordinary mobile context. MobileWorldSafety provides a foundation for quantifying these vulnerabilities and advancing research on robust mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [211] [MemCatalyst: Amplifying Data Auditing on Vision-Language Models via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17722)
*Xukun Luan,Jinyan Liu,Yuhui Gong,Yuanguo Bi,Bing Hu,Xuesong Li,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCatalyst，一种数据投毒工具集，通过在训练数据中注入文本和图像层面的投毒样本，迫使视觉语言模型(VLM)过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的特定不一致性，从而放大成员推断(MI)审计的灵敏度，使数据持有者能够更有效地检测其数据是否被未经授权用于模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM性能的提升，数据持有者（如艺术家）迫切需要确定其数据是否在未经授权的情况下被用于模型训练，这关系到知识产权保护和个人隐私。现有的数据审计方法（尤其是成员推断）在VLM上的效果有限，需要一种能够放大审计信号的手段。

Method: MemCatalyst采用两种投毒策略：1) 文本投毒(PT)和2) 图像投毒(PI)。通过在训练阶段强制VLM过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的人为不一致性，提高模型对成员信息的可审计性。同时验证了投毒样本在不同VLM架构间的可迁移性，支持黑盒场景下的应用。

Result: 在两种主流VLM上使用五种最先进的数据审计方法进行广泛评估，结果表明MemCatalyst能以极少的投毒样本预算显著提升成员推断的AUC分数，同时对模型性能的负面影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MemCatalyst证明了数据投毒技术可以作为数据审计的有效放大器，在保障模型性能的同时显著增强数据持有者对其数据使用情况的检测能力，为知识产权保护和隐私审计提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) achieve outstanding performance largely due to the amount of training data available on the internet. At the same time, data holders (e.g., artists) urgently need to determine whether their data has been used for model training without authorization, which concerns both intellectual property rights and personal privacy. Data auditing, particularly through membership inference (MI), has attracted attention as a direct tool. This work proposes MemCatalyst, a set of data poisoning tools, aiming to amplify the data auditing performance on VLMs. MemCatalyst employs two strategies: Poisoning Text (PT) and Poisoning Image (PI). MemCatalyst forces VLMs to over-learn specific inconsistencies between image features and textual semantics during training, thereby increasing their susceptibility to membership information auditing. Crucially, the transferability of poisoned samples across different VLM architectures is demonstrated to be effective in the black-box setting. Extensive evaluations using five state-of-the-art data audits on two prominent VLMs demonstrate that MemCatalyst markedly enhances MI AUC scores with a minimal budget of poisoned samples, while maintaining a negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [212] [BullsEye: Directed Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17729)
*Lorenzo Ralli,Emilio Coppa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BULLSEYE是首个以基本块级距离调度闭源Linux固件模糊测试的定向灰盒模糊测试（DGF）框架，在40个漏洞点（跨32个固件镜像）上全部成功复现，相比四种基线基线将暴露时间几何平均缩短9.5倍至72.5倍，相比最先进的固件重宿主框架GREENHOUSE快9.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备的广泛采用扩大了数字攻击面，固件分析对现代软件安全至关重要。固件经常复用第三方软件组件，从而引入已知漏洞，但固件镜像是否真正暴露这些漏洞是开放问题，且公开的POC漏洞利用使回答这一问题变得紧迫。现有DGF技术虽能定向探索特定二进制位置，但在固件领域仅达到函数粒度，过于粗糙而无法精确瞄准漏洞本身所在的基本块。

Method: BULLSEYE结合静态与动态分析，在受限的固件域中实现DGF，采用基本块级距离度量调度闭源Linux固件模糊测试，并提出解决传统方法局限的新型DGF启发式策略，聚焦于复用第三方组件中的漏洞。

Result: 在40个跨32个固件镜像的漏洞点上，BULLSEYE在预算内全部复现目标；相比四种最强的基线（包括AFLGO和WINDRANGER的重实现），最强基线仅复现35个目标；BULLSEYE将暴露时间几何平均缩短9.5倍至72.5倍。针对GREENHOUSE管线支持的18个目标，BULLSEYE比其快9.8倍（几何平均）。

Conclusion: BULLSEYE通过基本块级粒度的定向灰盒模糊测试，显著提升了闭源Linux固件中已知漏洞的检测效率与覆盖率，证明其在固件模糊测试领域的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has expanded the digital attack surface, making firmware analysis critical for modern software security. A key security concern stems from the frequent reuse of third-party software components, a practice that often introduces known vulnerabilities into firmware images. Whether a given image actually exposes such a flaw is an open question, and public proof-of concept exploits make answering it urgent. Directed Greybox Fuzzing (DGF), a technique that enables targeted exploration of specific binary locations, offers a promising solution for detecting such vulnerabilities. However, DGF has reached firmware only at function granularity, too coarse to aim at the vulnerable block itself.
  This article presents BULLSEYE, the first DGF framework to schedule closed-source Linux-based firmware fuzzing by basic-block-level distance to user-specified targets. Our methodology combines static and dynamic analysis to enable DGF in the constrained firmware domain, focusing on vulnerabilities in reused third-party components. We introduce novel DGF heuristics that address limitations of traditional approaches. We compare BULLSEYE against four greybox-fuzzing baselines sharing its execution back-end, including reimplementations of AFLGO and WINDRANGER, and against GREENHOUSE, a state-of-the-art firmware re-hosting framework. On 40 vulnerability sites across 32 firmware images, BULLSEYE reproduces every target within budget, against 35 for the strongest of the four baselines, and reduces Time-to-Exposure by a geometric mean of 9.5x to 72.5x over them; against GREENHOUSE, on the 18 targets its pipeline supports, BULLSEYE is faster by a geometric mean of 9.8x.

</details>


### [213] [Achievement Unlocked: Let's Get Hacked! An Empirical Study of Cybercrime in the Video Gaming Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17754)
*Janine Schneider,Jan Kallenborn,Tim Hoffmann,Maximilian Eichhorn,Thorsten Holz,Bhupendra Acharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过定性分析与观察性分析相结合的实证研究，系统性地考察了影响视频游戏玩家的网络犯罪问题。研究基于57名国际参与者的在线调查、两名确认受害者的半结构化访谈，以及对2,574条公开举报帖子的分析，揭示了数字物品盗窃是主要攻击动机，游戏特性和服务（如物品交易、队伍投票、锦标赛）促使用户参与风险互动，并指出客服流程的薄弱环节被定向利用，部分安全机制仅提供虚假的安全感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频游戏行业规模庞大、用户基数巨大，已演变为复杂的社会和经济生态系统，但游戏生态中的网络犯罪现象在以往研究中缺乏系统性的关注。网络犯罪分子利用游戏特有机制针对玩家实施攻击，这一问题亟待实证研究来填补空白。

Method: 采用混合研究方法，结合定性分析与观察性分析：1) 对57名国际参与者进行在线问卷调查；2) 对两名已确认的游戏相关网络犯罪受害者进行半结构化访谈；3) 分析多个在线游戏平台上2,574条公开举报网络犯罪事件的帖子。通过三角验证方法表征攻击类型、识别常见攻击向量和动机，并考察玩家应对行为。

Result: 主要研究发现包括：数字物品盗窃是攻击的主要动机；游戏特性和服务（如物品交易、队伍投票、锦标赛）为玩家创造了参与风险互动的激励；攻击者定向利用客服流程中的弱点；部分现有安全机制仅给玩家提供虚假的安全感，未能提供实质性保护。

Conclusion: 研究系统性地描绘了游戏生态系统中的网络犯罪图景，强调游戏特有的机制和服务在无意中增加了玩家的风险敞口，并指出平台方在客服流程和安全机制设计中存在的漏洞需要改进，以更好地保护玩家免受网络犯罪的侵害。

Abstract: The ubiquity of the video game industry and its large user base have transformed video games into complex social and economic ecosystems. Unfortunately, this growing popularity also attracts cybercriminals who deliberately exploit game-specific mechanisms to target players. Despite this growing threat, cybercrime in the gaming ecosystem has received little systematic attention in prior research.
  In this work, we present an empirical study of cybercrime affecting video game players, combining qualitative and observational analyses to characterize gaming-related attacks, identify common attack vectors and motivations, and examine player responses. Our study is based on an online survey with 57 international participants, semi-structured interviews with two confirmed victims of gaming-related cybercrime, and an analysis of 2,574 publicly available posts reporting cybercrime incidents across multiple online gaming platforms. Our findings indicate that the theft of digital items is a prevalent motivation for attacks. We further observe that gaming-related features and services, such as item trading, team voting, and tournaments, create incentives for players to engage in risky interactions. In addition, our results highlight the targeted exploitation of weaknesses in customer support processes and reveal that certain security mechanisms provide only a false sense of protection.

</details>


### [214] [Efficient Fuzzy PSI under One-Sided Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17770)
*Xinpeng Yang,Meng Hao,Yanxue Jia,Chenkai Weng,Yonggang Wen,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在单侧几何分离假设下、针对一般L_{p∈[1,∞]}距离的具体高效模糊隐私集合交集（Fuzzy PSI）协议，仅依赖轻量级对称密钥原语，并首次将前缀字典树技术融入协议，实现O(logδ)复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊PSI构造在一般Minkowski距离下要么依赖强的双侧几何分离假设，要么在单侧假设下产生大量开销。缺乏在单侧假设下具体高效且支持一般L_p距离的协议。

Method: 提出了基于轻量级对称密钥原语的模糊PSI协议，支持发送方侧和接收方侧两种设置；针对稀疏输入分布设计了更高效的定制协议；非平凡地融入前缀字典树（prefix trie）技术以降低阈值δ带来的开销，实现O(logδ)复杂度。

Result: 协议在一般L_{p∈[1,∞]}距离下首次实现O(logδ)复杂度（优于先前O((logδ)^d)或O(δ)）。实验显示：相比van Baarsen和Pu (EUROCRYPT'24)最多快239倍计算、低20倍通信；相比Dang等人(CCS'25)最多518倍加速、63倍通信降低；相比Bui等人(ASIACRYPT'25)最多4818倍计算加速、282倍通信降低。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了单侧假设下一般L_p距离模糊PSI协议的效率空白，通过前缀字典树技术大幅降低阈值复杂度，在大量参数设置下显著优于现有最优方案。

Abstract: Fuzzy private set intersection (PSI) enables two parties to identify approximately matching elements between their input sets, where two elements are considered a match if their distance is at most a threshold $δ$ under a given metric. Although substantial progress has been made, existing constructions for general Minkowski distances either rely on strong two-sided geometric separation assumptions or incur substantial overhead under one-sided assumptions.
  In this work, we present the first concretely efficient fuzzy PSI protocols for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances under one-sided assumptions, relying solely on lightweight symmetric-key primitives. Our constructions support both sender-sided and receiver-sided settings. We further study sparser input distributions and present more efficient protocols tailored to this case. To reduce the overhead scaling with $δ$, we non-trivially incorporate prefix trie techniques into our protocols, achieving $O(\logδ)$ complexity for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances for the first time, improving upon $O((\logδ)^d)$ or $O(δ)$ complexities of prior works.
  Extensive experiments, across a wide range of parameter settings, show that our protocols significantly outperform prior works under the same assumptions. Specifically, against van Baarsen and Pu (EUROCRYPT'24), our protocols achieve up to $239\times$ faster computation and up to $20\times$ lower communication. Against Dang et al. (CCS'25), we achieve up to $518\times$ speedup and up to $63\times$ communication reduction. Against Bui et al. (ASIACRYPT'25), we achieve up to $4818\times$ faster computation and up to $282\times$ lower communication.

</details>


### [215] [Diff-DDoS: Realistic Cyber-Physical Attack Synthesis and Robust Detection for 5G-Enabled CPS Using Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17796)
*Bilal Hussain,Xiao Tang,Qinghe Du,Tan Li,Muhammad Azhar,Danista Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-DDoS框架，利用表格扩散模型（TabDDPM）为5G网络物理系统中的DDoS检测器生成真实且分布保持的攻击样本，并通过对抗扩散训练（ADT）提升检测器鲁棒性，在米兰CDR数据集上显著恢复F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络物理系统中的深度学习DDoS检测器面临标注攻击数据稀缺和合成数据不真实的问题。基于手工设计攻击（固定缩放倍数）训练的检测器在面对真实分布保持的样本时性能急剧退化（F1分数下降47%-100%），亟需更真实的攻击合成方法和鲁棒性训练策略。

Method: 提出Diff-DDoS三阶段框架：(1) 基于CDR时空网格训练基线CNN小区级检测器；(2) 使用表格去噪扩散概率模型（TabDDPM）在正常CDR聚合数据上生成逼真攻击样本以揭示检测器漏洞；(3) 引入对抗扩散训练（ADT），通过逆分类器引导生成困难但分布保持的样本，迭代训练直至检测器收敛。

Result: 在米兰CDR数据集上，覆盖SMS洪泛、静默呼叫、互联网信令和混合场景。ResNet50配合ADT后恢复F1分数：静默呼叫79.62%、互联网100%、混合92.79%。经验证集阈值校准后，ADT在SMS场景达到100% F1（对比CTGAN仅47.3%），在静默呼叫场景与最强梯度对抗训练基线持平。

Conclusion: 表格扩散模型能够有效生成真实且分布保持的攻击样本，用于压力测试和加固数据稀缺的5G网络物理系统中的入侵检测器，显著优于CTGAN等传统生成方法。

Abstract: Deep learning-based DDoS detectors for 5G-enabled cyber-physical systems face scarce labeled attack data and unrealistic synthetic substitutes, which limit robustness against adaptive adversaries. Detectors trained on hand-crafted attacks with fixed scaling multipliers degrade catastrophically (F1-score drops of about 47 percent to 100 percent, depending on scenario) when confronted with realistic, distribution-preserving samples. We propose Diff-DDoS, a three-phase framework for realistic attack synthesis and robust detection using tabular diffusion models. Phase 1 trains a baseline CNN cell-level detector on spatiotemporal grids from call detail records (CDRs). Phase 2 trains a tabular denoising diffusion probabilistic model (TabDDPM) on normal CDR aggregates to generate realistic attacks and expose detector vulnerabilities. Phase 3 introduces adversarial diffusion training (ADT), using inverse classifier guidance to generate hard yet distribution-preserving samples until the detector converges. On a Milano CDR dataset across SMS-flooding, silent-call, Internet-signaling, and blended scenarios, ResNet50 with ADT recovers F1-scores of 79.62 percent (silent-call), 100 percent (Internet), and 92.79 percent (blended). After validation-based threshold calibration, ADT reaches 100 percent SMS F1 versus 47.3 percent for CTGAN, and matches the strongest gradient-based adversarial-training baseline on silent-call. These results support tabular diffusion models for stress-testing and hardening intrusion detectors in data-scarce 5G cyber-physical deployments.

</details>


### [216] [The Model's Tell: Measuring Context-Leakage Attack Signals with Behavior Gauges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17829)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出LeakGauge，通过给用户查询追加一个"测量后缀"并利用其prefill token概率映射为攻击风险分数，在不访问隐藏状态的情况下检测LLM在生成前是否可能泄露系统提示或检索文档中的机密上下文。在11个LLM（包括GLM-5.2 753B和Kimi-K3 2.8T）上达到0.944-0.996的AUROC，且对未见攻击、跨语言内容及语义性泄露均保持稳健，额外开销仅需<0.5K参数和10.34ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对外部上下文（系统提示或检索文档）的依赖带来了信息泄露的攻击面。虽然已有研究表明隐藏状态中存在泄露相关信号，但提取隐藏状态在部署中难以实现。因此，作者希望探索这种内部信号是否在解码前留下更易获取的"痕迹"。

Method: 提出LeakGauge框架：(1) 在用户查询后追加一个"测量后缀"（gauge suffix），通过映射该后缀的prefill token概率来计算攻击风险分数；(2) 对比直接测量后缀（使用机密内容的初始token）与内容无关后缀（将泄露行为语言化）的效果，发现后者信号更稳健；(3) 通过激活导向（activation steering）干预来验证风险分数与模型内部泄露相关方向的敏感性。

Result: 在11个LLM上（含GLM-5.2 753B和Kimi-K3 2.8T），LeakGauge对未见攻击的AUROC达到0.944-0.996；当内容语言变化或攻击从逐字泄露转为语义泄露时信号稳定；风险分数对内部泄露相关方向敏感；部署开销极低，仅需少于0.5K额外参数和10.34ms额外延迟。

Conclusion: LLM的泄露倾向在解码前通过token概率就能被有效探测，无需访问隐藏状态。内容无关的测量后缀能提供更稳健的信号，且该可观测信号与模型内部的泄露相关表征密切相关，LeakGauge是一种低开销、高精度、部署友好的攻击风险检测方案。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly rely on external contexts, such as pre-defined system prompts or retrieved documents, to improve generation quality. However, processing these contexts alongside user queries creates an attack surface: adversarial inputs can induce models to disclose them. Prior probing studies suggest that leakage-related signals emerge in hidden states, yet the need to extract these states poses additional deployment challenges. In this paper, we explore whether this internal signal leaves a more accessible ``tell'' before decoding. We propose LeakGauge, which probes this response by appending a suffix that gauges leakage behavior and mapping its prefill token probabilities to an attack-risk score. While a direct gauge uses the initial tokens of confidential content, we find that a content-agnostic one that verbalizes leakage behavior yields more robust signals. Across 11 LLMs, including GLM-5.2 (753B) and Kimi-K3 (2.8T), LeakGauge reaches an AUROC range of 0.944--0.996 on unseen attacks. The signal remains stable when the content changes language or the attack shifts from verbatim to semantic disclosure. By activation-steering interventions, we further show that the risk score is sensitive to an internal leakage-related direction, relating the observable signal to the model's internal representation. In addition, LeakGauge enables an input detector with fewer than 0.5K extra parameters and added latency of 10.34 ms. Code: \href{https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakGauge}.

</details>


### [217] [COMA: A Compositional Misleading Attack Class on Security-RAG, and a Causal Counterfactual Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17960)
*Chinmay Gondhalekar,Urjitkumar Patel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全运营中心（SOC）中面向分析师的检索增强生成（RAG）系统所面临的一类新型攻击——组合误导（COMA）。这类攻击中每篇对抗文档都事实正确、无指令、无矛盾且在分布上良性，但通过文档间的组合效应误导模型给出错误的漏洞评估和修复建议。作者提出了两种攻击实现方式（action-corruption和verdict-flip），并设计了因果反事实防御（CCD）审计方法，通过留一法衡量每篇文档的因果影响力来识别攻击性文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全场景下的RAG系统即使所有检索到的文档都是真实、无指令、无矛盾的，仍可能被引导对关键可利用漏洞做出错误评估并推荐无效修复方案。这种攻击模式与以往依赖注入恶意指令或虚假内容的方法不同，属于一类新型的安全威胁，亟需系统性的研究和防御。

Method: 作者识别出一类名为COMA（组合误导）的攻击，通过两种具体实现：1) action-corruption——将正确诊断的漏洞引导至劣质修复方案；2) verdict-flip——通过不可判定的可达性链条使可利用性判定不稳定。防御方面提出CCD（因果反事实防御），通过留一法衡量每篇检索文档的因果影响力，并设计聚合变体应对自适应影响力扩散的对手。

Result: action-corruption攻击在所有五种测试模型（包括前沿推理模型）上每次运行都成功，覆盖两个合成域和一个真实CVE（CVE-2021-33813）；verdict-flip攻击随机生效，随模型能力增强而减少但从未消失。CCD在四个良性多文档对照中没有误报，能精准定位攻击者控制的文档；聚合变体能捕获自适应影响力扩散的攻击者。

Conclusion: 攻击成功的关键原则是：当消除歧义的事实必须被"推断"而非"读取"时，组合攻击就能成功。CCD提供了一种有效的防御手段，作者同时发布了攻击种子和参考实现以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Every document a security copilot retrieves can be true, instruction-free, and non-contradictory --- and the copilot can still be driven to assess a critical, exploitable vulnerability correctly and then recommend a remediation that leaves it open. We study this failure in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) backing analyst-facing copilots in Security Operations Centers, and identify a class of attacks, \emph{\compmis{}} (COMA), in which every adversarial document is factually correct, instruction-free, non-contradictory, and distributionally benign --- yet the answer is misled by their \emph{composition}. We realize \compmis{} through \emph{action-corruption}, which steers a correctly-diagnosed vulnerability toward an inferior remediation, and \emph{verdict-flip}, which destabilizes the exploitability verdict via an undecidable reachability chain. Action-corruption bites all five tested models --- including frontier reasoning models --- on every run, on two synthetic domains and a real CVE (CVE-2021-33813); verdict-flip bites stochastically, decreasing with model capability but never vanishing. A single principle governs both: the attack succeeds when the disambiguating fact must be \emph{inferred} rather than \emph{read}. We propose \ccd{} (Causal Counterfactual Defense), an audit that measures the leave-one-out causal influence of each retrieved document and flags answers whose influence concentrates on low-trust documents. \ccd{} localizes the attack to attacker-controlled documents with no false positives on four benign multi-document controls; an adaptive influence-spreading adversary is caught by an \emph{aggregate} variant. We release attack seeds and a \ccd{} reference implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍GxP-Agent，一种将临床实验编程中的监管流程编码为有向无环图（DAG）的多智能体系统，将数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点执行。在CDISC-Bench基准上，Claude Sonnet 4.6驱动该系统实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条记录），远超最佳RAG基线（59.2%）和所有单智能体/扁平多智能体方法（0%），且DAG拓扑使较弱模型GPT-4.1从0%提升至59.2%，并在ADAE任务上首次尝试即达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实验编程（将研究协议转换为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集）是监管提交的瓶颈，基于LLM的代码生成在此任务上灾难性失败——五个前沿模型在11次单次尝试中均未生成有效的受试者级分析数据集，亟需新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），将整体数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，每个节点由携带pharmaverse技能上下文的worker agent执行，并配备验证门控和条件重试机制。构建了基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01（254名受试者，49个ground-truth ADSL变量）的执行型基准CDISC-Bench。

Result: GxP-Agent配Claude Sonnet 4.6在CDISC-Bench三次独立运行中均实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条正确记录）；最佳RAG基线仅为59.2%，所有单智能体和扁平多智能体均为0%。GPT-4.1在该DAG下从0%提升至59.2%均值结构匹配。该方法泛化至ADAE（9节点分支DAG，55变量，1,191条记录），首次尝试即达100%。

Conclusion: 将领域流程知识编码为图拓扑结构——而非仅依赖LLM推理——是可靠、GxP合规的临床实验编程的关键使能因素，该范式可显著缓解LLM在此高难度任务上的灾难性失败。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [219] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Aegis，一个面向Agentic AI系统的运行时治理系统。它将模型输出视为"行动提案"，在工具执行前通过可信决策层进行裁决，实现"模型提议、运行时决定"的安全边界。在6,300行对照数据和2,100行Aegis治理数据的评估中，该系统的策略违规率从79行降至0行，全部治理行保留了可信溯源，所有Senate式结算行均有仲裁与签名证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统会发起修改文件、发送消息、启动任务等工具操作，安全问题的本质从"有害文本生成"转变为"有害操作副作用"。仅靠提示级别的治理可以塑造模型行为，但无法建立真正的执行边界——缺少运行时对动作的硬性控制机制。

Method: Aegis采用三层核心设计：(1) 将模型输出视为"行动提案"，通过可信决策层进行中介；(2) 基于活跃策略状态评估提案，在服务端解析溯源信息，并在不确定情况下失败关闭；(3) 对选定案例引入"Senate式结算"路径，即基于法定人数的非单边授权机制。评估采用重复沙箱语料库，涵盖5个运行族、42个任务、3种条件、每个族10次重复。

Result: 在6,300行对照数据中，提示策略条件产生79行高风险路径泄漏；在2,100行Aegis治理数据中，目标工具应用和风险副作用完成均为0。全部1,832行Aegis尝试行保持可信溯源，全部1,019行Senate结算行具备法定人数和最终签名计数证据。

Conclusion: 结果不支持一般性的自主智能体安全结论，但支持一个更窄的系统性主张：在该评估沙箱语料库内，运行时动作边界治理成功防止了所有被观察到的风险提案演变为受治理的副作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [220] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过注册的配对对照实验（Sonnet 5 显式高推理努力 vs. 省略努力项），基于30道AIME 2026题目各5次调用，发现显式高努力合约使平均交付成本每调用增加$0.01031[+$0.00204, +$0.01974]，但未检测到准确性差异（+0.0133[-0.0267,+0.0467]），每正确解答成本略高。研究还揭示了模型特定的"省略"语义及数据契约中的推理努力项影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API买家购买的是带有日期限定的合约而非单纯的模型名称，其中推理努力项是重要契约条款，但其对成本和准确性的实际影响缺乏系统的实验证据，需要严谨的对照研究来刻画其语义和影响。

Method: 采用注册的配对对比设计：对Sonnet 5在显式高努力与省略努力两种合约下各调用30道AIME 2026题目、每道5次；每个付费尝试分配唯一冻结的终端类别；推理时对题目重采样并保留其重复调用；统计计划和分析管线在查看结果前冻结。

Result: 显式高努力合约比省略合约平均每次调用成本高$0.01031（95%区间[+$0.00204,+$0.01974]）；准确性对比为+0.0133（区间[-0.0267,+0.0467]），未能检测到显著差异，但区间上限不排除高达4.67个百分点的增益；每正确解答成本分别为$0.08665（高努力）vs. $0.07662（省略）。数据契约普查和元数据探针进一步记录了模型特定的省略语义（包括同一供应商内部）。

Conclusion: 推理努力项在不同合约下会产生可测量的成本差异，但本设计中未检测到对应准确性收益；结论局限于所研究的模型、任务和采集日期，且部分原始结构不确定的声明仅保持文档级证据强度。方法论上展示了将预注册对照实验应用于API商业合约分析的价值。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [221] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedPref是一种联邦学习框架，通过冻结的公共语言模型生成备选JSON结构化提取结果，再由本地标注进行排序，各站点仅共享模型更新即可协作训练Qwen3-8B适配器，实现放射学报告关系抽取，无需共享报告或标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本描述发现和位置，但下游搜索和分析需要固定的结构化模式。然而训练关系抽取所需的标签在不同机构间分布不均：小医院本地证据少，数据池化又可能不可行。因此需要一种在不共享数据的前提下，让数据量不均的机构从协作中受益的联邦学习方案。

Method: FedPref包含三个核心组件：(1) 冻结的公共语言模型作为教师池，提出备选的JSON结构化提取结果；(2) 本地标注对候选结果进行排序，构建偏好数据；(3) 各站点在仅共享模型更新的条件下协作训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器。采用异质教师池提供跨模型对比，应对单模型重复采样导致的坍塌问题。

Result: 在六个模拟医院（数据量和疾病患病率不均）的开发数据上，FedPref相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点，数据最少的站点收益最大。集中式训练在池化偏好对并集上比FedPref客户端平均F1高2.66点。在锁定的400份人工验证金标准测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，池化训练为71.67，保持相同排序。

Conclusion: FedPref使得数据量不均、无法池化数据的机构无需共享报告或标注即可从协作中获益，且在数据稀缺站点上的增益最为显著。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [222] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, and Recommend），将人类输入从预先选定的单一问题转变为研究方向，系统自动在文献语料库中搜索候选问题，并通过级联过滤将稀缺的人类审阅资源集中在最有价值的产物上。在组合学试点中，系统从5,245篇论文中过滤出77个项目供人工审阅，产生了多项重要发现。


<details>
