MCTH treats these models as frozen black-box operators and uses Monte Carlo Tree Search to allocate a fixed inference budget across competing design trajectories, incorporating model confidence and uncertainty, as well as cross-expert consensus/disagreement when multiple predictors are available. Across protein-RNA, protein-DNA, protein-protein, and protein-ligand design, matched-budget experiments show that adaptive search improves over simpler sampling and cycling strategies, while held-out AlphaFold3 and Chai-1 evaluations demonstrate transfer beyond the search-time oracle. MCTH provides a shared planning layer across modalities while allowing task-specific folding, inverse-folding, and biophysical modules, requiring no fine-tuning or backpropagation through component models.

</details>


### [287] [DMT-Dens: Density-preserving manifold visualization for biological data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17571)
*Ruizhe Wang,Yixuan Dong,Bolin Yang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zelin Zang*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: DMT-Dens是一种基于潜在Token Transformer编码器的参数化流形可视化方法，通过引入硬对聚合和基于k近邻对数半径的Pearson相关损失函数，在保持标签可分性的同时显著提升单细胞高维数据可视化的密度保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低维嵌入方法虽然能保留局部邻域结构，但可能扭曲观测数据的表观采样密度，导致细胞状态可视界面中致密区与稀疏区域的对比失真，干扰对稀有、过渡性或连续细胞状态群体的正确解读。

Method: 该方法构建在潜在Token Transformer编码器之上，是一种参数化流形可视化模型。它结合基于秩的流形对齐与硬对聚合策略，并通过优化Pearson相关损失来保密度——该损失衡量处理后的输入空间与二维嵌入空间中k近邻对数半径估计之间的一致性。

Result: 基准测试表明DMT-Dens在密度保持方面表现优异，尤其在生物学数据集上优势明显，同时保持了具有竞争力的标签可分性。

Conclusion: DMT-Dens有效解决了现有可视化方法密度失真问题，在不牺牲标签可分性的前提下实现了更好的密度保真，为单细胞状态探索提供了一种更可靠的可视化工具。

Abstract: Motivation: Low-dimensional embeddings are widely used to explore cell-state heterogeneity in single-cell and other high-dimensional biological data. Although many methods preserve local neighborhoods, they may distort the apparent sampling density of processed observations, altering the visual contrast between dense and sparse regions and complicating the interpretation of rare, transitional, or continuous cell-state populations. Results: We present DMT-Dens, a parametric manifold-visualization method built on a latent-token Transformer encoder. The model integrates rank-based manifold alignment with hard-pair aggregation. To preserve density, it optimizes a loss based on the Pearson correlation between k-nearest-neighbor log-radius estimates in the processed input and two-dimensional embedding spaces. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate strong density preservation, particularly on biological datasets, while retaining competitive label separability. Availability: Source code, data-processing scripts, and resolved experiment configurations are available at https://github.com/Ruizhe-wang/DMT-Dens.

</details>


### [288] [A Leakage-Proof Benchmark and Conformal Selective Triage for Electrohysterogram-Based Preterm Birth Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17712)
*Sunday A. Adetunji,Rhoda O. Oyewusi*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本研究在Term-Preterm Electrohysterogram Database上建立了患者独立的电子宫缩描记（EHG）早产预测基准，发现当记录被正确分组（跨记录验证）时，92特征弹性网络逻辑回归模型的AUROC仅为0.493，表明早产预测性能在此前的分割验证中可能被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHG作为一种无创测量子宫肌电活动的方法被用于早产预测，但此前研究中若同一母体记录的不同片段被同时划分到训练集和验证集，性能估计可能存在偏差。作者旨在形式化区分片段级验证与患者独立的分组验证，并建立无偏的基准。

Method: 将300条记录（含38例早产）按预设的三个阶段进行分析。使用92特征的弹性网络逻辑回归模型，采用记录分组嵌套交叉验证；预处理、模型拟合、Platt校准和共形估计均限制在训练数据内，性能基于严格出折的记录级预测通过1000次Bootstrap重采样计算。同时评估类别条件共形选择性预测。

Result: 患者独立评估下AUROC为0.493（95%CI 0.467-0.520），AUPRC为0.122，Brier分数为0.115。26周前AUROC为0.514，之后为0.469。在误覆盖α=0.10时，边际覆盖率为0.897，弃权率72.7%，单例预测准确率为0.624。

Conclusion: 研究建立了记录分离的EHG预测参考基准，并提出更广泛的分割生理数据验证原则：重采样的单位应与预测性能预期泛化的单位一致。共形预测进一步量化了何时可用信息支持单例分类、何时不确定性需要延迟决策。

Abstract: Preterm birth remains a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality worldwide. Electrohysterography (EHG), a noninvasive measure of uterine myoelectrical activity, has been studied for preterm-birth prediction, but performance estimates may be biased when segments from the same maternal record are split across training and validation data. We formalized the distinction between segment-level and patient-independent record-grouped validation, established a patient-independent benchmark on the Term-Preterm Electrohysterogram Database, and evaluated class-conditional conformal selective prediction. All 300 records (38 preterm) were analyzed across three prespecified regimes. A 92-feature elastic-net logistic model was evaluated with record-grouped nested cross-validation; preprocessing, model fitting, Platt calibration, and conformal estimation were confined to training data, and performance was calculated from strictly out-of-fold record-level predictions with 1,000 bootstrap resamples. Under patient-independent evaluation, AUROC was 0.493 (95% CI 0.467-0.520), AUPRC 0.122 (0.095-0.152), and Brier score 0.115 (0.094-0.137). AUROC was 0.514 at or before 26 weeks and 0.469 thereafter. At miscoverage alpha=0.10, marginal coverage was 0.897, abstention 72.7%, and singleton-prediction accuracy 0.624. These findings establish a record-separated reference benchmark for EHG prediction and a broader validation principle for segmented physiological data: the unit of resampling should correspond to the unit at which predictive performance is intended to generalize. Conformal prediction further quantifies when the available information supports a singleton classification and when uncertainty warrants deferral.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [SeqFeed: Improving Agentic RTL Code Generation with Sequential Behavior Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16934)
*Yuxin Du,Juxin Niu,Tao Hu,Xi Wang,Zhe Jiang,Nan Guan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SeqFeed框架，通过SeQuery（类SQL波形查询语言）和SeGraph（跨时钟周期依赖图）两种互补机制，向LLM智能体提供事件可寻址、依赖可追踪、可迭代查询的时序反馈，从而提升RTL代码生成的正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在硬件设计中，智能体生成正确的RTL代码需要理解信号在多时钟周期内的时序行为和传播过程。然而，RTL代码本身不暴露特定执行的周期级信号行为，而完整仿真波形又过于冗长和嘈杂，不适合LLM分析。现有反馈方式无法有效传达时序信息，这成为自动化RTL生成的瓶颈。

Method: SeqFeed包含两个互补机制：(1) SeQuery，一种类SQL的波形查询语言，使智能体能够将查询锚定到语义事件上，并在相对时间点采样信号值；(2) SeGraph，一种依赖图，跨时钟周期追踪信号的传播路径。这两种机制基于对人类工程师推理时序行为方式的洞察，满足事件可寻址、依赖可追踪、可迭代查询三项需求。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验结果表明，SeqFeed有效提升了RTL代码生成的通过率。SeQuery和SeGraph各自独立使用时均有效，且二者结合使用时能带来互补的增益。

Conclusion: SeqFeed通过提供结构化、可查询的时序反馈，有效解决了智能体在RTL代码生成中难以理解信号时序行为的问题，为自动化硬件设计中的时序推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: RTL code generation is a critical stage in hardware design, and the emergence of agentic systems offers new opportunities to automate this process. To generate correct RTL code, agents must understand sequential behavior, including how signals evolve and propagate over multiple clock cycles. However, effectively conveying such temporal information to agents remains a significant challenge. RTL code does not expose cycle-level signal behavior for a specific execution, whereas full simulation waveforms are too voluminous and noisy for effective LLM analysis. To address these limitations, we study how human engineers reason about sequential behavior and identify three requirements for effective feedback: it should be event-addressable, dependency-traceable, and iteratively-queryable. Guided by these requirements, we propose \textit{SeqFeed}, which comprises two complementary mechanisms: (1) \textit{SeQuery}, an SQL-like waveform query language that enables agents to anchor queries to semantic events and sample signal values at relative time points; and (2) \textit{SeGraph}, a dependency graph that tracks signal propagation across clock cycles. Experimental results across multiple LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of SeqFeed in improving pass rates. SeQuery and SeGraph are each effective independently and provide complementary benefits when used together.

</details>


### [290] [DTX: A Throughput-First Training Accelerator for Diffusion and Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16953)
*Shashank*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DTX是一种面向扩散与Transformer模型的吞吐优先训练加速器，通过消除所有循环进位依赖（将每次归约构建为流水线二叉树、每个FP算子流水线化），在64KB统一tile空间上以4槽VLIW协同调度8x8脉动阵列、8通道向量单元、融合AdamW流水线与Philox高斯源，实现216 FLOP/cycle，并通过容差验证、语义门槛与sky130物理实现证明其正确性与能效优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何经由单一FP32加法器串行求和的归约操作都会形成循环进位依赖，将机器性能钳制在约2 FLOP/cycle附近，这严重限制了训练加速器的实际吞吐。作者旨在从架构层面彻底消除这一瓶颈，实现真正的吞吐优先设计。

Method: DTX在架构层面确保不存在任何串行加法链：所有归约均为流水线化二叉树，所有浮点算子为两阶段流水线且启动间隔为1。硬件组成包括8x8权重驻留脉动阵列（带融合偏置/激活/类型转换尾声）、8通道向量单元、8通道融合AdamW流水线及流水线化Philox高斯源，由4槽VLIW指令在统一64KB tile空间上协同调度。验证采用基于FP64黄金模型的容差比较（无规范求和顺序），并附加精确相等例外与严格界限；通过语义门槛（片上扩散MLP训练损失下降）和计数器级证明验证计算/DMA重叠；以分析性等节点分解进行GPU对比；并在sky130工艺上完成脉动阵列的DRC-clean GDS物理实现。

Result: DTX达到216 FLOP/cycle，约为循环进位依赖下限的108倍。17/17项测试、107,108个元素全部通过且零失败；违反前提的程序实测超预算5,340倍，说明界限严格可控。片上扩散-MLP运行将损失从56.4降至26.0；计数器级证明显示计算/DMA重叠可维持峰值吞吐；分析表明相对GPU有6-10倍每瓦吞吐优势；sky130后布线达83.3 MHz，比同节点流程上优化的循环进位MAC基线提升1.9倍。

Conclusion: DTX验证了通过架构级消除循环进位依赖可以彻底突破传统2 FLOP/cycle的性能钳制，实现两个数量级以上的每周期浮点吞吐提升，同时保持正确的容差化数值验证与可实际流片的物理可实现性，为未来训练加速器设计提供了重要的架构范式参考。

Abstract: DTX is a throughput-first training accelerator for diffusion and transformer models. Any summation serialized through a single FP32 adder is a loop-carried dependence that pins a machine near 2 FLOP/cycle regardless of physical design; DTX is built so no such chain exists anywhere -- every reduction is a pipelined binary tree, every FP operator a two-stage pipeline with initiation interval 1. An 8x8 weight-stationary systolic array with a fused bias/activation/cast epilogue, an 8-lane vector unit, an 8-lane fused AdamW pipeline, and a pipelined Philox Gaussian source are co-issued by a 4-slot VLIW word over a unified 64 KB tile space: 216 FLOP/cycle, roughly 108x the loop-carried floor per clock. With no canonical sum order, verification is tolerance-based against an FP64 golden model, with exact-equality carve-outs and a demonstrably tight bound (a premise-violating program measured 5,340x over budget; 17/17 tests, 107,108 elements, zero failures). Semantic gates confirm an on-device diffusion-MLP run reduces its loss (56.4 to 26.0), counter-level proof shows compute/DMA overlap sustains the peak, an analytical iso-node decomposition bounds the GPU comparison at 6-10x throughput per watt, and a sky130 campaign hardens the systolic array to DRC-clean GDS at 83.3 MHz post-route -- 1.9x an optimized loop-carried MAC baseline on the same node and flow.

</details>


### [291] [RENESIS: Energy-Aware Synthesis of Adiabatic Logic from Irreversible Netlists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17139)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Renesis，一个自动化综合工具，可将普通不可逆网表转换为经过验证的、技术映射的能量回收（绝热）电路。该工具采用向量空间建模，以能量而非面积或延迟为优化准则，通过前向/反向遍历填充数据标签，将电路映射到八种能量回收族的逻辑可逆电路，并通过等价性检查和安全验证确保每次变换的能量改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路综合以面积或延迟为优化目标，而绝热/能量回收电路需要在设计层面就以能量为主要优化准则。此外，绝热门的信息擦除会带来完整的非绝热CV²放电惩罚（而非热力学极限k_B T ln 2），因此需要一种专门的能量驱动综合方法，并确保每次变换的实际能量收益都经过验证。

Method: Renesis采用向量空间公式对网表建模，将仿真和合理性扫描表示为前向和反向遍历，其成本与电路组件数量成线性关系。遍历使用数据标签填充账本，以自然Rényi阶次表征开关、擦除和可观测性信息。输出映射到八种能量回收族之一的逻辑可逆电路，并报告相关参数。每次合成变换都进行等价性检查，并必须改进两个成本表（一个无上限，一个在序列可实现性边界之后）中的至少一个，同时不恶化另一个才被接受。此外还实现了一个认证的最优性差距程序，计算合成电路与工具搜索空间可证明下界之间的距离。

Result: 在二十个电路的开发集上，可选的重综合遍次改善了十四个电路；在二十个电路的留出集上，十九个中的十五个得到改善，最佳臂中位数为默认能量的0.91。设备级SPICE仿真再现了参考族上的每周期能量数字。工具、基准网表、验证程序和运行记录均开源发布。

Conclusion: Renesis展示了以能量为优化准则的自动化绝热电路综合是可行且有效的，通过等价性检查和安全验证保证了变换的正确性，并且其合成结果接近自身搜索空间的可证明下限。完全可复现的开源发布增强了该方法的可验证性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We describe Renesis, an automated synthesis tool that accepts an ordinary irreversible netlist and produces a verified, technology-mapped energy-recovery (adiabatic) circuit, using energy rather than area or delay as the optimization criterion. Renesis models the netlist with a vector-space formulation that expresses simulation and justification sweeps as forward and reverse traversals whose cost is linear in the number of circuit components. The traversals populate ledgers with data tags that characterize switching, erasure, and observability information at their natural Rényi orders. The output is a logically reversible circuit mapped to one of eight energy-recovery families, with the associated parameters reported. Reversibility is treated here as a circuit-level requirement rather than a thermodynamic one. When an adiabatic gate erases information the penalty is not $k_B T \ln 2$ but a full non-adiabatic $CV^2$ discharge, which is comparable to the switching energy the circuit style exists to recover. Every synthesis transformation is equivalence-checked, and it must improve one of two reported cost tables, one uncapped and one after a series-realizability bound, while worsening neither before it is accepted. Across a twenty-circuit development set, optional re-synthesis passes improve fourteen circuits. On a held-out set of twenty circuits, fifteen of nineteen are improved, with a best-arm median of $0.91$ of the default energy. A certified optimality-gap program computes the distance between the synthesized circuits and the provable floor of the tool's own search space. A device-level SPICE deck reproduces the tool's per-cycle energy figures on the reference family. The tool, the benchmark netlists, the validation procedure, and the run records behind every reported number are released as open source.

</details>


### [292] [The Road Less Traveled: Congestion-Aware NoC Placement and Packet Routing for FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17266)
*Soheil Gholami Shahrouz,Vaughn Betz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出多种新方法以减少FPGA中NoC拥塞，同时最小化对其他设计指标的影响。将NoC链路拥塞成本整合到VPR布局引擎中，结合turn model路由算法，并引入SAT求解和强化学习增强，最多减少95.1%的NoC拥塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代FPGA架构集成了片上网络（NoC）以支持更大规模和更复杂的设计。NoC增强了系统集成和设计复用，但同时给FPGA CAD流程带来了新约束和指标，布局布线需要同时优化NoC的延迟、带宽利用率和链路拥塞，以及可编程路由资源的使用，这增加了设计复杂度。

Method: 1) 将NoC链路拥塞成本整合进开源CAD流程VPR的布局引擎中；2) 在布局引擎中集成turn model NoC路由算法，利用路径多样性进一步降低拥塞；3) 当增强布局和路由仍无法完全解决拥塞时，将NoC路由问题形式化为布尔可满足性（SAT）问题；4) 在VPR布局引擎的强化学习（RL）智能体中引入NoC感知的移动类型。

Result: 在29个基准测试上，结合布局拥塞建模与turn model分组路由平均将NoC拥塞减少90.7%，聚合带宽需求仅增加4%。SAT方法带来进一步改进，组合算法相比基线布局减少95.1%的拥塞。强化学习增强在大量使用NoC的设计上实现了8.8%的线长减少。

Conclusion: 多种NoC拥塞缓解方法的组合能够显著降低NoC拥塞（最高95.1%），同时仅以少量带宽需求增加为代价，并且NoC感知的强化学习移动类型还能进一步改善线长效率。

Abstract: To help scale to ever-larger and more complex designs, recent FPGA architectures now integrate network-on-chips (NoCs). NoCs help transfer high-bandwidth data over long distances within the chip without using scarce low-delay long routing wire segments. While NoC-enhanced FPGAs aid system integration and design reuse, they also complicate FPGA computer-aided design (CAD) flows by introducing new constraints and metrics. Placement and routing need to optimize NoC metrics like latency and bandwidth utilization and avoid link oversubscription (congestion), while simultaneously optimizing the programmable routing resource usage of the design modules attached to NoC routers.
  In this work, we develop several new approaches to reduce NoC congestion while minimizing the impact on other design metrics. First, we incorporate a NoC link congestion cost into the placement engine of the open-source CAD flow, versatile place & route (VPR). Second, we integrate turn model NoC routing algorithms into the placement engine to leverage path diversity to further reduce congestion. On average over a suite of 29 benchmarks, combining placement congestion modeling with turn model packet routing reduces NoC congestion by 90.7% at the cost of increasing aggregate bandwidth demand by 4%. In cases where the enhanced placement engine and NoC routing fail to fully resolve congestion, we formulate NoC routing as a Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problem. This approach yields significant additional improvements; the combined algorithm reduces congestion by 95.1% compared to the baseline placement. Finally, we enhance the reinforcement learning (RL) agent in VPR's placement engine by introducing a NoC-aware move type, resulting in an 8.8% reduction in wirelength on designs that make extensive use of the NoC.

</details>


### [293] [NeuroAbs: A Neuro-Symbolic RTL Abstraction Framework for Property Checking Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17304)
*Zhiyuan Yan,Xiaofeng Zhou,Ziyue Zheng,Ziyi Yang,Wenbin Che,Wei Zhang,Yangdi Lyu,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NeuroAbs是一种用于RTL抽象化的神经符号框架，通过LLM辅助分析识别可抽象信号，结合LLM抽象与基于AST的符号RTL表示，利用SMT求解验证抽象的正确性，并通过CEGAR迭代细化模型，显著提升硬件属性检查的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在硬件设计的形式化验证中，属性检查面临越来越复杂的RTL设计挑战。现有RTL抽象方法要么需要大量人工干预，要么依赖缺乏灵活性的基于规则的技术，迫切需要一种更高效且灵活的自动化抽象方法。

Method: NeuroAbs框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 使用LLM辅助的RTL分析来识别适合抽象的信号；(2) 结合LLM-based抽象与基于AST的符号RTL表示，使生成的抽象与预期的转换更对齐；(3) 使用SMT（可满足性模理论）求解来验证每个抽象的正确性（soundness）；(4) 当抽象过粗导致证明失败时，采用反例引导的抽象细化（CEGAR）进行迭代优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，NeuroAbs在多种验证任务中显著提高了硬件属性检查的效率。

Conclusion: NeuroAbs通过神经符号结合的方式，将LLM的灵活性与形式化方法（SMT、CEGAR）的严谨性相结合，为RTL抽象提供了一种高效、自动化且可靠的解决方案，有效提升了硬件属性检查的整体效率。

Abstract: Formal verification is a crucial technique for ensuring the functional correctness of hardware designs. In the context of property checking, a key challenge is how to efficiently prove a user-specified property in the face of increasingly complex RTL designs. To address this challenge, abstraction techniques are often employed to reduce system complexity and accelerate the verification process. However, prior RTL abstraction methods either require significant manual effort or rely on rule-based techniques that lack flexibility. This paper introduces NeuroAbs, a neuro-symbolic framework for RTL abstraction. NeuroAbs first uses LLM-assisted RTL analysis to identify signals suitable for abstraction. It then combines LLM-based abstraction with an AST-based symbolic RTL representation to better align the generated abstraction with the intended transformation. The soundness of each abstraction is checked using satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) solving. If the abstraction is too coarse for a successful proof, NeuroAbs applies counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) to iteratively refine the model. Experimental results show that NeuroAbs significantly improves the efficiency of hardware property checking across a range of verification tasks.

</details>


### [294] [ETHEREAL: A 25.6-$μ$s/inf. Low-latency Event-driven Graph-neural-network Processor for High-resolution Vision at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17787)
*Adrian Kneip,Martin Lefebvre,Daniel Gehrig,Victoria Catalán Pastor,Davide Scaramuzza,Marian Verhelst,Charlotte Frenkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ETHEREAL是首款专用于事件驱动图神经网络（EV-GNN）的处理器芯片，通过邻居并行样条卷积引擎和创新的分裂2D/3D存储层次（带有新式时空事件缓存机制），在DAGr-GNN工作负载和VGA分辨率DSEC数据集上实现了25.6μs的端到端事件级推理延迟和1.6μJ的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态视觉传感器（DVS）具有μs级时间分辨率和低延迟优势，是边缘视觉应用的理想前端，但事件驱动的图神经网络（EV-GNN）在算法上高效准确，却缺乏能够同时支持密集规则计算和稀疏不规则内存访问的专用硬件，这是目前亟需填补的空白。

Method: ETHEREAL采用邻居并行样条卷积引擎来处理EV-GNN的核心计算，并设计了分裂2D/3D存储层次结构，创新性地提出时空事件缓存机制，以高效管理稀疏-不规则的内存访问模式。

Result: 在DAGr-GNN工作负载和VGA分辨率（640x480像素）DSEC数据集上，实现了25.6μs的端到端事件级推理延迟和1.6μJ的能耗。

Conclusion: ETHEREAL首次证明了EV-GNN可以在专用芯片上高效运行，显著降低了推理延迟和能耗，为边缘视觉应用中DVS前端与图神经网络后端的协同设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic vision sensors (DVS) are enticing candidates to reach the low-latency, sub-ms target of edge-vision applications, as they generate events with a $μ$s-level time resolution. However, using DVS front ends also calls for novel algorithm/hardware back ends capable of efficiently handling streams of sparse spatiotemporal events. While event-driven graph neural networks (EV-GNNs) have emerged as a solution on the algorithmic side that is both accurate and efficient, there is no dedicated hardware to date capable of efficiently supporting their mixed requirements of dense-regular compute operations and sparse-irregular memory accesses. We therefore introduce ETHEREAL, the first EV-GNN processor chip, capable of bridging this gap by means of a neighbor-parallel spline-convolution engine combined with a split-2D/3D memory hierarchy that introduces a novel spatiotemporal event-caching mechanism. Measurement results demonstrate a 25.6$μ$s latency and a 1.6$μ$J energy per end-to-end event-wise inference on the state-of-the art DAGr-GNN workload and VGA-resolution (640x480 pixels) DSEC dataset.

</details>


### [295] [ESR-HGNN: Eliminating Semantic Redundancy for Efficient Mini-batch HGNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17865)
*Dengke Han,Mingyu Yan,Duo Wang,Wenming Li,Xiaochun Ye,Dongrui Fan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于冗余感知的异构图表征学习采样范式ESR-HGNN，通过元路径字典树重用遍历路径消除冗余内存访问，并结合多通道硬件采样单元和可重用性驱动的元路径分组技术，实现比CPU和GPU高一个数量级的采样性能提升和显著能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构图表征学习（HGNN）在端到端推理中，基于元路径的迷你批次采样因图结构的不规则遍历导致大量随机内存访问，成为显著的性能瓶颈。现有采样范式由于语义冗余导致过多冗余遍历，严重降低采样效率，进而导致次优的迷你批次推理性能。

Method: 1) 提出冗余感知的HGNN采样范式，利用元路径字典树（metapath trie）重用遍历路径，消除冗余内存访问。2) 将采样范式映射到多通道硬件采样单元ESR-HGNN上。3) 引入可重用性驱动的元路径分组技术，在硬件通道内优化聚类元路径以最大化可重用遍历路径，提升语义并行场景下的效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，ESR-HGNN相比CPU和GPU实现了平均一个数量级的采样性能提升，并伴随显著的能耗节省。此外，与GPU和最新HGNN推理加速器集成时，端到端迷你批次推理也获得了实质性的加速。

Conclusion: 通过硬件-算法协同设计，ESR-HGNN有效解决了HGNN迷你批次采样中的冗余内存访问瓶颈，在性能和能耗方面均大幅优于现有CPU/GPU方案，同时在不影响推理准确性的前提下提升了端到端推理效率。

Abstract: Heterogeneous graph neural networks (HGNNs) are highly effective in processing heterogeneous graph data and have been widely adopted in critical domains. As real-world graph data continues to scale, performing direct inference on entire graphs becomes increasingly infeasible, making mini-batch methods the standard approach. However, in end-to-end HGNN inference, metapath-based mini-batch sampling constitutes a significant performance bottleneck due to the extensive random memory accesses induced by the irregular traversal of graph structures. Existing sampling paradigms suffer from excessive redundant traversals caused by inherent semantic redundancy, severely degrading sampling efficiency and, consequently, leading to suboptimal mini-batch inference performance.
  In this work, we propose a redundancy-aware HGNN sampling paradigm that leverages a metapath trie to reuse traversal paths, effectively eliminating redundant memory accesses. We then map it onto a multi-channel hardware sampling unit denominated ESR-HGNN. Furthermore, we introduce a reusability-driven metapath grouping technique that optimally clusters metapaths to maximize reusable traversal paths within hardware channels, enhancing efficiency in scenarios with semantic parallelism. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that ESR-HGNN achieves an average sampling performance improvement of one order of magnitude over CPU and GPU, accompanied by significant energy savings. Additionally, it delivers substantial speedup in end-to-end mini-batch inference when integrated with GPU and state-of-the-art HGNN inference accelerator.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Recovering protein conformations from single-particle cryo-EM data via indirect shape matching gradient flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17759)
*Erik Jansson,Jonathan Krook,Ozan Öktem,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从单颗粒冷冻电镜投影图像重建蛋白质主链原子点云的方法，无需先重构三维静电势图，通过间接形状匹配和Lie群上的梯度流实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统cryo-EM重构需要先重建三维静电势图，再从中提取蛋白质骨架结构，这是一个中间且耗时的步骤。本文旨在绕过静电势图重构，直接从投影数据重建蛋白质主链原子结构。

Method: 将问题建模为间接形状匹配：一个主链点云模板通过变形使其模拟投影与观测数据一致，结构仅通过成像算子被观测。变形通过Lie群上的梯度流计算，先在一般几何框架中推导，再适配到单颗粒cryo-EM场景。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法能成功恢复单链和多链蛋白质结构，并能捕捉构象转变。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了一条无需重构三维静电势图即可直接从cryo-EM投影重建蛋白质主链的有效路径，并展示了处理多链蛋白及构象变化的潜力。

Abstract: Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy images a macromolecule as many noisy tomographic projections of its electrostatic potential. We reconstruct the protein backbone directly from such projections, as an atomic point cloud, without the intermediate step of reconstructing the 3D electrostatic potential map. We formulate this as an indirect shape matching problem: a point-cloud template of the backbone is deformed until its simulated projections agree with the data, with the structure observed only through the imaging operator. The deformation is computed via a gradient flow on a Lie group, and we derive the resulting framework in a general geometric setting before adapting it for single-particle cryo-electron microscopy. On synthetic data, we recover single- and multichain proteins and capture conformational transitions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [297] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值量化危险驾驶行为，构建时空热力图识别高风险区域，并基准测试了集成学习、深度学习和经典时间序列三大类共8种预测模型。ARIMA以最低MAE(162.21)表现最佳，与LSTM(163.92)相当，优于所有集成方法。CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown被识别为持续高风险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测是反应式的，依赖事故记录分析，无法在事故前主动识别高风险位置和危险驾驶行为。这一前瞻性风险识别挑战尚未被充分探索，本文旨在弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 利用网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶，构建省级政府辖区(LGA)层面的时空热力图；基准测试8种预测模型，涵盖集成学习（RF、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时序方法（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet）。

Result: ARIMA取得最低MAE(162.21)，与LSTM(163.92)表现相当，优于所有集成方法；N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼内城区和西区LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续性高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简约的时间序列模型与深度学习方法具有竞争力。研究展示了基于IoT的网联车辆数据在支持主动式道路安全干预方面的潜力，为政策制定者提供针对性行动依据。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [298] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种统计检验工具，能从单次拟合中同时区分"混合PDE参数估计器假设了错误的算子"和"参数仅不可识别"两种失败模式，且无需先知（oracle）。通过信息矩阵统计量与秩统计量的双重读数，在正确设定、误设定和不可识别三种设计下均实现了分离，贡献在于分离而非检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精度检查在判定PDE参数估计器是否错误时是盲目的：误设定估计器的域内RMSE低于观测噪声水平（σ≥0.05时为2.7×10^-2），但系数偏离真实值高达29.7%-31.2%。需要一种能从单次拟合中区分"算子假设错误"与"参数不可识别"这两种本质上不同的失败的工具。

Method: 构建一个仅需单次拟合、无需oracle的统计仪器，核心包括：(1) 信息矩阵统计量，采用plug-in尺度估计和per-seed参数设置，用于检测算子误设定；(2) 秩统计量，用于检测不可识别性（在预注册边界c_5^*=2.15×10^-3处归零）。两种统计量从同一次拟合中读取，实现两种失败模式的分离。

Result: 在正确设定下，信息矩阵统计量中位数为0.19，拒绝率为0.033（低于预注册上限0.10）；在两种误设定下分别升至224和85，所有重复试验均触发报警。在正确设定但不可识别的设计下该统计量保持沉默（n=200时0.050，Clopper-Pearson区间[0.024,0.090]），而秩统计量在预注册边界处归零。此外，一参数曲线拟合、裸参数、49参数和241参数的MLP都收敛到同一伪真值（闭式匹配误差0.07%），而物理信息网络因复合目标收敛到不相交的另一个值。

Conclusion: 该工具的核心贡献是从一次拟合中分离算子误设定与不可识别性两种失败，而非单纯的检测。此外报告了工具的盲区、预注册阴性结果（神经估计器在恢复任务上输给Tikhonov正则化反演）以及保证成立但训练网络违背的假设条件。样本内是界限，样本外是方向。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [299] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法，通过单步探测观察目标模型的局部训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型，并结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边缘多样性构建稳定有效的训练子集，在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上超越基线并稳定优于全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性，导致所选数据与目标模型不匹配。

Method: Data-DPO包含三个阶段：(1) 通过单步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈；(2) 将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型以学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；(3) 在最终选择阶段，结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边缘多样性，构建稳定且有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个基准上，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下持续优于现有数据选择基线，并且稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过将数据价值建模为与目标模型能力分布动态相关的属性，能够更有效地选择与目标模型兼容的高质量数据，在降低训练成本的同时保持甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [300] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASS是一种从粗到细的分层数据选择方法，通过密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再用TopK稀疏自编码器在组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上 outperforms 多个强基线，部分设置下用小量子集即可匹配或超越全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监督微调数据规模不断增大，如何从大规模候选池中筛选高价值子集对降低训练成本和提升模型性能至关重要。现有方法直接在原始嵌入空间中度量数据多样性，但几何度量会纠缠主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致数据选择效果受限。

Method: MASS将数据选择建模为从粗到细的分层覆盖问题。首先使用密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标以进行粗粒度语义分组，然后在每个组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而在保持全局语义覆盖的同时捕捉细粒度监督差异。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上的实验表明，MASS在多种预算设置下 consistently 优于强数据选择基线，并且在若干场景下仅使用一小部分数据即可匹配或超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将数据选择分解为粗粒度语义分组和细粒度质量感知覆盖两个阶段，MASS有效解决了原始嵌入空间中几何度量带来的语义混杂问题，为大规模监督微调数据筛选提供了更高效且更优的方案。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [301] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Strategic 16K语料库——一个从WikiLeaks PlusD（美国外交电报库）构建的16,000条、经泄漏控制的敏感性分类基准数据集，并系统评估了六种模型架构。通过严格的泄漏去除协议消除了三类残留分类标记，在干净基准上BERT达到最优性能（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%）。这是首个在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感性分类基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档的敏感性自动分类是一个关键但未得到充分解决的问题，错误分类可能导致监管违规或安全漏洞。然而，AI方法在该领域的可靠性根本上依赖于训练数据的完整性。该领域存在一个普遍但未充分报告的问题——标签泄漏（label leakage）：文档正文中残留的分类标记使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真正的内容级敏感性信号，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠。作者旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 构建了Strategic 16K语料库：从WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD)中筛选16,000条外交电报，并设计了扩展的泄漏去除协议，识别并消除三类残留分类标记。随后在干净基准上系统评测了六种模型架构，涵盖经典机器学习方法（如TF-IDF+Logistic Regression）和基于Transformer的方法（如BERT、ELECTRA）。

Result: 在干净基准上，BERT表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），ELECTRA紧随其后（Accuracy=88.57%, F1=88.90%）。经典模型中，TF-IDF+Logistic Regression在显著更低的计算成本下取得了最强性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感性分类基准，揭示了标签泄漏对性能估计的负面影响，并为该领域提供了一个可靠的评估基础，证明了Transformer模型在敏感性分类中的优势，同时指出了经典模型在成本效益方面的竞争力。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [302] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mr.Dec（多模态再入院风险预测解码器），将住院期间的每日多模态临床事件按时间顺序建模，利用Transformer解码器融合每日EHR更新和间歇性CXR图像，并辅以疾病特异性监督对比学习作为正则化，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上取得最优性能，同时识别住院期间的"关键日"以提供可解释的实时风险分层依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将复杂纵向住院历史压缩为固定表示，丢失了反映患者生理状态演变的日级临床信号。而30天再入院风险预测本质上是一个随住院期间证据积累而动态变化的过程，需要精确捕捉这种随时间演进的轨迹。

Method: Mr.Dec将每次入院建模为每日多模态事件的自然时间序列，通过Transformer解码器在时间对齐的流中整合每日EHR更新与间歇性CXR发现，模拟真实临床工作流；并使用疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中引入诊断感知结构以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估显示Mr.Dec达到最优性能，同时能识别入院期间的"关键日"，提供可操作且具有临床依据的解释以支持实时风险分层。

Conclusion: 通过保持临床序列的完整性，Mr.Dec在预测性能和可解释性方面均优于现有方法，为临床实时再入院风险监控提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [303] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMAN框架，网络从单路径训练起步，仅当持续出现优化证据时才动态生长出独立的等容量路径，突破了传统多任务学习受限于预定义结构或任务边界触发的容量变化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法（硬共享、多路径/专家、自适应共享、动态扩展）的容量变化通常受限于预定义结构或由任务边界/冲突信号触发，无法做到真正的"按需生长"。作者旨在回答一个根本问题：网络能否从精确的单路径计算开始，仅当持续优化证据出现时才生长出新的独立路径。

Method: EMAN包含两个核心机制：(1) 通过潜在相对相位在不实例化第二条路径的情况下暴露反对称生长方向；(2) 在训练过程中监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构决策。仅在认证通过后，才物化两条等容量的独立路径，并自适应地在共享与任务特定表示容量间分配。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上验证了有效性，以有竞争力的计算成本取得改进的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN证明了优化驱动的结构生长是可行的——网络可以从单路径出发，依据持续优化证据动态生长独立路径，从而更高效地适配不同任务对共享与专用容量的需求。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [304] [rl-triton: High-Performance Triton GPU Kernels for Reinforcement Learning Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17641)
*Lars Simon Zehnder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 rl-triton，一个基于 Triton 的强化学习信用分配 GPU 核函数库。其核心贡献是将七种 RL 估计算法（GAE、V-Trace、Retrace(λ)、TD(λ) 回报、折扣回报、资格迹和情节前缀和）统一为单一的一阶线性递推，通过关联扫描在 O(log T) 并行步内求解，并在多个 GPU 上实现了相对于 torch.compile 基线 1.6-5.70 倍的全调用加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习信用分配算法（如 GAE、V-Trace 等）在具有大量并行环境（如数千个环境）的模拟场景下性能瓶颈显著，现有实现通常缺乏高效的 GPU 并行策略；同时这些算法在数学结构上存在共性，但缺少一个统一的、可复用的高性能实现框架。

Method: 作者将七种 RL 估计算法统一重述为一阶线性递推（first-order linear recurrence）的实例，所有算法共享同一个关联扫描（associative scan）算子。算法特定的融合 Triton 核函数在片上构建递推系数；对关联算子进行代数验证，并显式定义了终止（terminated）与截断（truncated）情节的处理方式。该库实现为开源的 rl-triton。

Result: 在数千个环境、短轨迹的大规模并行模拟场景下，所有七种算法在两个 GPU 上（含/不含逐步截断处理）均获得相对向量化 torch.compile 基线 1.6-5.70 倍的全调用加速。多数算法的加速比随序列长度增加而提高，因为基线在 log T 增大时需要更多扫描阶段，每阶段额外引入一次 HBM 往返开销。

Conclusion: rl-triton 证明了将多种 RL 信用分配算法统一到单一路射扫描框架的可行性与高效性，提供了开源实现以服务社区，并展示了在长序列下相对编译基线更优的可扩展性。

Abstract: We present rl-triton, an open-source library of high-performance GPU kernels for reinforcement learning credit assignment, implemented in Triton. The core contribution is a unified associative scan framework that recasts seven distinct RL estimation algorithms - Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE), V-Trace, Retrace($λ$), TD($λ$) returns, discounted returns, eligibility traces, and episodic prefix sums - as instances of a single first-order linear recurrence solved in $O(\log T)$ parallel steps. All algorithms share the same associative scan operator, with algorithm-specific fused Triton kernels constructing their recurrence coefficients on-chip. We verify the associative operator algebraically and define the treatment of terminated and truncated episodes explicitly. Benchmarks show a 1.6-5.70$\times$ full-call speedup over a vectorized torch.compile baseline in the massively parallel simulation regime (thousands of environments, short rollouts). The reported range covers all seven algorithms on both GPUs, both with and without per-step truncation handling. For most algorithms, speedups increase at longer sequence lengths, as the baseline requires more scan stages as $\log T$ grows, each adding an intermediate HBM round-trip. The library is available at https://github.com/simonsays1980/rl-triton.

</details>


### [305] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究EEG基础模型在"公共编码器-私有下游"部署场景下的对抗迁移攻击，提出SW-ProxyCE（收缩白化代理交叉熵）攻击框架，利用小规模标记参考集恢复任务级决策几何结构，在无需训练替代分类器的情况下实现可迁移的对抗样本生成，并在多个EEG任务和基础编码器上验证了攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的开放发布在促进下游发展的同时引入了此前未被探索的安全风险：公开可用的表示可能使私有下游模型变得脆弱。攻击者可以白盒访问公开编码器，但对受害者模型无任何访问权限，需要研究这种场景下的对抗迁移攻击可行性。

Method: 提出Shrinkage-Whiten Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)框架：通过shrinkage-whitened类原型从少量带标签参考集合中恢复任务级决策几何结构，无需训练额外的代理分类器，即可生成可迁移的对抗样本。

Result: 在3个EEG任务上使用3个通用基础编码器和1个范式特定预训练编码器进行评测，覆盖线性探测和全微调下游模型，以及跨受试者和受试者内场景。SW-ProxyCE在性能上持续优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击，表明EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 公开EEG编码器加私有下游模型的部署架构存在可被利用的对抗迁移攻击漏洞，需要重新审视EEG基础模型的安全评估与部署策略；模型的高迁移性不等同于对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [306] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DOW-KE，一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，遵循"优化即部署"原则，通过完整模型反向传播最终编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，消除锚点法中局部求解导致的闭包差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层定位-编辑（locate-then-edit）知识编辑方法存在根本缺陷：它们优化中间表示的锚点（anchor），但部署的是通过真实前向传播的多层权重更新，二者的联合效果从未被直接优化。锚点设置或传播方式如何变化，每次更新都来自局部求解，导致传播引起的衰减和失真无法被纠正，即在锚点目标与实际编辑之间存在"闭包差距"（closure gap）。

Method: 提出DOW-KE，遵循单一原则：所优化的必须精确等于所部署的。具体而言：（1）将最终编辑目标通过完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，使跨层传播与耦合进入每个梯度步；（2）将保持投影（preservation projection）嵌入更新参数化中并置于计算图内部，使每个梯度作用于实际部署的更新，避免事后约束重新引入间隙，同时保持编辑不侵犯受保护知识。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型上的大规模顺序编辑实验中，DOW-KE在六个模型-数据集设置中有五个取得了最高的综合Score和邻域特异性（Specificity），优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: DOW-KE通过消除优化目标与部署更新之间的闭包差距，实现了更精确、更具特异性的知识编辑，验证了"优化即部署"原则的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [307] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过EdNet-KT3数据集检验了学习分析中的一个常见假设：将智能辅导系统（ITS）日志的无监督聚类结果作为能预测学习效果的"学习者类型"。研究发现，对5000名活跃学习者的学习策略特征进行聚类，得到稳定层次化的8种学习策略，早期聚类虽能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106）但不能预测后期无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093）。知识追踪模型（SAKT）对下一题正确率的预测仅比基线好0.051 AUC，且掌握状态信号与行为风格几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。结论是行为聚类描述的是学习风格和参与度，而非知识增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域普遍将ITS日志的无监督聚类结果视为能预测学习成果的"学习者类型"，但这种假设缺乏严格检验。作者旨在通过时间分离的前后段设计（早期聚类预测后期结果），系统地验证行为聚类是否真正具有学习结果的预测效力，以及行为风格与知识掌握状态之间是否存在关联。

Method: 采用EdNet-KT3数据集，对5000名活跃学习者的学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、题目练习）进行聚类分析。使用轮廓系数选择父级切分（k=5），对残差群体再聚类得到4种更细风格，最终生成有bootstrap稳定性验证的8种策略层次结构。通过响应计数拆分每个学习者的时间线（早期聚类、晚期结果），并引入SAKT知识追踪模型评估掌握信号与行为聚类的关系。

Result: 得出8种稳定层次化的学习策略；早期聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021）但无法预测无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093）。纯基于学习量的聚类与策略标签匹配度极低（ARI=0.064）；SAKT知识追踪模型预测力仅比基线提升0.051 AUC，知识状态与行为风格几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类有效描述了学习风格和参与度模式，但并不能反映知识掌握或学习成效。这挑战了学习分析中将聚类作为"能预测学习"的学习者类型的常见实践，建议研究者在使用行为聚类推断学习效果时保持谨慎，或将行为风格与知识状态视为相互独立的维度分别建模。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [308] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种基于广义钟形(gbell)隶属函数的鲁棒随机向量功能链接网络，通过样本级双重加权策略处理类别不平衡和标签噪声问题，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上优于现有RVFL变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中的多数类主导问题使随机神经网络决策边界产生偏差，现有方法（如SMOTE和类加权损失函数）主要处理类别比例但忽略类内分布，对标签噪声和异常值敏感，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双重样本级加权机制：对少数类样本使用单位权重以严格保留其信息，对多数类样本通过核诱导特征空间中的概率加权广义钟形(gbell)隶属函数自适应调节其影响，有效抑制噪音、边界和异常样本。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集以及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试中，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于最新的RVFL变体。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于稳健的RVFL学习至关重要，传统全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境下效果不佳。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [309] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSigBERT，一个基于路径签名表示的多模态序贯生存分析统一框架，将电子健康记录中的自由文本报告和结构化协变量融合为联合时间轨迹，通过签名变换捕捉跨模态高阶时间交互，并利用LASSO正则化Cox模型估计个体风险评分，在包含2,500多名患者的真实肿瘤队列上达到0.743的C指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）包含互补的异构数据（叙事临床报告、数值测量、结构化变量），但大多数生存模型局限于单一模态，或未能充分利用患者轨迹的时间特性。现有方法无法有效融合多模态时间信息以提升临床决策质量。

Method: MultiSigBERT框架包括四个关键步骤：(1) 将自由文本临床报告通过BERT提取上下文词嵌入并平均得到句子嵌入；(2) 通过模态特定的PCA压缩嵌入，并与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时间轨迹；(3) 利用粗糙路径理论中的签名变换（Signature transform）编码轨迹，无需监督即可捕获跨模态高阶时序交互；(4) 将签名特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化Cox模型，估计个体化风险评分。

Result: 在Léon Bérard中心超过120,000份医疗报告和2,500多名患者的真实肿瘤队列上，模型在独立测试集上达到0.743的C指数（标准差0.029），验证了联合建模多模态时间动态和患者级几何结构对生存预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合建模多模态时序动态与患者级几何结构可以显著提升生存预测性能，签名变换为多模态EHR轨迹的融合提供了一种高效、无需监督的特征表示方法。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [310] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出生成模型中的公平性问题本质上源于评估问题——公平性发现难以跨论文比较或用于部署决策。论文诊断了当前实践中反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式，提出用"公平卡"（Fairness Cards）作为最小报告工件，使评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标、拒答处理）显式化，从而促进可复现性、可比性和问责性，并倡导评估标准的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过去十年生成模型取得了突破性进展，但其缺乏公平性、强化社会不平等、危害边缘群体等问题仍未得到充分解决且难以采取行动。公平性发现很少能在不同论文间进行比较，也难以指导部署决策，作者认为这一评估困境是生成模型公平性问题的根本症结。

Method: 论文采用立场论证方式：(1) 诊断当前实践中反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式；(2) 提出"公平卡"（Fairness Cards）作为最小报告工件，明确标注评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标和拒答处理）；(3) 提出从临时性偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型专用评估范式的建议。

Result: 提出了Fairness Cards这一报告工件，旨在通过显式化评估设计选择来实现公平性评估的可复现性、可比性和问责性，并给出了促使评估标准范式转变的额外建议。

Conclusion: 作者认为生成模型公平性问题需要从临时性的偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型专用的评估体系，公平卡是实现这一转向的关键工具，并期待借此推动评估标准的范式转变。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [311] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过工具让智能体在agent轨迹中途自主切换专门的LoRA适配器，以应对多能力组合任务中的灾难性遗忘问题。实验表明该方法能解决模型此前无法解决的问题，相比单一适配器最多降低18x的能力代价，并显著优于子代理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练（post-training）可以解锁新能力并提升特定任务表现，但常常以其他领域的灾难性遗忘为代价。在需要组合多种能力的长时间agent轨迹中，这种tradeoff成为一个问题。作者拒绝这种权衡，提出让agent在轨迹中途切换不同LoRA适配器的方案。

Method: 作者给agent一个工具，使其能够在agent执行轨迹（mid-trace）中切换不同的专门LoRA适配器。为测试有效性，设计了两个逻辑简单但需要专业化的合成编码任务。对比基线包括：使用单一适配器的agent、人类启发式切换策略，以及生成子代理的基线方案。

Result: 模型能够解决此前无法解决的问题；能够自主切换适配器，且在一个任务上发现了优于人类启发式基线的新策略；相比使用单一专门适配器的agent，能力代价（capability tax）最多降低18倍；在任务能力和token使用方面均显著优于生成子代理的基线方法。

Conclusion: 在agent轨迹中途切换LoRA适配器是一种有效解决多能力组合任务中灾难性遗忘问题的方法，既保留了各领域专门化能力，又避免了单一适配器带来的能力退化，且优于子代理方法。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [312] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了J-Miner方法，从微调后的语言模型分类器中挖掘内部决策知识，将其编码为可检查、可验证、可复用的可执行表示（命名概念+决策规则），在多个分类任务上规则可复现高达98.3%的源分类器决策，且决策知识可迁移到参数仅为源模型1/24的轻量级学生模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调后可以成为性能优异的专用分类器，但它们通常只输出最终标签，通过微调获得的决策知识隐含在模型内部，无法被检查、验证或复用。作者希望将这些内部决策知识挖掘出来，编码为显式、可执行且可迁移的表示形式。

Method: 作者提出J-Miner方法，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过聚合跨层和跨token位置的词汇对齐内部信号，挖掘文本级别的命名概念；(2) 利用分类器自身的预测结果，在这些命名概念之上学习可执行的决策规则。整个过程将局部内部读出走蒸馏为显式的分类器级别知识表示。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，J-Miner规则可复现高达98.3%的源分类器决策，比从输入词学习的同等紧凑规则在行为保真度上高出6.0-29.5个百分点。命名概念反映了与任务决策相关的内部语义证据，学习到的规则将分布式信号整合为可检查的决策结构。决策知识还能迁移到约1/24参数量（相对源分类器）的轻量级学生模型，保留99.8%的源分类器平均任务准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明任务特定的决策知识可以被忠实地表示为显式、可执行的形式，并且可以超越源分类器本身被复用，为解释LLM分类决策和知识迁移提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models can be fine-tuned into specialized classifiers that perform well across diverse text tasks and make complex judgments, but they typically expose only final labels, leaving the decision knowledge acquired through fine-tuning implicit within the model. We study how to mine this internal decision knowledge from a fine-tuned classifier and encode it in an executable representation that can be inspected, validated, and reused beyond the source classifier. We introduce J-Miner, which mines text-level named concepts by aggregating vocabulary-aligned internal signals across layers and token positions, and uses the classifier's own predictions to learn executable decision rules over them. This process distills local internal readouts into an explicit classifier-level knowledge representation. Across multiple classification tasks, J-Miner rules reproduce up to 98.3\% of source-classifier decisions and achieve 6.0--29.5 percentage points higher behavioral fidelity than equally compact rules learned from input words. Further analysis shows that the named concepts reflect internal semantic evidence associated with task decisions, while the learned rules consolidate these distributed signals into inspectable decision structures. The resulting decision knowledge also transfers to lightweight standalone students: using about 1/24 as many parameters as the source classifiers, they reconstruct and execute the representation from raw text while retaining 99.8\% of the source classifiers' mean task accuracy. These findings show that task-specific decision knowledge can be faithfully represented in an explicit, executable form and reused beyond the classifier in which it was learned.

</details>


### [313] [Certified but Private: Scalable Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Neural Network Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17070)
*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANDA是一个基于零知识证明（ZKP）的可扩展系统，能够在不泄露模型私有参数的前提下，形式化证明神经网络模型的鲁棒性和公平性。PANDA建立在CROWN鲁棒性认证框架之上，核心贡献是提出了一个新的算法来证明非线性激活层的线性松弛界，从而生成简洁轻量的证明。该系统可在5分钟内为超过290万参数的神经网络生成本地鲁棒性证明，并在10秒内完成验证，较此前基于ZKP的方法网络规模提升4个数量级，且验证开销显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型在安全关键和合规场景中的广泛部署，模型鲁棒性和公平性的形式化保证变得日益重要。然而模型参数通常是商业机密，无法直接披露给审计者或终端用户。现有方法无法在不泄露模型参数的情况下对模型属性进行可扩展的形式化验证，因此需要一种既能保护模型隐私又能提供可验证保证的方案。

Method: PANDA的核心方法是将零知识证明（ZKP）与CROWN鲁棒性认证框架相结合。其关键技术贡献是一个新颖的算法，用于为非线性激活层证明线性松弛（linear relaxation）的界限，从而产生简单、轻量的证明。这种方法避免了此前基于ZKP的系统中使用的指数级时间算法，使得系统的计算复杂度随网络神经元数量多项式增长。

Result: PANDA能够在5分钟内为超过290万参数的神经网络生成本地鲁棒性证明，并在10秒内完成验证。相比此前基于ZKP的鲁棒性系统（依赖指数级时间算法，无法扩展到非平凡网络），PANDA支持的网络规模提升了4个数量级，同时显著降低了证明者的开销。该系统的复杂度与网络神经元数量呈多项式关系。

Conclusion: PANDA首次将基于ZKP的鲁棒性和公平性证明扩展到了大规模神经网络，通过多项式复杂度的线性松弛证明算法克服了此前方法的可扩展性瓶颈。该系统为在保护模型商业机密的前提下实现形式化验证提供了实用化的解决方案，有助于推动机器学习模型在安全关键和合规场景中的可信部署。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [314] [Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Calibration for Reliable Economic Forecast Intervals under Multiple Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17079)
*Bogdan Oancea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态机制感知共形预测（DRACP），一种统一的加权共形校准框架，用于处理经济预测中违反可交换性假设的问题，在48个真实经济序列上相比六个基线提供最可靠的覆盖率校准（最接近名义0.90，达到0.890），但效率不如强自适应在线共形预测（后者区间约窄20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供无分布假设的预测区间，但其依赖的可交换性假设在经济预测中常被违反（如协变量漂移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在机制转换），需要一种能够适应这些非平稳性的加权共形校准方法。

Method: DRACP结合密度比加权、局部核加权和概率机制感知加权三种加权机制，并集成自调优在线显著性控制器，统一到加权共形校准框架中。提供三个理论结果：oracle重要性权重下的有限样本有效性、估计权重的覆盖率差距界（有效样本量速率）、以及在线控制器的确定性或遗憾保证。

Result: 在48个经济时间序列（欧元区和EU-27的HICP通胀、美国宏观经济与能源指标、日频金融序列）上与六个基线对比。DRACP达到最佳校准性能：覆盖率0.890最接近名义0.90，所有序列均不低于0.80，在所有预测水平上保持最佳覆盖率，在2021-2023年通胀激增期间表现最佳。但非最有效方法：强自适应在线共形预测的区间得分最优，区间约窄20%。消融实验表明在线控制器和条件尺度归一化贡献了大部分性能增益，加权组件贡献较小。

Conclusion: DRACP提供了校准与效率之间的原理性权衡，在预测区间必须满足覆盖率标准时选择优先保证可靠性。尽管效率不及强自适应在线共形预测，但其在覆盖率的稳定性、最坏情况保障和极端时期（通胀激增）的表现上具有独特优势。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals but relies on exchangeability, an assumption often violated in economic forecasting because of covariate shift, concept drift, local heterogeneity and latent regimes. We propose Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP), which combines density-ratio, localized kernel and probabilistic regime-aware weighting with a self-tuning online significance controller in a unified weighted conformal calibration framework. We distinguish three theoretical results: finite-sample validity under oracle importance weights, a coverage-gap bound for estimated weights with rates in effective sample size, and deterministic or regret guarantees for the online controller. We evaluate DRACP against six baselines on 48 real forecasting series covering euro-area and EU-27 HICP inflation, US macroeconomic and energy indicators, and daily financial series. Recent online methods (FACI, strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction and conformal PID) were verified against the authors' implementations. DRACP is not the most efficient method: strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction achieves the best interval score and intervals about 20% narrower. Instead, DRACP provides the most reliable calibration, achieving coverage closest to the nominal 0.90 (0.890), never falling below 0.80 on any series, maintaining the best coverage at all forecast horizons, and performing best during the 2021-2023 inflation surge. The strongly-adaptive method undercovers on 20 of 48 series versus 10 for DRACP. DRACP therefore offers a principled trade-off between calibration and efficiency, favoring reliable coverage when prediction intervals must satisfy coverage standards. An ablation study shows that the online controller and conditional-scale normalization provide most of the performance gain, whereas the weighting components make a smaller contribution.

</details>


### [315] [Backward through Time, Algebraically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17087)
*Konstantinos Kogkalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个代数泛型且可微分的线性时序逻辑（LTL）求值引擎，允许用户选择不同语义代数来评估软值系统（如神经策略、自适应控制器）的时序性质。该引擎提供了可执行规范来描述可接受的代数，并在PyTorch库telos中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性时序逻辑是描述系统随时间行为的规范语言，但其经典布尔域语义难以直接用于软值系统（如神经网络、自适应控制器等）。在这些场景中，公式的满足程度需要作为训练信号，因此可微性至关重要。虽然现存有多种可微分语义，但实现通常是浅嵌入（shallow embedding），要求使用者在使用前就承诺选择单一的语义代数及其（通常隐含的）行为方式，这使得导航和选择非常困难。

Method: 论文从函数式程序员视角切入，拒绝"预先承诺单一语义代数"的困境，转而设计了一个代数泛型（algebra-generic）且可微分的求值引擎。该引擎配有可执行的规范，用于定义它能接受的代数类型。文中实现了多种代数，并对其前向和反向（求导）行为进行了审计。

Result: 提出了telos库（基于PyTorch），实现了代数泛型且可微分的LTL求值引擎，支持多种语义代数。通过审计发现，每种代数本质上代表了一种"在哪个方向以及如何失望"（即如何以不同方式"软化"时序性质的不满足）的选择。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是打破了LTL语义选择上的"一次性承诺"限制，提供了一种灵活、模块化、可微分的LTL评估框架，使研究者和工程师可以根据具体需求选择或自定义语义代数，同时保持前向求值和反向传播的能力。该工作以开源库telos的形式发布。

Abstract: Linear temporal logic is a modal extension of propositional logic that allows one to state how a system should behave over time. Its canonical domain is the booleans, but discretely-valued judgements are of little use in steering softly-valued systems (neural policies, adaptive controllers, sequence models, etc). In such cases, the goal formula's (dis)satisfaction becomes a training signal, and differentiability becomes a prime concern. Candidate differentiable semantics abound, but navigating them is tricky. Implementations, where available, are shallow embeddings, demanding an upfront commitment to a single semantic algebra and its (usually implicit) conduct. The paper casts the reader as a functional programmer asked to come to terms with this predicament, and refusing. Out of that refusal comes an evaluation engine that is algebra-generic and amenable to differentiation, together with an executable specification of the algebras it can accept. Various algebras are implemented and audited for their behavior, both forward and backward. Each algebra turns out to be a choice of which direction to disappoint, and how. Everything described (and more) is part of the PyTorch library telos, to be found at https://github.com/konstantinosKokos/telos.

</details>


### [316] [Deep Learning for Cross-Border Electricity Price Forecasting: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17091)
*Hadeer Elashhab,Sai Srijan Papineni,Marvin Dorn,Veit Hagenmeyer,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于公开电力市场数据，构建了可复现的电力价格预测（EPF）基准框架，对六种深度学习模型（涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构）在跨市场场景下进行了系统比较，并在德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）竞价区2024年数据上通过零样本、单样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场数据虽然是预测研究的重要资源，但缺乏公认的基准数据集和标准化的比较框架，导致各研究使用不同的数据集和指标在孤立环境下评估方法，难以一致地衡量进展和比较最先进的方法。

Method: 使用公开数据构建可复现的评估框架，对六种深度学习模型（包括状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构，如N-HiTS、NBEATSx等）进行跨市场泛化能力的比较评估；使用日历、历史价格和市场衍生特征建立标准化数据集，并通过零样本、单样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件。

Result: N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现具有竞争力；Transformer模型能达到相近精度但需要更多适应和调参；模型性能受益于谨慎的特征选择和超参数调优；最强模型之间的差异通常很小。

Conclusion: 本文建立了可复现的EPF基准框架，填补了该领域缺乏标准化基准数据集的空白，为后续研究提供了统一的比较平台。

Abstract: While publicly available electricity market data presents a valuable resource for forecasting research, the field lacks established benchmark datasets for standardized comparison. As a result, many studies have relied on different datasets and metrics to evaluate methods in isolated settings, making it difficult to assess progress and compare state-of-the-art approaches consistently. In this work, we use public data to evaluate deep learning models for electricity price forecasting (EPF) across multiple market settings. Our goal is to establish a reproducible framework that enables a consistent evaluation of forecasting models. Although deep learning has been explored for day-ahead EPF, many prior studies are limited to single-market settings, narrow feature sets, or fixed training regimes. This work presents a comparative evaluation of six deep learning models--covering state-space, MLP, RNN, and Transformer-based architectures--emphasizing generalization across markets. We simulate low-data target-market conditions using zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot learning. Our test set focuses on the Germany-Luxembourg (DE-LU) bidding zone in 2024 using a standardized dataset with calendar, historical price, and market-derived features. Our findings suggest that N-HiTS and NBEATSx perform competitively in limited-data scenarios, while transformer-based models can reach comparable accuracy but tend to require more adaptation and tuning. Model performance also benefits from careful feature selection and hyperparameter tuning, and we note that the differences between the strongest models are often small.

</details>


### [317] [From Abductive Explanations to Global Logical Rules for Node Classification in SGCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17103)
*Bryan Lima Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于逻辑的框架，用于Simple Graph Convolution (SGC)网络的节点分类解释。该框架使用最小溯因解释（minimal abductive explanations）作为中间表示，为每个节点计算一组最小节点-特征对以保留预测类别，然后利用这些解释训练决策树以提取全局逻辑规则。实验表明该框架能产生紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于逻辑的解释方法（如LogicXGNN）从解释性子图集合中提取全局逻辑规则，但这些子图可能包含冗余的结构信息，且局限于特定节点，限制了所提取规则的泛化性。

Method: 提出一个逻辑框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 为每个节点计算最小溯因解释，即一组最小节点-特征对，足以保留预测类别；(2) 使用这些最小解释作为中间表示来训练决策树；(3) 从决策树中提取全局逻辑规则。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，所提出的框架能够产生紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。

Conclusion: 通过使用最小溯因解释替代解释性子图作为中间表示，可以有效消除冗余的节点特定结构信息，从而提取出更紧凑且更具泛化性的全局逻辑规则，同时不牺牲对原始模型的保真度。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. Recent logic-based approaches, such as LogicXGNN, derive global logical rules for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) from collections of explanatory subgraphs. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. For each node, we compute a minimal set of node-feature pairs sufficient to preserve the predicted class. These explanations are then used to train decision trees from which global logical rules are extracted. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact global rules while maintaining high fidelity to the original SGC model.

</details>


### [318] [Causal Discovery in Equal Variance Linear Gaussian DAGs via SURE-Tuned Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17132)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SURE-Ridge是一种非迭代、闭式解的因果发现方法，适用于等方差线性高斯SEM模型。该方法通过并行节点级回归配合SURE自适应正则化参数选择，并采用自适应阈值化提取DAG，在小样本和计算受限场景下优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续优化的因果发现方法（如梯度下降类方法）存在两个关键缺陷：一是需要逐问题超参数调优，不适用于样本受限（样本数小于等于节点数）的场景；二是迭代优化过程计算开销大，不适合计算受限场景。

Method: 提出SURE-Ridge方法：(1) 对每个节点独立执行并行Ridge回归；(2) 通过Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）自适应地选取正则化参数，避免人工调参；(3) 对得到的软邻接矩阵应用自适应阈值化过程，提取最终的DAG结构。

Result: 在小样本场景下SURE-Ridge取得最低的结构汉明距离（SHD），在所有测试样本量下运行时间最短，均优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL三种基线方法。

Conclusion: SURE-Ridge通过非迭代的闭式解设计，避免了昂贵的迭代优化和超参数调优，同时兼得结构恢复精度和计算效率，尤其适合样本受限和计算受限的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Recovering the directed acyclic graph (DAG) of a structural equation model (SEM) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery. The iterative gradient descent and per-problem hyperparameter tuning of continuous-optimization methods are poorly suited to two practically important regimes: the sample-limited regime, where the number of samples is comparable to or smaller than the number of nodes in the DAG, and the compute-limited regime. This work proposes SURE-Ridge, a non-iterative, closed-form estimator for equal variance linear Gaussian SEM. The method performs parallel node-wise regressions with regularization parameters chosen adaptively by Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), and applies an adaptive thresholding procedure to extract a DAG from the resulting soft adjacency matrix. Numerical results show that SURE-Ridge achieves the lowest structural Hamming distance in the small-sample regime and the lowest run time across all sample sizes tested, compared with NOTEARS, DAGMA, and GBNSL baselines.

</details>


### [319] [Iterative tensor network transformations for element-wise evaluation of elementary and filtering functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17135)
*Xiao Wang,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种在张量列车（TT）压缩域中直接执行逐元素非线性运算和滤波函数的通用算法框架，避免了将数据解压的昂贵步骤，并展示了其在3D反应流场非线性函数求值和大规模Max-SAT优化（高达2^70配置空间）中的强大能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络是压缩大规模数据的有力格式，但受限于难以在压缩域中执行非线性操作，阻碍了其在通用数据处理中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNT）算法框架，完全在压缩域中运行，对编码为张量列车（TT）格式的数据执行逐元素的初等函数和滤波函数的求值，通过迭代方式控制计算成本。

Result: 在两大领域展示有效性：(I) 对3D反应流场求值高度非线性的初等函数和滤波函数，实现高保真反应速率计算与区域滤波；(II) 在复杂优化问题中寻找极值，可求解高达2^70配置空间的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNT确立了自身作为基础性工具的地位，为张量网络方法赋予了通用数据科学和大规模优化的能力，填补了张量网络在非线性操作方面的空白。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>


### [320] [OraclePhys: A Systematic Framework for LLM Fine-Tuning on Structural Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17162)
*Mingyu Li,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OraclePhys框架，系统研究微调中标签形式（而非数据量）如何因果地决定大语言模型学到的能力。实验发现：标签的答案形式决定是否安装前向物理模型（排序目标可安装完整的OOD前向模型，标量目标部分安装，布尔目标无检测结果）；基于文本或分数过滤的答案可安装该能力，而GRPO优势加权分数仅提升奖励却在保留物理任务上统计等价于初始模型。训练后的8B模型达到数据精度前沿，零样本和32样本均超过前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从微调中内化的内容通常只能事后诊断，本文希望将这一过程变为可控的实验变量，系统探究标签形式如何因果地影响微调效果。

Method: 构建OraclePhys框架，包含三个组件：OraclePhys-Bench（基于有限元oracle精确评分的结构力学基准，无人工标注和无LLM评判）；OraclePhys-30K（七种答案形式的监督数据集，覆盖字节级相同的结构描述）；以及跨七种答案形式和三种验证器角色的受控训练研究。

Result: （1）标签的答案形式而非比特数因果决定微调所学：排序目标安装完整OOD前向模型，标量目标仅部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；向量-标量鸿沟在第二物理域、第二模型族和改写评估面上均保持。（2）文本或分数过滤答案可安装能力，而GRPO优势加权分数仅提升奖励，保留物理任务上统计等效于初始模型。（3）训练后的8B模型成为首个空间结构响应的LLM，达到数据精度前沿，零样本和32样本超过前沿LLM，达到专家水平。

Conclusion: 标签所明确的目标计算形式决定了微调所学到的东西；训练数据的形式决定了模型的用途路由。即"标签写明什么，微调就教什么；你训练什么，就路由什么"。

Abstract: What a language model internalizes from fine-tuning is usually diagnosed after the fact. We make it an experimental variable. OraclePhys is a systematic fine-tuning framework with three components: OraclePhys-Bench, an exactly-graded structural-mechanics benchmark whose finite-element oracle scores every answer and counterfactual edit -- no human labels, no LLM judging; OraclePhys-30K, a supervision dataset of seven answer forms over byte-identical structure descriptions; and a controlled training study across the seven forms and three verifier roles. The study yields two findings. First, the label's answer form -- not its bit count -- causally determines what fine-tuning teaches: a ranking objective installs an out-of-distribution forward model where the untrained base sits at the guessing prior, a scalar objective at best a partial one, a boolean nothing detectable; the vector-scalar gulf survives a second physics domain, a second model family, and a paraphrased evaluation surface.
